## Immigration and Refugee Protection Regulations {#immigration-and-refugee-protection-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/2002-227
:::

[FINANCIAL ADMINISTRATION ACT](/eng/acts/F-11)

[IMMIGRATION AND REFUGEE PROTECTION ACT](/eng/acts/I-2.5)

Registration 2002-06-11

Immigration and Refugee Protection Regulations

C.P. 2002-997 2002-06-11
::::

::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
Whereas, pursuant to subsection 5(2) of the [Immigration and Refugee
Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#footnotea_e){#footnotea_e-ID0EDABA}, the Minister
of Citizenship and Immigration has caused a copy of the proposed
[Immigration and Refugee Protection
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2002-227) to be laid before each House
of Parliament, substantially in the form set out in the annexed
Regulations;

Therefore, Her Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the
recommendation of the Minister of Citizenship and Immigration and the
Treasury Board, pursuant to subsection 5(1) of the [Immigration and
Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5)^a^ and paragraphs
19(1)(a)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#footnoteb_e){#footnoteb_e-ID0EJDAA} and 19.1(a)^b^
and subsection 20(2) of the [Financial Administration
Act](/eng/acts/F-11), and, considering that it is in the public interest
to do so, subsection 23(2.1)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^c^](#footnotec_e){#footnotec_e-ID0EDDAA} of that Act,
hereby makes the annexed [Immigration and Refugee Protection
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2002-227).

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#footnotea_e-ID0EDABA){#footnotea_e}S.C. 2001, c.
  27
  :::

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#footnoteb_e-ID0EJDAA){#footnoteb_e}S.C. 1991, c.
  24, s. 6
  :::

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^c^](#footnotec_e-ID0EDDAA){#footnotec_e}S.C. 1991, c.
  24, s. 7(2)
  :::
:::

## [PART 1]{.HLabel1}[Interpretation and Application]{.HTitleText1} {#h-685363 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-685364 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  definitions in this subsection apply in the Act and in these
  Regulations.

  [[common-law partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[common-law partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in relation to a
      person, an individual who is cohabiting with the person in a
      conjugal relationship, having so cohabited for a period of at
      least one year. ([conjoint de fait]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[excessive demand]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[excessive demand]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a demand on health services or social services
        for which the anticipated costs exceed triple the average
        Canadian per capita health services and social services costs
        over a period of five consecutive years following the most
        recent medical examination required under paragraph 16(2)(b) of
        the Act; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a demand on health services or social services
        that would add to existing waiting lists and would increase
        morbidity or the mortality rate in Canada as a result of an
        inability to provide timely services to Canadian citizens or
        permanent residents. ([fardeau excessif]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[health services]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[health services]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any health services
      -- including the provision of devices related to the services,
      laboratory services and the supply of pharmaceuticals -- for which
      more than half of the funds are contributed by governments, either
      directly or through publicly funded agencies. ([services de
      santé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[social services]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[social services]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means residential or
      institutional care, including the provision of devices related to
      the care, that is recommended by a health professional and for
      which more than half of the funds are contributed by governments,
      either directly or through publicly funded agencies. ([services
      sociaux]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[student]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[student]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2014-14, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[studies]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[studies]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2014-14, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[study permit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[study permit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2014-14, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation --- common-law partner

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of the Act and these Regulations, an
  individual who has been in a conjugal relationship with a person for
  at least one year but is unable to cohabit with the person, due to
  persecution or any form of penal control, shall be considered a
  common-law partner of the person.

- Definition of [[family member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of the Act, other than section 12
  and paragraph 38(2)(d), and for the purposes of these Regulations,
  other than paragraph 7.1(3)(a) and sections 159.1 and 159.5, [[family
  member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in respect of a person means

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the spouse or common-law partner of the person;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a dependent child of the person or of the person's
    spouse or common-law partner; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a dependent child of a dependent child referred to
    in paragraph (b).

::: HistoricalNote

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
[[agent]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} --- section 148 of the Act

- **[[1.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of section 148 of the Act, agent includes any person ---
  whether or not an independent contractor --- who provides services as
  a representative of a vehicle owner, operator or charterer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[agent]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} --- paragraph 148(1)(d) of the Act

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph 148(1)(d) of the Act,
  agent includes, in addition to a person referred to in subsection (1),
  a charterer and an owner or operator of a reservation system.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-37, s. 1
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

**[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** The definitions in this
section apply in these Regulations.

[[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Immigration and Refugee
    Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5). ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[administration fee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[administration fee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a portion of the
    average cost incurred by Her Majesty in right of Canada in respect
    of foreign nationals referred to in subsection 279(1), and includes
    the costs relating to

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} examinations;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} detention;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} investigations and admissibility hearings in
      respect of inadmissible foreign nationals;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} fingerprinting, photographing and the
      verification of documents with other governments and national or
      international police agencies;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} translation and interpretation; and

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} proceedings before the Immigration
      Division. ([frais administratifs]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[agent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[agent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2016-37, s.
    2\]]{.Repealed}

[[authorized representative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[authorized representative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/2011-129, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[Canadian citizen]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Canadian citizen]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a citizen referred to
    in subsection 3(1) of the [Citizenship
    Act](/eng/acts/C-29). ([citoyen canadien]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[Canadian Language Benchmarks]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Canadian Language Benchmarks]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, for the
    English language, the Canadian Language Benchmarks: English as a
    Second Language for Adults developed by the Centre for Canadian
    Language Benchmarks, as amended from time to time. ([Canadian
    Language Benchmarks]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[commercial transporter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[commercial transporter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a transporter
    who operates a commercial vehicle. ([transporteur
    commercial]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[commercial vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[commercial vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle that is
    used for commercial purposes. ([véhicule
    commercial]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[conjugal partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[conjugal partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in relation to a
    sponsor, a foreign national residing outside Canada who is in a
    conjugal relationship with the sponsor and has been in that
    relationship for a period of at least one year. ([partenaire
    conjugal]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[COVID-19]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[COVID-19]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means coronovirus disease
    2019. ([COVID-19]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Department]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Department]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Department of
    Citizenship and Immigration. ([ministère]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[dependent child]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[dependent child]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a parent,
    means a child who

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has one of the following relationships with the
      parent, namely,

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is the biological child of the parent, if the
        child has not been adopted by a person other than the spouse or
        common-law partner of the parent, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is the adopted child of the parent; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is in one of the following situations of
      dependency, namely,

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is less than 22 years of age and is not a
        spouse or common-law partner, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is 22 years of age or older and has depended
        substantially on the financial support of the parent since
        before attaining the age of 22 years and is unable to be
        financially self-supporting due to a physical or mental
        condition. ([enfant à charge]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[guardianship]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[guardianship]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2005-61, s.
    1\]]{.Repealed}

[[Hague Convention on Adoption]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Hague Convention on Adoption]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
    Convention on the Protection of Children and Co-operation in respect
    of Inter-Country Adoption that was concluded on May 29, 1993 and
    came into force on May 1, 1995. ([Convention sur
    l'adoption]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Indian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Indian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any person registered as an
    Indian under the [Indian
    Act](/eng/acts/I-5). ([Indien]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[in-flight security officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[in-flight security officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person
    who is on board a commercial passenger aircraft and whose duty it is
    to protect the passengers and the members of the crew as well as the
    aircraft itself. ([agent de sécurité aérien]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[in-transit passenger]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[in-transit passenger]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who
    arrives by aircraft at a Canadian airport from another country for
    the sole purpose of reboarding their flight or boarding a connecting
    flight departing from that airport to a country other than
    Canada. ([passager en transit]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[in-transit preclearance passenger]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[in-transit preclearance passenger]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
    in-transit passenger who is subject to a preclearance procedure in
    accordance with the [Preclearance Act,
    2016](/eng/acts/P-19.32). ([passager en transit bénéficiant d'un
    précontrôle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[live-in caregiver]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[live-in caregiver]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/2017-78, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[marriage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[marriage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a marriage that took
    place outside Canada, means a marriage that is valid both under the
    laws of the jurisdiction where it took place and under Canadian
    law. ([mariage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[minimum necessary income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[minimum necessary income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the amount
    identified, in the most recent edition of the publication concerning
    low income cut-offs that is published annually by Statistics Canada
    under the [Statistics Act](/eng/acts/S-19), for urban areas of
    residence of 500,000 persons or more as the minimum amount of
    before-tax annual income necessary to support a group of persons
    equal in number to the total number of the following persons:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a sponsor and their family members,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the sponsored foreign national, and their family
      members, whether they are accompanying the foreign national or
      not, and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} every other person, and their family members,

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in respect of whom the sponsor has given or
        co-signed an undertaking that is still in effect, and

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in respect of whom the sponsor's spouse or
        common-law partner has given or co-signed an undertaking that is
        still in effect, if the sponsor's spouse or common-law partner
        has co-signed with the sponsor the undertaking in respect of the
        foreign national referred to in paragraph (b). ([revenu vital
        minimum]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister referred to in
    section 4 of the Act. ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[National Occupational Classification]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[National Occupational Classification]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
    the National Occupational Classification implemented by the
    Department of Employment and Social Development, as amended from
    time to time. ([Classification nationale des
    professions]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Niveaux de compétence linguistique canadiens]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Niveaux de compétence linguistique
    canadiens]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, for the French language, the
    [Niveaux de compétence linguistique canadiens : français langue
    seconde pour adultes]{.otherLang lang="fr"} developed by the Centre
    for Canadian Language Benchmarks, as amended from time to
    time. ([Niveaux de compétence linguistique
    canadiens]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person designated as an
    officer by the Minister under subsection 6(1) of the
    Act. ([agent]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[port of entry]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[port of entry]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2020-55, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a place designated by the Minister under section
      26 as a port of entry, on the dates and during the hours of
      operation designated for that place by the Minister. ([point
      d'entrée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[relative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[relative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who is related to
    another person by blood or adoption. ([membre de la
    parenté]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[social assistance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[social assistance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any benefit in the
    form of money, goods or services provided to or on behalf of a
    person by a province under a program of social assistance, including
    a program of social assistance designated by a province to provide
    for basic requirements including food, shelter, clothing, fuel,
    utilities, household supplies, personal requirements and health care
    not provided by public health care, including dental care and eye
    care. ([assistance sociale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[sterile transit area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[sterile transit area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an area in an
    airport where in-transit passengers, in-transit preclearance
    passengers or goods that are in transit or precontrolled are
    physically separated from other passengers and goods. ([espace de
    transit isolé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[study permit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[study permit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a written authorization
    to engage in academic, professional, vocational or other education
    or training in Canada that is issued by an officer to a foreign
    national. ([permis d'études]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[TEER Category]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[TEER Category]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a category representing
    the level of training, formal education and experience required to
    gain entry into an occupation, and the responsibilities related to
    the occupation, as set out in the National Occupational
    Classification. ([catégorie FÉER]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[time of departure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[time of departure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a commercial vehicle that carries
      persons or goods by air, the time of take-off from the last point
      of embarkation of persons before the vehicle arrives in Canada;
      and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a commercial vehicle that carries
      persons or goods by water or land, the time of departure from the
      last point of embarkation of persons before the vehicle arrives in
      Canada. ([moment du départ]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[transporter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[transporter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person who owns, operates, charters or manages
      a vehicle or fleet of vehicles;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person who owns or operates an international
      tunnel or bridge;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a designated airport authority as defined in
      subsection 2(1) of the [Airport Transfer (Miscellaneous Matters)
      Act](/eng/acts/A-10.4); or

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an agent for a person or authority referred to in
      paragraphs (a) to (c). ([transporteur]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a means of transportation that
    may be used for transportation by water, land or
    air. ([véhicule]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vessel within the meaning of
    section 2 of the [Canada Shipping
    Act](/eng/acts/S-9). ([bâtiment]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an activity for which wages are
    paid or commission is earned, or that is in direct competition with
    the activities of Canadian citizens or permanent residents in the
    Canadian labour market. ([travail]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[work permit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[work permit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a written authorization to
    work in Canada issued by an officer to a foreign national. ([permis
    de travail]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation --- member of a crew

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of these Regulations,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} [[member of a crew]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    person who is employed on a means of transportation to perform
    duties during a voyage or trip, or while in port, related to the
    operation of the means of transportation or the provision of
    services to passengers or to other members of the crew, but does not
    include

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any person whose fare is waived in exchange for
      work to be performed during the voyage or trip,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any person who performs maintenance or repairs
      under a service contract with a transporter during the voyage or
      trip or while the means of transportation is in Canada,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any other person who is on board the means of
      transportation for a purpose other than to perform duties that
      relate to the operation of the means of transportation or to
      provide services to passengers or members of the crew, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} any in-flight security officer; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person ceases to be a member of a crew if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} they have deserted;

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an officer believes on reasonable grounds that
      they have deserted;

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} they have been hospitalized and have failed to
      return to the means of transportation or leave Canada after
      leaving the hospital, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} they have been discharged or are otherwise
      unable or unwilling to perform their duties as a member of a crew
      and failed to leave Canada after the discharge or after they first
      became unable or unwilling to perform those duties.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation --- adoption

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of these Regulations,
  [[adoption]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, for greater certainty, means an
  adoption that creates a legal parent-child relationship and severs the
  pre-existing legal parent-child relationship.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-167, s. 2
- SOR/2010-253, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-3-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 3"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Family Relationships]{.HTitleText2} {#h-685476 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Bad faith

- **[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of these Regulations, a foreign national shall not be
  considered a spouse, a common-law partner or a conjugal partner of a
  person if the marriage, common-law partnership or conjugal partnership

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} was entered into primarily for the purpose of
    acquiring any status or privilege under the Act; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is not genuine.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adopted children

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A foreign national shall not be considered an adopted
  child of a person if the adoption

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} was entered into primarily for the purpose of
    acquiring any status or privilege under the Act; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} did not create a genuine parent-child relationship.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sponsorship of adopted children

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not apply to adoptions referred
  to in paragraph 117(1)(g) and subsections 117(2) and (4).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-167, s. 3(E)
- SOR/2010-208, s. 1
- SOR/2018-251, s. 1(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-4-20100930.html "Link to previous version of section 4"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}New relationship

**[[4.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4.1 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
these Regulations, a foreign national shall not be considered a spouse,
a common-law partner or a conjugal partner of a person if the foreign
national has begun a new conjugal relationship with that person after a
previous marriage, common-law partnership or conjugal partnership with
that person was dissolved primarily so that the foreign national,
another foreign national or the sponsor could acquire any status or
privilege under the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-167, s. 4
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Excluded relationships

**[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of these
Regulations, a foreign national shall not be considered

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the spouse or common-law partner of a person if the
  foreign national is under the age of 18 years;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the spouse of a person if

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national or the person was, at the time
    of their marriage, the spouse of another person, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person has lived separate and apart from the
    foreign national for at least one year and is the common-law partner
    of another person; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the spouse of a person if at the time the marriage
  ceremony was conducted either one or both of the spouses were not
  physically present unless the person was not physically present at the
  ceremony as a result of their service as a member of the Canadian
  Forces and the marriage is valid both under the laws of the
  jurisdiction where it took place and under Canadian law.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-139, s. 1
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-5-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 5"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 2]{.HLabel1}[General Requirements]{.HTitleText1} {#h-685503 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Documents Required Before Entry]{.HTitleText2} {#h-685504 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permanent resident

**[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** A foreign national may not
enter Canada to remain on a permanent basis without first obtaining a
permanent resident visa.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Temporary resident

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  foreign national may not enter Canada to remain on a temporary basis
  without first obtaining a temporary resident visa.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a foreign national
  who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is exempted under Division 5 of Part 9 from the
    requirement to have a temporary resident visa;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} holds a temporary resident permit issued under
    subsection 24(1) of the Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is authorized under the Act or these Regulations to
    re-enter Canada to remain in Canada; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2023-106, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When *[certificat d'acceptation du
  Québec]{.otherLang lang="fr"}* required

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In addition to any visa required under this section,
  a foreign national who is seeking to enter and remain in Canada on a
  temporary basis to receive medical treatment in the Province of Quebec
  must hold a *[certificat d'acceptation du Québec]{.otherLang
  lang="fr"}* if the laws of that Province require the foreign national
  to hold that document.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-109, s. 1
- [SOR/2023-106, s. 1](#1399000-1400946){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1399000-1400946.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-7-20180605.html "Link to previous version of section 7"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Nationals of certain countries

- **[[7.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7.01
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection 7(1), a foreign
  national who is a citizen of a country listed in the table to this
  subsection and who is seeking to enter Canada by air may not enter
  Canada to remain on a temporary basis without first obtaining a
  temporary resident visa or an electronic travel authorization.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for electronic travel
  authorization --- conditions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A foreign national referred to in subsection (1) may
  only make an application for an electronic travel authorization if
  they

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} have held a temporary resident visa at any time
    during the 10-year period immediately preceding the day on which
    they make their application; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} hold a valid United States nonimmigrant visa on the
    day on which they make their application.

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-7.01-20230606.html "Link to previous version of section 7.01"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electronic travel authorization

- **[[7.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  foreign national referred to in paragraph 7(2)(a) who is exempt from
  the requirement to obtain a temporary resident visa and who is seeking
  to enter Canada by air to remain on a temporary basis is required to
  obtain an electronic travel authorization before entering Canada,
  unless they are exempt from that requirement under subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Holder of temporary resident visa

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a foreign national
  who holds a temporary resident visa.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exempt persons

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The following persons are exempt from the requirement
  to obtain an electronic travel authorization:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Her Majesty in right of Canada and any member of
    the Royal Family;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a national of the United States or a person who has
    been lawfully admitted to the United States for permanent residence;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a foreign national referred to in paragraph
    190(2)(a);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a foreign national seeking to enter and remain in
    Canada solely

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} as a member of a crew of a means of
      transportation that may be used for transportation by air or to
      become a member of such a crew, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to transit through Canada after working, or to
      work, as a member of a crew of a means of transportation that may
      be used for transportation by air, if they possess a ticket for
      departure from Canada within 24 hours after their arrival in
      Canada;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a citizen of France who is a resident of St. Pierre
    and Miquelon who seeks to enter Canada directly from St. Pierre and
    Miquelon; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a foreign national referred to in any of paragraphs
    190(3)(b), (b.1), (c), (d), (f), (g) or (h).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-77, s. 2
- SOR/2017-53, s. 2
- SOR/2018-109, s. 2
- [SOR/2019-212, s. 10(F)](#1138241-1145984){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1138241-1145984.html"}
- [SOR/2022-80, s. 1](#1343214-1344568){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1343214-1344568.html"}
- [SOR/2023-106, s. 3](#1399003-1400955){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1399003-1400955.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-7.1-20220426.html "Link to previous version of section 7.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Work permit

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  foreign national may not enter Canada to work without first obtaining
  a work permit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a foreign national
  who is authorized under section 186 to work in Canada without a work
  permit.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Study permit

- **[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  foreign national may not enter Canada to study without first obtaining
  a study permit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a foreign national
  who is authorized under section 188 or 189 to study in Canada without
  a study permit.

### [DIVISION 1.1]{.HLabel2}[Use of Electronic Means]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1137913 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electronic means

- **[[9.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 10, when an
  application, request or claim is made, a document or information is
  submitted electronically, or a signature by electronic means is
  provided, it must be made, submitted or provided using the electronic
  means that is made available or specified by the Minister for that
  purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Supporting documents for electronic
  applications

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When an application, request or claim is made by
  electronic means, any required information, documents or evidence of
  payment required in these Regulations must be submitted by the same
  means.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-174, s. 1](#1135464-1137974){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1135464-1137974.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application by electronic means

**[[9.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9.2 .sectionLabel}** The following
applications must be made by the electronic means made available or
specified by the Minister for that purpose:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an application for an extension of an authorization
  to remain in Canada as a temporary resident;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} an application that is made in Canada for a work or
  study permit or for renewal of such a permit;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} an application for the restoration of temporary
  resident status;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} an application that is made pursuant to a youth
  mobility arrangement entered into by Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-174, s. 1](#1135464-1137982){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1135464-1137982.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed receipt by the Minister

- **[[9.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9.3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  application, request, claim, document or information sent by a foreign
  national, an individual or an entity using electronic means is deemed
  to have been received by the Minister at the time and on the date
  indicated by the electronic means that is made available or specified
  by the Minister for that purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed receipt by recipient

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any notice, request, decision, determination,
  document or information sent by the Minister using electronic means is
  deemed to have been received by the intended recipient at the time and
  on the date it was sent by the Minister as indicated by the electronic
  means that is used or specified by the Minister for that purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-174, s. 1](#1135464-1137989){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1135464-1137989.html"}
- [SOR/2023-249, s. 1(E)](#1429140-1429227){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1429140-1429227.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-9.3-20190603.html "Link to previous version of section 9.3"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- use of other means

**[[9.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9.4 .sectionLabel}** Despite other
provisions of these Regulations, the Minister may require a foreign
national, an individual or an entity to make an application, request or
claim, submit any document or information or provide a signature by any
other means made available or specified by the Minister for that purpose
if the Minister determines that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the infrastructure in a country is inadequate or
  incompatible with the electronic means made available or specified by
  the Minister for that purpose; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a natural disaster, political turmoil or other
  situation prevents or impedes the use of electronic means or makes
  using them unreliable.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-174, s. 1](#1135464-1137995){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1135464-1137995.html"}
- [SOR/2023-249, s. 2(E)](#1429142-1429229){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1429142-1429229.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-9.4-20190603.html "Link to previous version of section 9.4"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disability

**[[9.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9.5 .sectionLabel}** A foreign national or
an individual who, because of a disability is unable to meet a
requirement to make an application, request or claim, submit any
document or provide a signature or information using electronic means,
may do so by any other means that is made available or specified by the
Minister for that purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-174, s. 1](#1135464-1137999){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1135464-1137999.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment of fees

- **[[9.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9.6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  fees to be paid or evidence of payment of fees referred to in these
  Regulations must be paid or provided by the electronic means that is
  made available or specified by the Minister for that purpose except in
  the following circumstances:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if services for the collection of payment are
    provided by an entity under an agreement or arrangement with Canada
    for that purpose; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if fees may be paid at a port of entry by other
    means.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment in exceptional circumstances

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), the required fees may be paid
  or evidence of payment provided by any other means that is made
  available or specified by the Minister for that purpose if the
  Minister determines that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the infrastructure in a country is inadequate or
    incompatible with the electronic means made available or specified
    by the Minister for that purpose; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a natural disaster, political turmoil or other
    situation prevents or impedes the use of electronic means or makes
    using them unreliable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed receipt

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any fee paid by electronic means is deemed to be paid
  on the date and at the time recorded by the electronic means that is
  made available or specified by the Minister for that purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- disability

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A foreign national or an individual who, because of a
  disability is unable to pay the required fees referred to in
  subsection (1) by electronic means, may do so by other means that is
  made available or specified by the Minister for that purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-174, s. 1](#1135464-1138014){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1135464-1138014.html"}
:::

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Applications]{.HTitleText2} {#h-685562 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form and content of application

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to paragraphs 28(b) to (d)
  and 139(1)(b), an application under these Regulations shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be made in writing using the form, if any, provided
    by the Department or, in the case of an application for a
    declaration of relief under subsection 42.1(1) of the Act, by the
    Canada Border Services Agency;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be signed by the applicant;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} include all information and documents required by
    these Regulations, as well as any other evidence required by the
    Act;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} be accompanied by evidence of payment of the
    applicable fee, if any, set out in these Regulations; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if there is an accompanying spouse or common-law
    partner, identify who is the principal applicant and who is the
    accompanying spouse or common-law partner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Required information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The application shall, unless otherwise provided by
  these Regulations,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} contain the name, birth date, address, nationality
    and immigration status of the applicant and of all family members of
    the applicant, whether accompanying or not, and a statement whether
    the applicant or any of the family members is the spouse, common-law
    partner or conjugal partner of another person;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} indicate whether they are applying for a visa,
    permit or authorization;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} indicate the class prescribed by these Regulations
    for which the application is made;

  - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} if the applicant is represented in connection
    with the application, include the name, postal address and telephone
    number, and fax number and electronic mail address, if any, of any
    person or entity --- or a person acting on its behalf ---
    representing the applicant;

  - [(c.2)]{.lawlabel} if the applicant is represented, for
    consideration in connection with the application, by a person
    referred to in any of paragraphs 91(2)(a) to (c) of the Act, include
    the name of the body of which the person is a member and their
    membership identification number;

  - [(c.3)]{.lawlabel} if the applicant has been advised, for
    consideration in connection with the application, by a person
    referred to in any of paragraphs 91(2)(a) to (c) of the Act, include
    the information referred to in paragraphs (c.1) and (c.2) with
    respect to that person;

  - [(c.4)]{.lawlabel} if the applicant has been advised, for
    consideration in connection with the application, by an entity ---
    or a person acting on its behalf --- referred to in subsection 91(4)
    of the Act, include the information referred to in paragraph (c.1)
    with respect to that entity or person; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} include a declaration that the information provided
    is complete and accurate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- adoption

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} An application to sponsor and the accompanying
  application for permanent residence for a person described in
  paragraph 117(1)(b) involving an international adoption or another
  person referred to in paragraph 117(1)(g) can be submitted without the
  information referred to in paragraph 10(2)(a) concerning that person
  but that information must be provided before the application is
  approved.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clarification

  [(2.2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, neither the information
  referred to in subparagraphs 12.3(b)(i) and (ii) nor the fee referred
  to in section 315.1 form part of the application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of family members

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The application is considered to be an application
  made for the principal applicant and their accompanying family
  members.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application --- sponsorship

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An application made by a foreign national as a member
  of the family class must be accompanied by a sponsorship application
  referred to in paragraph 130(1)(c).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Multiple applications

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} No sponsorship application may be filed by a sponsor
  in respect of a person if the sponsor has filed another sponsorship
  application in respect of that same person and a final decision has
  not been made in respect of that other application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Invalid sponsorship application

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A sponsorship application that is not made in
  accordance with subsection (1) is considered not to be an application
  filed in the prescribed manner for the purposes of subsection 63(1) of
  the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-59, s. 2

- SOR/2011-129, s. 2

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application --- other means

**[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** If an application is not
made by electronic means it must be submitted to the address specified
by the Minister, including the address specified on the Department's
website for that purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-167, s. 6
- SOR/2012-154, s. 2

Version](section-11-20121018.html "Link to previous version of section 11"){.PITLink}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of application

**[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 140.4,
if the requirements of sections 10 and 11 are not met, the application
and all documents submitted in support of it, except the information
referred to in subparagraphs 12.3(b)(i) and (ii), shall be returned to
the applicant.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-225, s. 3
- SOR/2018-128, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-12-20121018.html "Link to previous version of section 12"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request or application at port of entry

**[[12.001]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.001 .sectionLabel}** Subject to
sections 198 and 214, a person --- who under section 10.01 of the Act is
required to provide their biometric information --- may only make a
request for a temporary resident permit under subsection 24(1) of the
Act or an application for a work or study permit at a port of entry if
that port of entry provides collection services for biometric
information.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-128, s. 2
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Invitation --- application for permanent
residence

**[[12.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.01 .sectionLabel}** An application for
permanent residence that is made in response to an invitation issued by
the Minister under Division 0.1 of the Act must be made by the
electronic means that is made available or specified by the Minister for
that purpose.

SOR/2019-174, s. 4\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-12.03-20150101.html "Link to previous version of section 12.03"){.PITLink}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electronic travel authorization ---
application by electronic means

- **[[12.04]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.04
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 10, an application
  for a travel authorization under subsection 11(1.01) of the Act must
  be made by the electronic system that is made available or specified
  by the Minister for that purpose.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2019-174, s. 5\]]{.Repealed}

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2019-174, s. 5\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Required information

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The application must contain the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's name;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's date and place of birth;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's gender;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's address;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's nationality;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the number of the applicant's passport or other
    travel document, together with its date of issue and its expiry date
    and the country or the authority that issued it;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if the applicant is an applicant referred to in any
    of paragraphs 10(2)(c.1) to (c.4), the information required under
    that paragraph;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} if the applicant is making the application by the
    electronic system referred to in subsection (1), the applicant's
    e-mail address;

  - [(h.1)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a foreign national referred to in
    paragraph 7.01(2)(b), the valid United States nonimmigrant visa
    number; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a declaration that the information provided in the
    application is complete and accurate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Combined applications --- foreign
  nationals referred to in subsection 7.01(1)

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} An application for a renewal of a work permit or
  study permit that is made by a foreign national referred to in
  subsection 7.01(1) is considered to constitute an application for an
  electronic travel authorization.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Combined applications --- foreign
  nationals referred to in subsection 7.1(1)

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} An application for a work permit or study permit or
  for renewal of a work permit or study permit that is made by a foreign
  national who is required under subsection 7.1(1) to obtain an
  electronic travel authorization is considered to constitute an
  application for an electronic travel authorization.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-77, s. 3
- SOR/2017-53, s. 3

- [SOR/2019-174, s. 6](#1135477-1138035){.wb-lbx

:::

[Previous
Version](section-12.04-20170501.html "Link to previous version of section 12.04"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of validity

- **[[12.05]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.05
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An electronic travel authorization
  is valid for a period of five years from the day on which it is issued
  to the applicant or until the earliest of the following days, if they
  occur before the end of that period:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the applicant's passport or other
    travel document, in respect of which the electronic travel
    authorization was issued, expires,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the electronic travel
    authorization is cancelled,

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the day on which a new electronic travel
    authorization, in respect of the same passport or the same travel
    document, is issued to the applicant,

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the country or authority referred
    to in paragraph 190(1)(a) that issued the passport or other travel
    document in respect of which the electronic travel authorization was
    issued is no longer referred to in that paragraph, and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the country referred to in
    subsection 7.01(1) that issued the passport or other travel document
    in respect of which the electronic travel authorization was issued
    is no longer referred to in that subsection, unless that country is
    listed in Schedule 1.1.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}United States lawful permanent
  residents

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Any electronic travel authorization that is issued
  to a citizen of a country other than those listed in Schedule 1.1 on
  the basis of their lawful admission to the United States for permanent
  residence ceases to be valid at 08:00:00 Eastern daylight time on
  April 26, 2022.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- Mexico

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(d) does not apply to an electronic
  travel authorization issued to a foreign national who is a citizen of
  Mexico if, on the same day referred to in that paragraph, the foreign
  national holds a work permit or study permit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(d) does not apply to an electronic
  travel authorization that is issued in respect of a machine-readable
  passport that contains a contactless integrated circuit chip and that
  is issued by Romania.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ineligibility

**[[12.06]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.06 .sectionLabel}** A foreign national
who holds an electronic travel authorization becomes ineligible to hold
such an authorization if, following its issuance,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} they are the subject of a declaration made under
  subsection 22.1(1) of the Act;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} they were issued a temporary resident permit under
  subsection 24(1) of the Act;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} they are the subject of a report prepared under
  subsection 44(1) of the Act;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} they are the subject of a removal order made under
  subsection 44(2) or paragraph 45(d) of the Act;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} they withdrew their application to enter Canada under
  subsection 42(1);

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} they were refused a temporary resident visa because
  they did not meet the requirement set out in paragraph 179(b);

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} they were refused a work permit because they did not
  meet the requirement set out in paragraph 200(1)(b);

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} they were refused a study permit because they did not
  meet the requirement set out in paragraph 216(1)(b);

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a foreign national referred to in
  subsection 7.01(1), it is established that, on the day on which they
  made their application for an electronic travel authorization, they
  did not meet either of the conditions set out in paragraphs 7.01(2)(a)
  and (b);

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} an officer has reasonable grounds to believe that the
  foreign national will not leave Canada by the end of the period
  authorized for their stay under Division 2 of Part 9; or

- [(k)]{.lawlabel} they were refused a work permit or study permit in
  the course of an application for or renewal of a work permit or study
  permit referred to in subsection 12.04(6).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-77, s. 3

:::
