:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation

**[[12.07]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.07 .sectionLabel}** An officer may
cancel an electronic travel authorization that was issued to a foreign
national if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national is inadmissible;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national becomes ineligible to hold such
  an authorization under section 12.06; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the officer is satisfied that the electronic travel
  authorization was issued based on an administrative error.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-53, s. 5
- [SOR/2025-11, s. 2](#1508527-1508721){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1508527-1508721.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-12.07-20170501.html "Link to previous version of section 12.07"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation

**[[12.08]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.08 .sectionLabel}** An electronic
travel authorization that was issued to a foreign national is cancelled
if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national becomes a permanent resident;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the passport or other travel document in respect of
  which the electronic travel authorization was issued is lost, stolen
  or destroyed;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the passport or other travel document in respect of
  which the electronic travel authorization was issued is abandoned by
  the foreign national; or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national is deceased.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-11, s. 2](#1508527-1508734){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1508527-1508734.html"}
:::

### [DIVISION 2.1]{.HLabel2}[Collection and Verification of Biometric Information]{.HTitleText2} {#h-685699 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Collection --- claims, applications, and
requests

**[[12.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.1 .sectionLabel}** Section 10.01 of the
Act does not apply to claims, applications and requests under the Act,
other than the following:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an application for a permanent resident visa;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} an application to remain in Canada as a permanent
  resident;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} an application for permanent resident status made by
  a person who, when they made their claim for refugee protection
  outside Canada, was exempted, under section 12.8, from providing their
  biometric information under section 10.01 of the Act;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} an application for a temporary resident visa;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} an application for an extension of authorization to
  remain in Canada as a temporary resident;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} a request for a temporary resident permit;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} an application for restoration of temporary resident
  status;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} an application for a work permit or renewal of a work
  permit;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} an application for a study permit or renewal of a
  study permit;

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} an application to remain in Canada as a permanent
  resident, made in Canada by a protected person, within the meaning of
  subsection 95(2) of the Act;

- [(k)]{.lawlabel} a claim for refugee protection, made in Canada;

- [(l)]{.lawlabel} a claim for refugee protection, made outside Canada;
  and

- [(m)]{.lawlabel} an application for a new permanent resident card made
  for the first time by a person, 14 years of age or older, who, when
  they made an application referred to in any of paragraphs (a) to (c),
  was exempted under paragraph 12.2(1)(a) from providing their biometric
  information under section 10.01 of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-73, s. 1
- SOR/2018-128, s. 3
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-12.1-20130429.html "Link to previous version of section 12.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application --- persons

- **[[12.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Section 10.01 of the Act does not
  apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person who is under the age of 14;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person who is over the age of 79, unless that
    person makes a claim in Canada for refugee protection;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a person who makes an application or request
    referred to in any of paragraphs 12.1(e) to (i) if they are

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} Her Majesty in right of Canada and any member of
      the Royal Family, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a national of the United States;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a person who makes an application referred to in
    paragraph 12.1(h) or (i) if they are

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in Canada and have made a claim for refugee
      protection that has not yet been determined by the Refugee
      Protection Division,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in Canada and have had refugee protection
      conferred on them, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a member of the Convention refugees abroad
      class or a member of a humanitarian-protected persons abroad
      class;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a person who is a head of state or head of
    government who makes an application or request referred to in any of
    paragraphs 12.1(d) to (i);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a person who seeks to enter Canada in the course of
    official duties as a properly accredited diplomat, consular officer,
    representative or official of a country other than Canada, of the
    United Nations or any of its agencies or of any intergovernmental
    organization of which Canada is a member;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a person who is a family member of a person
    referred to in paragraph (f);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a person who holds a valid United States entry
    visa, is destined to or returning from that country and seeks to
    enter Canada for a period of less than 48 hours and who

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is travelling by transporter's vehicle to a
      destination other than Canada, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is transiting through or stopping over in Canada
      for refuelling or for the continuation of their journey in another
      vehicle of a transporter;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a foreign national referred to in paragraph 7(2)(a)
    who makes an application for temporary resident status as a member
    of the visitor class; or

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a foreign national referred to in subsection
    7.01(1) who is the holder of an electronic travel authorization and
    makes an application for temporary resident status as a member of
    the visitor class.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of age

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (1), the age of a
  person shall be determined as of the date on which the application,
  claim or request is made.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2018-128, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-128, s. 3
- [SOR/2023-249, s. 10(F)](#1429156-1429254){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1429156-1429254.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-12.2-20181231.html "Link to previous version of section 12.2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Procedure for collection of biometric
information

**[[12.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.3 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
section 10.01 of the Act, the procedure for the collection of biometric
information is the following:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person who makes a claim, application or request
  shall present themself at:

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a location where services for the collection of
    biometric information are provided by an entity under an agreement
    or arrangement with the Minister for that purpose, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} at an immigration office, a port of entry or
    another location, if authorized or directed by an officer to do so,
    for

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} reasons relating to the national interest,

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} reasons relating to operational requirements, or

    - [(C)]{.lawlabel} any other reason that may be necessary in the
      circumstances; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} they shall have their photograph taken --- showing
  the person's face unobscured by sunglasses or any other object --- and
  fingerprints captured, at any location referred to in subparagraph
  (a)(i) or (ii), for the collection of the following information:

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} photograph, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} fingerprints.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-128, s. 3
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Processing of biometric information

**[[12.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.4 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
section 10.01 of the Act, a biometric template may be created, and the
information collected under paragraph 12.3(b) may be converted into a
digital biometric format by using the template.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-128, s. 3
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Verification of biometric information ---
procedure

**[[12.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.5 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
section 10.01 of the Act, the procedure for the verification of
biometric information to be followed by a person who has made a claim,
application or request referred to in any of paragraphs 12.1(a) to (m)
is that, on seeking to enter Canada and when directed by an officer or
by alternative means of examination referred to in paragraph 38(b), the
person shall provide the information listed in subparagraphs 12.3(b)(i)
and (ii) by means of an electronic device made available for that
purpose, in order to verify the biometric information that they provided
under paragraph 12.3(b).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-128, s. 3
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- multiple applications or
requests

**[[12.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.6 .sectionLabel}** A person who makes
more than one application or request before providing their biometric
information referred to in any of paragraphs 12.1(d) to (i) is not
required to provide their biometric information more than once with
respect to those applications or requests.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-128, s. 3
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- biometric information
previously provided

- **[[12.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.7
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 12.9, a person
  who makes an application or request referred to in any of paragraphs
  12.1(d) to (i) is not required to provide their biometric information
  under section 10.01 of the Act if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} they had already provided it --- under subsection
    12.1(1) of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2002-227) as they read immediately
    before the coming into force of this paragraph --- in support of an
    application referred to in that subsection, within the 10-year
    period before the day on which they make the current application;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} they had already provided it --- under section
    10.01 of the Act --- in support of an application or request
    referred to in any of paragraphs 12.1(d) to (i), within the 10-year
    period before the day on which they make the current application or
    request; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (2), they had already
    provided it under section 10.01 of the Act when they made a claim or
    application referred to in any of paragraphs 12.1(a) to (c) or (j)
    to (l) and that claim or application is still pending on the day on
    which they make an application or request referred to in any of
    paragraphs 12.1(d) to (i).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(c) ceases to apply on the latest of the
  expiry dates of any work permit or study permit --- including any
  renewal of them --- any temporary resident visa or any temporary
  resident permit having an expiry date that was issued to the person if
  that person made the application for the permit or visa during the
  processing of their claim or application, referred to in any of
  paragraphs 12.1(a) to (c) or (j) to (l), and the claim or application
  referred to in any of paragraphs 12.1(a) to (c) or (j) to (l) is
  refused.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-128, s. 3
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- collection of biometric
information impossible or not feasible

**[[12.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.8 .sectionLabel}** A person who makes a
claim, application or request referred to in any of paragraphs 12.1(a)
to (m) is not required to provide, with respect to the claim,
application or request in question, the information referred to in
subparagraph 12.3(b)(i) or (ii), as the case may be, if the collection
is impossible or not feasible.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-128, s. 3
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

**[[12.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.9 .sectionLabel}** Paragraph 12.7(1)(b)
does not apply to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person referred to in paragraph 12.7(1)(b) who was
  exempted under section 12.8 from providing certain biometric
  information for a reason relating to a temporary situation if the
  situation no longer exists; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person referred to in paragraph 12.7(1)(c).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-128, s. 3
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clarification

**[[12.91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.91 .sectionLabel}** For greater
certainty, despite sections 12.7 to 12.9, a person may choose to provide
their biometric information under section 10.01 of the Act in support of
their claim, application or request.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-128, s. 3
:::

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Documents and Certified Copies]{.HTitleText2} {#h-685791 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Production of documents

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), a
  requirement of the Act or these Regulations to produce a document is
  met

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} by producing the original document;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by producing a certified copy of the original
    document; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an application, if there is an
    application form on the Department's website, by completing and
    producing the form printed from the website or by completing and
    submitting the form on-line, if the website indicates that the form
    can be submitted on-line.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Unless these Regulations provide otherwise, a
  passport, a permanent resident visa, a permanent resident card, a
  temporary resident visa, a temporary resident permit, a work permit or
  a study permit may be produced only by producing the original
  document.

### [DIVISION 4]{.HLabel2}[Disclosure of Information]{.HTitleText2} {#h-685800 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized disclosure

**[[13.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13.1 .sectionLabel}** If a member of the
Board or an officer determines that the conduct of a person referred to
in any of paragraphs 91(2)(a) to (c) of the Act in connection with a
proceeding --- other than a proceeding before a superior court --- or
application under the Act is likely to constitute a breach of the
person's professional or ethical obligations, the Department, the Canada
Border Services Agency or the Board, as the case may be, may disclose
the following information to a body that is responsible for governing or
investigating that conduct or to a person who is responsible for
investigating that conduct:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} any information referred to in paragraphs 10(2)(c.1)
  to (c.3); and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any information relating to that conduct, but --- in
  the case of any information that could identify any other person ---
  only to the extent necessary for the complete disclosure of that
  conduct.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-77, s. 1
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-13.1-20110630.html "Link to previous version of section 13.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 4.1]{.HLabel2}[Use and Disclosure of Biometric Information and Related Personal Information]{.HTitleText2} {#h-685807 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure of information

- **[[13.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13.11
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any biometric information and
  related personal information set out in subsection (2) that is
  collected under the Act and provided to the Royal Canadian Mounted
  Police may be used or disclosed by it to a law enforcement agency in
  Canada for the following purposes, if there is a potential match
  between fingerprints collected under the Act and fingerprints
  collected by it or submitted to it by a law enforcement agency in
  Canada:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to establish or verify the identity of a person in
    order to prevent, investigate or prosecute an offence under any law
    of Canada or a province; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to establish or verify the identity of a person
    whose identity cannot reasonably be otherwise established or
    verified because of a physical or mental condition or because of
    their death.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information that may be used or
  disclosed

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following information in respect of a foreign
  national or a permanent resident may be used or disclosed by the Royal
  Canadian Mounted Police under subsection (1):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} their fingerprints and the date on which they were
    taken;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} their surname and first name;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} their other names and aliases, if any;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} their date of birth;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} their gender; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} any file number associated with the biometric
    information or related personal information.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-73, s. 2
- SOR/2014-83, s. 1
- [SOR/2019-212, s. 1](#1138218-1145949){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1138218-1145949.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-13.11-20140404.html "Link to previous version of section 13.11"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 5]{.HLabel2}[Designated Body --- Information Requirements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-685823 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}General requirement

- **[[13.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  body that is designated under subsection 91(5) of the Act must provide
  to the Minister, within 90 days after the end of each of its fiscal
  years, the following information and documents:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} its most recent annual report;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} its most recent financial statement and the
    auditor's report on that financial statement;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} its instrument of incorporation, with an indication
    of any changes that have been made to that document since the last
    time it provided that document to the Minister in accordance with
    this section;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} its by-laws, with an indication of any changes that
    have been made to those by-laws since the last time it provided them
    to the Minister in accordance with this section;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the minutes of each of the general meetings of its
    members that has been held during its last completed fiscal year;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the terms of reference of its board of directors,
    if any, with an indication of any changes that have been made to
    those terms of reference since the last time it provided them to the
    Minister in accordance with this section;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the conflict of interest code for its directors, if
    any, with an indication of any changes that have been made to that
    code since the last time it provided the code to the Minister in
    accordance with this section;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the name, professional qualifications and term of
    office of each of its directors, with any change in the board of
    director's composition that has occurred since the last time it
    provided the names of its directors to the Minister in accordance
    with this section;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the minutes of each meeting of its board of
    directors that has been held during its last completed fiscal year;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the name, terms of reference and composition of
    each of its executive committees, if any, as well as the name and
    professional qualifications of each of their members;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} the minutes of each meeting of its executive
    committees, if any, that has been held during its last completed
    fiscal year;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} any sums disbursed to its directors and officers as
    remuneration and any cash benefits or financial advantages granted
    to them, during its last completed fiscal year;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} the name and membership number of each of its
    members;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} the rules that govern the conduct of its members,
    with an indication of any changes that have been made to those rules
    since the last time it provided them to the Minister in accordance
    with this section;

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} information, made anonymous, concerning the number
    and type of any complaints that it received during its last
    completed fiscal year in relation to the conduct of any of its
    members, including the distribution of those complaints by type,
    country of origin and, in the case of Canada, province of origin,
    the measures that it took to deal with those complaints and any
    decision that it rendered and sanction that it imposed as a
    consequence of those complaints;

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} information in aggregate form, made anonymous,
    concerning any investigation by it, during its last completed fiscal
    year, into the conduct of any of its members if that conduct likely
    constitutes a breach of their professional or ethical obligations;

  - [(q)]{.lawlabel} the amount of any fees charged by it to its
    members, including its membership fees, with any change in those
    fees that has occurred since the last time it provided that
    information to the Minister in accordance with this section;

  - [(r)]{.lawlabel} the nature and amount of its entertainment,
    hospitality, meal, transport, accommodation, training and incidental
    expenses, if any, that were incurred by any person during its last
    completed fiscal year, as well as the name of the person;

  - [(s)]{.lawlabel} any training requirements that it imposes on its
    members; and

  - [(t)]{.lawlabel} information concerning any training made available
    by it to its members during its last completed fiscal year,
    including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the professional qualifications required of
      trainers,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the identification of the mandatory courses from
      among those on offer,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any evaluation methods and applicable
      completion standards, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the name and professional qualifications of each
      trainer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special requirement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the ability of the designated body to govern its
  members in a manner that is in the public interest so that they
  provide professional and ethical representation and advice appears to
  be compromised, the body must provide to the Minister --- within 10
  business days after the day on which the body receives from the
  Minister a notice indicating the existence of such a situation and
  setting out any information or documents from among those referred to
  in paragraphs (1)(c) to (t) that are necessary to assist the Minister
  to evaluate whether the body governs its members in a manner that is
  in the public interest so that they provide professional and ethical
  representation and advice --- the documents or information set out in
  the notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Redacted information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The information and documents set out in
  subsections (1) and (2) may be provided in redacted form to exclude
  from them information that is subject to litigation privilege or
  solicitor-client privilege or, in civil law, to immunity from
  disclosure or professional secrecy of advocates and notaries.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electronic means

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection 13(1), any information or document
  set out in subsection (1) or (2) may be provided to the Minister by
  electronic means.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-77, s. 1
:::

### [DIVISION 6]{.HLabel2}[Cancellation of Visas or Other Documents]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1508735 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clarification

**[[13.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13.3 .sectionLabel}** For greater
certainty, nothing in these Regulations affects any other lawful
authority to cancel a visa or any other document, including an
electronic travel authorization, temporary resident visa, work permit
and study permit.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-11, s. 3](#1508529-1508744){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1508529-1508744.html"}
:::

## [PART 3]{.HLabel1}[Inadmissibility]{.HTitleText1} {#h-685859 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Determination of Inadmissibility]{.HTitleText2} {#h-685860 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of par. 34(1)(c) of the Act

**[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
determining whether a foreign national or permanent resident is
inadmissible under paragraph 34(1)(c) of the Act, if either the
following determination or decision has been rendered, the findings of
fact set out in that determination or decision shall be considered as
conclusive findings of fact:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a determination by the Board, based on findings that
  the foreign national or permanent resident has engaged in terrorism,
  that the foreign national or permanent resident is a person referred
  to in section F of Article 1 of the Refugee Convention; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a decision by a Canadian court under the [Criminal
  Code](/eng/acts/C-46) concerning the foreign national or permanent
  resident and the commission of a terrorism offence.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of par. 35(1)(a) of the Act

**[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
determining whether a foreign national or permanent resident is
inadmissible under paragraph 35(1)(a) of the Act, if any of the
following decisions or the following determination has been rendered,
the findings of fact set out in that decision or determination shall be
considered as conclusive findings of fact:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a decision concerning the foreign national or
  permanent resident that is made by any international criminal tribunal
  that is established by resolution of the Security Council of the
  United Nations, or the International Criminal Court as defined in the
  [Crimes Against Humanity and War Crimes Act](/eng/acts/C-45.9);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a determination by the Board, based on findings that
  the foreign national or permanent resident has committed a war crime
  or a crime against humanity, that the foreign national or permanent
  resident is a person referred to in section F of Article 1 of the
  Refugee Convention; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a decision by a Canadian court under the [Criminal
  Code](/eng/acts/C-46) or the [Crimes Against Humanity and War Crimes
  Act](/eng/acts/C-45.9) concerning the foreign national or permanent
  resident and a war crime or crime against humanity committed outside
  Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-251, s. 2(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-15-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 15"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of paragraph 35(1)(b) of the
Act

**[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraph 35(1)(b) of the Act, a prescribed senior official is a person
who, by virtue of the position they hold or held, is or was able to
exert significant influence on the exercise of government power or is or
was able to benefit from their position, and includes

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} heads of state or government;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} members of the cabinet or governing council;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} senior advisors to persons described in paragraph (a)
  or (b);

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} senior members of the public service;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} senior members of the military and of the
  intelligence and internal security services;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} ambassadors and senior diplomatic officials; and

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} members of the judiciary.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-136, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-16-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 16"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of paragraphs 37(1)(a) and
(b) of the Act

**[[16.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16.1 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
determining whether a foreign national or permanent resident is
inadmissible under paragraph 37(1)(a) or (b) of the Act, if either of
the following decisions has been rendered, the findings of fact set out
in that decision shall be considered as conclusive findings of fact:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a decision by a Canadian court under section 467.11,
  467.111, 467.12 or 467.13 of the [Criminal Code](/eng/acts/C-46)
  concerning the foreign national or permanent resident; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a sentencing decision by a Canadian court concerning
  the foreign national or permanent resident, made in accordance with
  the principle set out under subparagraph 718.2(a)(iv) of the [Criminal
  Code](/eng/acts/C-46).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-198, s. 1](#1375716-1376787){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1375716-1376787.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed period

**[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraph 36(3)(c) of the Act, the prescribed period is five years

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} after the completion of an imposed sentence, in the
  case of matters referred to in paragraphs 36(1)(b) and (2)(b) of the
  Act, if the person has not been convicted of a subsequent offence
  other than an offence designated as a contravention under the
  [Contraventions Act](/eng/acts/C-38.7) or an offence under the [Young
  Offenders Act](/eng/acts/Y-1); and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} after committing an offence, in the case of matters
  referred to in paragraphs 36(1)(c) and (2)(c) of the Act, if the
  person has not been convicted of a subsequent offence other than an
  offence designated as a contravention under the [Contraventions
  Act](/eng/acts/C-38.7) or an offence under the [Young Offenders
  Act](/eng/acts/Y-1).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rehabilitation

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of paragraph 36(3)(c) of the Act, the class of persons
  deemed to have been rehabilitated is a prescribed class.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Members of the class

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following persons are members of the class of
  persons deemed to have been rehabilitated:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} persons who have been convicted outside Canada of
    no more than one offence that, if committed in Canada, would
    constitute an indictable offence under an Act of Parliament, if all
    of the following conditions apply, namely,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the offence is punishable in Canada by a maximum
      term of imprisonment of less than 10 years,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} at least 10 years have elapsed since the day
      after the completion of the imposed sentence,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the person has not been convicted in Canada of
      an indictable offence under an Act of Parliament,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the person has not been convicted in Canada of
      any summary conviction offence within the last 10 years under an
      Act of Parliament or of more than one summary conviction offence
      before the last 10 years, other than an offence designated as a
      contravention under the [Contraventions Act](/eng/acts/C-38.7) or
      an offence under the [Youth Criminal Justice
      Act](/eng/acts/Y-1.5),

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the person has not within the last 10 years been
      convicted outside Canada of an offence that, if committed in
      Canada, would constitute an offence under an Act of Parliament,
      other than an offence designated as a contravention under the
      [Contraventions Act](/eng/acts/C-38.7) or an offence under the
      [Youth Criminal Justice Act](/eng/acts/Y-1.5),

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the person has not before the last 10 years been
      convicted outside Canada of more than one offence that, if
      committed in Canada, would constitute a summary conviction offence
      under an Act of Parliament, and

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the person has not committed an act described
      in paragraph 36(2)(c) of the Act, other than the one for which
      they have been convicted;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} persons convicted outside Canada of two or more
    offences that, if committed in Canada, would constitute summary
    conviction offences under any Act of Parliament, if all of the
    following conditions apply, namely,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} at least five years have elapsed since the day
      after the completion of the imposed sentences,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person has not been convicted in Canada of
      an indictable offence under an Act of Parliament,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the person has not within the last five years
      been convicted in Canada of an offence under an Act of Parliament,
      other than an offence designated as a contravention under the
      [Contraventions Act](/eng/acts/C-38.7) or an offence under the
      [Youth Criminal Justice Act](/eng/acts/Y-1.5),

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the person has not within the last five years
      been convicted outside Canada of an offence that, if committed in
      Canada, would constitute an offence under an Act of Parliament,
      other than an offence designated as a contravention under the
      [Contraventions Act](/eng/acts/C-38.7) or an offence under the
      [Youth Criminal Justice Act](/eng/acts/Y-1.5),

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the person has not before the last five years
      been convicted in Canada of more than one summary conviction
      offence under an Act of Parliament, other than an offence
      designated as a contravention under the [Contraventions
      Act](/eng/acts/C-38.7) or an offence under the [Youth Criminal
      Justice Act](/eng/acts/Y-1.5),

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the person has not been convicted of an offence
      referred to in paragraph 36(2)(b) of the Act that, if committed in
      Canada, would constitute an indictable offence, and

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the person has not committed an act described
      in paragraph 36(2)(c) of the Act; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} persons who have committed no more than one act
    outside Canada that is an offence in the place where it was
    committed and that, if committed in Canada, would constitute an
    indictable offence under an Act of Parliament, if all of the
    following conditions apply, namely,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the offence is punishable in Canada by a maximum
      term of imprisonment of less than 10 years,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} at least 10 years have elapsed since the day
      after the commission of the offence,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the person has not been convicted in Canada of
      an indictable offence under an Act of Parliament,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the person has not been convicted in Canada of
      any summary conviction offence within the last 10 years under an
      Act of Parliament or of more than one summary conviction offence
      before the last 10 years, other than an offence designated as a
      contravention under the [Contraventions Act](/eng/acts/C-38.7) or
      an offence under the [Youth Criminal Justice
      Act](/eng/acts/Y-1.5),

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the person has not within the last 10 years been
      convicted outside of Canada of an offence that, if committed in
      Canada, would constitute an offence under an Act of Parliament,
      other than an offence designated as a contravention under the
      [Contraventions Act](/eng/acts/C-38.7) or an offence under the
      [Youth Criminal Justice Act](/eng/acts/Y-1.5),

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the person has not before the last 10 years been
      convicted outside Canada of more than one offence that, if
      committed in Canada, would constitute a summary conviction offence
      under an Act of Parliament, and

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the person has not been convicted outside of
      Canada of an offence that, if committed in Canada, would
      constitute an indictable offence under an Act of Parliament.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed class

- **[[18.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The class of foreign nationals who
  are inadmissible solely on the basis of having been convicted in
  Canada of two or more offences that may only be prosecuted summarily,
  under any Act of Parliament, is a prescribed class for the application
  of paragraph 36(2)(a) of the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A member of the class prescribed in subsection (1) is
  exempt from the application of paragraph 36(2)(a) of the Act if it has
  been at least five years since the day after the completion of the
  imposed sentences.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-167, s. 8
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transborder criminality

- **[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of subsection 36(2.1) of the Act, the prescribed offences
  are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any indictable offence under the [Criminal
    Code](/eng/acts/C-46);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any indictable offence under the [Immigration and
    Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any offence under section 106, 107 or 110 of the
    [Firearms Act](/eng/acts/F-11.6);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any offence under subsection 159(1) of the [Customs
    Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) with respect to the following goods:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} [[child pornography]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as
      defined in subsection 163.1(1) of the [Criminal
      Code](/eng/acts/C-46),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an [[automatic firearm]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef},
      [[imitation firearm]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, [[non-restricted
      firearm]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, [[prohibited
      ammunition]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, [[prohibited
      device]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, [[prohibited
      firearm]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, [[prohibited
      weapon]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, [[replica
      firearm]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, [[restricted
      firearm]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} or [[restricted
      weapon]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as those terms are defined in
      subsection 84(1) of the [Criminal Code](/eng/acts/C-46),

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a substance listed in any of Schedules I to VI
      of the [Controlled Drugs and Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8) or
      a device listed in Schedule IX of that Act, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} [[cannabis]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined
      in subsection 2(1) of the [Cannabis Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any offence under subsection 160(1) of the [Customs
    Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) that is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a contravention of section 12 of that Act with
      respect to the following goods:

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} [[child pornography]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as
        defined in subsection 163.1(1) of the [Criminal
        Code](/eng/acts/C-46),

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} an [[automatic firearm]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef},
        [[imitation firearm]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, [[non-restricted
        firearm]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, [[prohibited
        ammunition]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, [[prohibited
        device]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, [[prohibited
        firearm]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, [[prohibited
        weapon]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, [[replica
        firearm]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, [[restricted
        firearm]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} or [[restricted
        weapon]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as those terms are defined in
        subsection 84(1) of the [Criminal Code](/eng/acts/C-46), or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} a substance listed in any of Schedules I to VI
        of the [Controlled Drugs and Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8),
        or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a contravention of section 156 of the [Customs
      Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} any offence under section 4, 5, 6 or 7.1 of the
    [Controlled Drugs and Substances Act](/eng/acts/C-38.8) or any
    offence under section 46 of that Act that is a contravention of
    subsection 46.3(1) of that Act; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} any offence under Division 1 of Part 1 of the
    [Cannabis Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5), except an offence under section 12
    of that Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishable by way of indictment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraphs (1)(a) and (b), an
  offence that may be prosecuted either summarily or by way of
  indictment is deemed to be an offence punishable by way of indictment,
  even if it has been prosecuted summarily.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inadmissibility on health grounds

- **[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  officer shall determine that a foreign national is inadmissible on
  health grounds if the officer determines that the foreign national's
  health condition is likely to be a danger to public health or public
  safety or, except for a foreign national referred to in subsection
  38(2) of the Act, might reasonably be expected to cause excessive
  demand.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Danger to public health or public
  safety

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before determining that the foreign national's health
  condition is likely to be a danger to public health or public safety,
  the officer shall consider any opinions of an officer who is assessing
  the foreign national's health condition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Excessive demand

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Before determining that the foreign national's health
  condition might reasonably be expected to cause excessive demand, the
  officer shall consider

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any opinions of an officer who is assessing the
    foreign national's health condition; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any relevant non-medical factors, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national's intent and financial
      ability to mitigate any excessive demand, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the feasibility of a mitigation plan, if any,
      submitted by the foreign national.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-39, s. 2](#1340406-1340679){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1340406-1340679.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-20-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 20"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Financial reasons

**[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** Protected persons within
the meaning of subsection 95(2) of the Act are exempted from the
application of section 39 of the Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Misrepresentation

**[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** Persons who have claimed
refugee protection, if disposition of the claim is pending, and
protected persons within the meaning of subsection 95(2) of the Act are
exempted from the application of paragraph 40(1)(a) of the Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of paragraph 41(a) of the Act

**[[22.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22.1 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
determining whether a foreign national is inadmissible under paragraph
41(a) of the Act for having failed to comply with the condition set out
in paragraph 43(1)(e) or 183(1)(d) of these Regulations, if the foreign
national has been convicted for having contravened an order or
regulation made under the [Emergencies Act](/eng/acts/E-4.5) or the
[Quarantine Act](/eng/acts/Q-1.1), the facts on which the conviction is
based shall be considered to be conclusively established.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-91, s. 2](#1243439-1244393){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1243439-1244393.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed circumstances --- family
members

**[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraph 42(1)(a) of the Act, the prescribed circumstances in which the
foreign national is inadmissible on grounds of an inadmissible
non-accompanying family member are that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national is a temporary resident or has
  made an application for temporary resident status, an application for
  a permanent resident visa or an application to remain in Canada as a
  temporary or permanent resident; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the non-accompanying family member is

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the spouse of the foreign national, except where
    the relationship between the spouse and foreign national has broken
    down in law or in fact,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the common-law partner of the foreign national,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a dependent child of the foreign national and
    either the foreign national or an accompanying family member of the
    foreign national has custody of that child or is empowered to act on
    behalf of that child by virtue of a court order or written agreement
    or by operation of law, or

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a dependent child of a dependent child of the
    foreign national and the foreign national, a dependent child of the
    foreign national or any other accompanying family member of the
    foreign national has custody of that child or is empowered to act on
    behalf of that child by virtue of a court order or written agreement
    or by operation of law.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-269, ss. 1, 6
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-23-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 23"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

- **[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  exception set out in paragraph 38(2)(a) of the Act does not apply to a
  child who is not a dependent child of the sponsor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed family members

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following family members of a foreign national
  referred to in paragraph 38(2)(a) of the Act are prescribed for the
  purpose of paragraph 38(2)(d) of the Act:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the dependent child of the sponsor's spouse or
    common-law partner;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the dependent child of the dependent child referred
    to in paragraph (a); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the dependent child of the sponsor's dependent
    child.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph 38(1)(c) of the Act does not apply to a
  foreign national who has been determined to be a member of the family
  class and is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the conjugal partner of a sponsor;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the dependent child of the conjugal partner
    referred to in paragraph (a);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the dependent child of the dependent child referred
    to in paragraph (b); or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a person referred to in paragraph 117(1)(g).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-61, s. 2
- [SOR/2019-212, s. 3](#1138222-1145966){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1138222-1145966.html"}
- [SOR/2023-249, s. 4(E)](#1429145-1429234){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1429145-1429234.html"}
:::
