##### [Experience]{.HTitleText4} {#h-687432 .Subheading}

- **[[103]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-103 .sectionLabel}** [(1) and
  (2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2016-316, s. 9\]]{.Repealed}

- [[Self-employed person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A member of the self-employed persons class shall be
  awarded assessment points up to a maximum of 35 points for relevant
  experience during the period beginning five years before the date of
  their application for a permanent resident visa and ending on the day
  a determination is made in respect of the application as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 20 points for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} two one-year periods of experience described in
      clause (a)(i)(A) or (B) of the definition [[relevant
      experience]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 88(1) or a
      combination of two one-year periods of such experience,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} two one-year periods of experience described in
      clause (a)(ii)(A) or (B) of the definition [[relevant
      experience]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 88(1) or a
      combination of two one-year periods of such experience, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} two one-year periods of experience described in
      subparagraph (a)(iii) of the definition [[relevant
      experience]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 88(1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 25 points for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} three one-year periods of experience described in
      clause (a)(i)(A) or (B) of the definition [[relevant
      experience]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 88(1) or any
      combination of three one-year periods of such experience,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} three one-year periods of experience described
      in clause (a)(ii)(A) or (B) of the definition [[relevant
      experience]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 88(1) or any
      combination of three one-year periods of such experience, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} three one-year periods of experience described
      in subparagraph (a)(iii) of the definition [[relevant
      experience]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 88(1);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} 30 points for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} four one-year periods of experience described in
      clause (a)(i)(A) or (B) of the definition [[relevant
      experience]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 88(1) or any
      combination of four one-year periods of such experience,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} four one-year periods of experience described in
      clause (a)(ii)(A) or (B) of the definition [[relevant
      experience]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 88(1) or any
      combination of four one-year periods of such experience, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} four one-year periods of experience described
      in subparagraph (a)(iii) of the definition [[relevant
      experience]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 88(1); and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} 35 points for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} five one-year periods of experience described in
      clause (a)(i)(A) or (B) of the definition [[relevant
      experience]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 88(1) or any
      combination of five one-year periods of such experience,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} five one-year periods of experience described in
      clause (a)(ii)(A) or (B) of the definition [[relevant
      experience]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 88(1) or any
      combination of five one-year periods of such experience, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} five one-year periods of experience described
      in subparagraph (a)(iii) of the definition [[relevant
      experience]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 88(1).

##### [Adaptability]{.HTitleText4} {#h-687455 .Subheading}

**[[104]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2016-316, s. 10\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-104-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 104"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Self-employed person

- **[[105]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-105 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  member of the self-employed persons class shall be awarded assessment
  points up to a maximum of 6 points for adaptability on the basis of
  any combination of the following elements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the educational credentials of the member's
    accompanying spouse or common-law partner, 3, 4 or 5 points
    determined in accordance with subsection (2);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for any previous period of study in Canada by the
    member or their spouse or common-law partner, 5 points;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for any previous period of work in Canada by the
    member or their spouse or common-law partner, 5 points; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} for being related to, or for having an accompanying
    spouse or accompanying common-law partner who is related to, a
    person living in Canada who is described in subsection (5), 5
    points.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Educational credentials of spouse or
  common-law partner

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (1)(a), an officer
  shall evaluate the educational credentials of the accompanying spouse
  or accompanying common-law partner of the member of the self-employed
  persons class as if the spouse or common-law partner were the member,
  and shall award points to the member as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a spouse or common-law partner who would be
    awarded 25 points, 5 points;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for a spouse or common-law partner who would be
    awarded 20 or 22 points, 4 points; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for a spouse or common-law partner who would be
    awarded 12 or 15 points, 3 points.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Previous study in Canada

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (1)(b), a member of the
  self-employed persons class shall be awarded 5 points if the member or
  their accompanying spouse or accompanying common-law partner, on or
  after their 17th birthday, completed a program of full-time study of
  at least two years' duration at a post-secondary institution in Canada
  under a study permit, whether or not they obtained an educational
  credential for completing that program.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Previous work in Canada

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of paragraph (1)(c), a member of the
  self-employed persons class shall be awarded 5 points if the member or
  their accompanying spouse or accompanying common-law partner engaged
  in at least one year of full-time work in Canada under a work permit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Family relationships in Canada

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (1)(d), a member of the
  self-employed persons class shall be awarded 5 points if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the member or their accompanying spouse or
    accompanying common-law partner is related by blood, marriage,
    common-law partnership or adoption to a person who is a Canadian
    citizen or permanent resident living in Canada and who is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} their father or mother,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the father or mother of their father or mother,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} their child,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a child of their child,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a child of their father or mother,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} a child of the father or mother of their father
      or mother, other than their father or mother, or

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} a child of the child of their father or mother;
      or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the member has a spouse or common-law partner who
    is not accompanying them and is a Canadian citizen or permanent
    resident living in Canada.

#### [Selection]{.HTitleText3} {#h-687489 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for visa

- **[[108]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-108
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5), if a
  foreign national makes an application as a member of the Quebec
  investor class, the Quebec entrepreneur class, the start-up business
  class, the self-employed persons class or the Quebec self-employed
  persons class for a permanent resident visa, an officer may only issue
  the visa to the foreign national and their accompanying family members
  if they meet the requirements of subsection 70(1) and, if applicable,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a foreign national who has made an
    application under the self-employed persons class and their
    accompanying family members, who intend to reside in a place in
    Canada other than a province whose government has, under subsection
    8(1) of the Act, entered into an agreement referred to in subsection
    9(1) of the Act with the Minister under which the province has sole
    responsibility for selection, the foreign national is awarded the
    minimum number of points referred to in subsection (4); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a foreign national and their
    accompanying family members, who intend to reside in a province
    whose government has, under subsection 8(1) of the Act, entered into
    an agreement referred to in subsection 9(1) of the Act with the
    Minister under which the province has sole responsibility for
    selection, the foreign national is named in a selection certificate
    issued by that province.

- [(2) and (3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2016-316, s.
  12\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minimum points --- self-employed
  persons

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall fix and make available to the
  public the minimum number of points required of a self-employed
  person, on the basis of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number of applications by members of the
    self-employed persons class currently being processed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of self-employed persons projected to
    become permanent residents according to the report to Parliament
    referred to in section 94 of the Act; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the potential, taking into account economic and
    other relevant factors, for the establishment of self-employed
    persons in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Federal-provincial agreement

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A permanent resident visa shall not be issued to a
  foreign national as a member of the Quebec investor class, or to their
  accompanying family members, if the Minister is engaged in
  consultations with the province in respect of the interpretation or
  implementation of the agreement, referred to in subsection 9(1) of the
  Act and entered into under subsection 8(1) of the Act, between the
  province and the Minister in respect of the selection of investors and
  the consultations have not been successfully completed.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Substitution of evaluation

- **[[109]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-109
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Whether or not a foreign national
  has been awarded the minimum number of required points referred to in
  subsection 108(1), an officer may substitute for the factors set out
  in subsection 102(1) their evaluation of the likelihood of the foreign
  national's ability to become economically established in Canada if the
  number of points awarded is not a sufficient indicator of whether the
  foreign national may become economically established in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Concurrence

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An evaluation made under subsection (1) requires the
  concurrence of a second officer.

## [PART 7]{.HLabel1}[Family Classes]{.HTitleText1} {#h-687524 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Family Class]{.HTitleText2} {#h-687525 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Family class

**[[116]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-116 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subsection 12(1) of the Act, the family class is hereby prescribed as a
class of persons who may become permanent residents on the basis of the
requirements of this Division.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Member

- **[[117]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-117 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  foreign national is a member of the family class if, with respect to a
  sponsor, the foreign national is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the sponsor's spouse, common-law partner or
    conjugal partner;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a dependent child of the sponsor;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the sponsor's mother or father;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the mother or father of the sponsor's mother or
    father;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2005-61, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a person whose parents are deceased, who is under
    18 years of age, who is not a spouse or common-law partner and who
    is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a child of the sponsor's mother or father,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a child of a child of the sponsor's mother or
      father, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a child of the sponsor's child;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a person under 18 years of age whom the sponsor
    intends to adopt in Canada if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the adoption is not being entered into primarily
      for the purpose of acquiring any status or privilege under the
      Act,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} where the adoption is an international adoption,
      the country in which the person resides is a party to the Hague
      Convention on Adoption and the Convention applies to their
      province of intended destination, the competent authority of the
      country and of the province have approved the adoption in writing
      as conforming to that Convention, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} where the adoption is an international adoption
      and the country in which the person resides is not a party to the
      Hague Convention on Adoption or the Convention does not apply to
      the person's province of intended destination

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the person has been placed for adoption in the
        country in which they reside or is otherwise legally available
        in that country for adoption and there is no evidence that the
        intended adoption is for the purpose of child trafficking or
        undue gain within the meaning of the Hague Convention on
        Adoption, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the competent authority of the person's
        province of intended destination has stated in writing that it
        does not object to the adoption; or

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a relative of the sponsor, regardless of age, if
    the sponsor does not have a spouse, a common-law partner, a conjugal
    partner, a child, a mother or father, a relative who is a child of
    that mother or father, a relative who is a child of a child of that
    mother or father, a mother or father of that mother or father or a
    relative who is a child of the mother or father of that mother or
    father

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} who is a Canadian citizen, Indian or permanent
      resident, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} whose application to enter and remain in Canada
      as a permanent resident the sponsor may otherwise sponsor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adoption --- under 18

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A foreign national who is the adopted child of a
  sponsor and whose adoption took place when the child was under the age
  of 18 shall not be considered a member of the family class by virtue
  of the adoption unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the adoption was in the best interests of the child
    within the meaning of the Hague Convention on Adoption; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the adoption was not entered into primarily for the
    purpose of acquiring any status or privilege under the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Best interests of the child

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The adoption referred to in subsection (2) is
  considered to be in the best interests of a child if it took place
  under the following circumstances:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a competent authority has conducted or approved a
    home study of the adoptive parents;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} before the adoption, the child's parents gave their
    free and informed consent to the child's adoption;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the adoption created a genuine parent-child
    relationship;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the adoption was in accordance with the laws of the
    place where the adoption took place;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the adoption was in accordance with the laws of the
    sponsor's place of residence and, if the sponsor resided in Canada
    at the time the adoption took place, the competent authority of the
    child's province of intended destination has stated in writing that
    it does not object to the adoption;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the adoption is an international adoption, the
    country in which the adoption took place is a party to the Hague
    Convention on Adoption and the Convention applies to the child's
    province of intended destination, the competent authority of the
    country and of the province have stated in writing that they approve
    the adoption as conforming to that Convention; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if the adoption is an international adoption and
    the country in which the adoption took place is not a party to the
    Hague Convention on Adoption or the Convention does not apply to the
    child's province of intended destination, there is no evidence that
    the adoption is for the purpose of child trafficking or undue gain
    within the meaning of that Convention.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adoption --- over 18

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A foreign national who is the adopted child of a
  sponsor and whose adoption took place when the child was 18 years of
  age or older shall not be considered a member of the family class by
  virtue of that adoption unless it took place under the following
  circumstances:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the adoption was in accordance with the laws of the
    place where the adoption took place and, if the sponsor resided in
    Canada at the time of the adoption, the adoption was in accordance
    with the laws of the province where the sponsor then resided, if
    any, that applied in respect of the adoption of a child 18 years of
    age or older;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a genuine parent-child relationship existed at the
    time of the adoption and existed before the child reached the age of
    18; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the adoption was not entered into primarily for the
    purpose of acquiring any status or privilege under the Act.

- [(5) and (6)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2005-61, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provincial statement

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If a statement referred to in clause (1)(g)(iii)(B)
  or paragraph (3)(e) or (f) has been provided to an officer by the
  competent authority of the foreign national's province of intended
  destination, that statement is, except in the case of an adoption that
  was entered into primarily for the purpose of acquiring any status or
  privilege under the Act, conclusive evidence that the foreign national
  meets the following applicable requirements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2005-61, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a person referred to in paragraph
    (1)(g), the requirements set out in clause (1)(g)(iii)(A); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a person referred to in paragraph
    (1)(b) who is an adopted child described in subsection (2), the
    requirements set out in paragraphs (3)(a) to (e) and (g).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}New evidence

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If, after the statement is provided to the officer,
  the officer receives evidence that the foreign national does not meet
  the applicable requirements set out in paragraph (7)(b) or (c) for
  becoming a member of the family class, the processing of their
  application shall be suspended until the officer provides that
  evidence to the competent authority of the province and that authority
  confirms or revises its statement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Excluded relationships

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} A foreign national shall not be considered a member
  of the family class by virtue of their relationship to a sponsor if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national is the sponsor's conjugal
    partner and is under 18 years of age;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national is the sponsor's spouse,
    common-law partner or conjugal partner, the sponsor has an existing
    sponsorship undertaking in respect of a spouse, common-law partner
    or conjugal partner and the period referred to in subsection 132(1)
    in respect of that undertaking has not ended;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national is the sponsor's spouse and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the sponsor or the foreign national was, at the
      time of their marriage, the spouse of another person, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the sponsor has lived separate and apart from
      the foreign national for at least one year and

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the sponsor is the common-law partner of
        another person or the sponsor has a conjugal partner, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national is the common-law partner
        of another person or the conjugal partner of another sponsor; or

  - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national is the sponsor's spouse and
    if at the time the marriage ceremony was conducted either one or
    both of the spouses were not physically present unless the foreign
    national was marrying a person who was not physically present at the
    ceremony as a result of their service as a member of the Canadian
    Forces and the marriage is valid both under the laws of the
    jurisdiction where it took place and under Canadian law;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (10), the sponsor previously
    made an application for permanent residence and became a permanent
    resident and, at the time of that application, the foreign national
    was a non-accompanying family member of the sponsor and was not
    examined.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (11), paragraph (9)(d) does
  not apply in respect of a foreign national referred to in that
  paragraph who was not examined because an officer determined that they
  were not required by the Act or the former Act, as applicable, to be
  examined.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of par. (9)(d)

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (9)(d) applies in respect of a foreign
  national referred to in subsection (10) if an officer determines that,
  at the time of the application referred to in that paragraph,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the sponsor was informed that the foreign national
    could be examined and the sponsor was able to make the foreign
    national available for examination but did not do so or the foreign
    national did not appear for examination; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national was the sponsor's spouse, was
    living separate and apart from the sponsor and was not examined.

- Definition of [[former Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} In subsection (10), [[former
  Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in section 187 of
  the Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Medical condition

**[[118]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-118 .sectionLabel}** A foreign national who
is an adopted dependent child or is a person referred to in paragraph
117(1)(f) or (g) shall not be issued a permanent resident visa as a
member of the family class unless the sponsor has provided a statement
in writing confirming that they have obtained information about the
medical condition of the child or of the foreign national.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-61, s. 4
- SOR/2010-195, s. 12
:::

Version](section-118-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 118"){.PITLink}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Withdrawal of sponsorship application

**[[119]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-119 .sectionLabel}** A decision shall not be
made on an application for a permanent resident visa by a member of the
family class if the sponsor withdraws their sponsorship application in
respect of that member.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approved sponsorship application

**[[120]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-120 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
Part 5,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a permanent resident visa shall not be issued to a
  foreign national who makes an application as a member of the family
  class or to their accompanying family members unless a sponsorship
  undertaking in respect of the foreign national and those family
  members is in effect; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a foreign national who makes an application as a
  member of the family class and their accompanying family members shall
  not become permanent residents unless a sponsorship undertaking in
  respect of the foreign national and those family members is in effect
  and the sponsor who gave that undertaking still meets the requirements
  of section 133 and, if applicable, section 137.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements

**[[121]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-121 .sectionLabel}** Subject to subsection
25.1(1), a person who is a member of the family class or a family member
of a member of the family class who makes an application under Division
6 of Part 5 must be a family member of the applicant or of the sponsor
both at the time the application is made and at the time of the
determination of the application.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-167, s. 42
- SOR/2014-133, s. 6

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements for accompanying family
members

**[[122]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-122 .sectionLabel}** A foreign national who
is an accompanying family member of a person who makes an application as
a member of the family class shall become a permanent resident if,
following an examination, it is established that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person who made the application has become a
  permanent resident; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the family member is not inadmissible.

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Spouse or Common-Law Partner in Canada Class]{.HTitleText2} {#h-687616 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Class

**[[123]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-123 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subsection 12(1) of the Act, the spouse or common-law partner in Canada
class is hereby prescribed as a class of persons who may become
permanent residents on the basis of the requirements of this Division.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Member

**[[124]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-124 .sectionLabel}** A foreign national is a
member of the spouse or common-law partner in Canada class if they

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} are the spouse or common-law partner of a sponsor and
  cohabit with that sponsor in Canada;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} have temporary resident status in Canada; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} are the subject of a sponsorship application.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Excluded relationships

- **[[125]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-125 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  foreign national shall not be considered a member of the spouse or
  common-law partner in Canada class by virtue of their relationship to
  the sponsor if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2023-249, s. 7\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national is the sponsor's spouse or
    common-law partner, the sponsor has an existing sponsorship
    undertaking in respect of a spouse or common-law partner and the
    period referred to in subsection 132(1) in respect of that
    undertaking has not ended;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national is the sponsor's spouse and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the sponsor or the spouse was, at the time of
      their marriage, the spouse of another person, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the sponsor has lived separate and apart from
      the foreign national for at least one year and

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the sponsor is the common-law partner of
        another person or the sponsor has a conjugal partner, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national is the common-law partner
        of another person or the conjugal partner of another sponsor;

  - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national is the sponsor's spouse and
    if at the time the marriage ceremony was conducted either one or
    both of the spouses were not physically present unless the foreign
    national was married to a person who was not physically present at
    the ceremony as a result of their service as a member of the
    Canadian Forces and the marriage is valid both under the laws of the
    jurisdiction where it took place and under Canadian law; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (2), the sponsor previously
    made an application for permanent residence and became a permanent
    resident and, at the time of that application, the foreign national
    was a non-accompanying family member of the sponsor and was not
    examined.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), paragraph (1)(d) does not
  apply in respect of a foreign national referred to in that paragraph
  who was not examined because an officer determined that they were not
  required by the Act or the former Act, as applicable, to be examined.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of par. (1)(d)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(d) applies in respect of a foreign
  national referred to in subsection (2) if an officer determines that,
  at the time of the application referred to in that paragraph,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the sponsor was informed that the foreign national
    could be examined and the sponsor was able to make the foreign
    national available for examination but did not do so or the foreign
    national did not appear for examination; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national was the sponsor's spouse, was
    living separate and apart from the sponsor and was not examined.

- Definition of [[former Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In subsection (2), [[former Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  has the same meaning as in section 187 of the Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Withdrawal of sponsorship application

**[[126]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-126 .sectionLabel}** A decision shall not be
made on an application for permanent residence by a foreign national as
a member of the spouse or common-law partner in Canada class if the
sponsor withdraws their sponsorship application in respect of that
foreign national.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approved sponsorship application

**[[127]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-127 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
Part 5, a foreign national who makes an application as a member of the
spouse or common-law partner in Canada class and their accompanying
family members shall not become a permanent resident unless a
sponsorship undertaking in respect of the foreign national and those
family members is in effect and the sponsor who gave that undertaking
still meets the requirements of section 133 and, if applicable, section
137.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements --- family member

**[[128]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-128 .sectionLabel}** The requirements with
respect to a person who is a family member of a member of the spouse or
common-law partner in Canada class who makes an application under
Division 6 of Part 5 are the following:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection 25.1(1), the person is a family
  member of the applicant both at the time the application is made and
  at the time of the determination of the application; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} at the time it is made, the application includes a
  request for the family member to remain in Canada as a permanent
  resident.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-133, s. 7
:::

:::

members

is an accompanying family member of a person who makes an application as
a member of the spouse or common-law partner in Canada class shall
become a permanent resident if, following an examination, it is

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Sponsors]{.HTitleText2} {#h-687662 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sponsor

- **[[130]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-130
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (3),
  a sponsor, for the purpose of sponsoring a foreign national who makes
  an application for a permanent resident visa as a member of the family
  class or an application to remain in Canada as a member of the spouse
  or common-law partner in Canada class under subsection 13(1) of the
  Act, must be a Canadian citizen or permanent resident who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is at least 18 years of age;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} resides in Canada; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has filed a sponsorship application in respect of a
    member of the family class or the spouse or common-law partner in
    Canada class in accordance with section 10.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sponsor not residing in Canada

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A sponsor who is a Canadian citizen and does not
  reside in Canada may sponsor a foreign national who makes an
  application referred to in subsection (1) and is the sponsor's spouse,
  common-law partner, conjugal partner or dependent child who has no
  dependent children, if the sponsor will reside in Canada when the
  foreign national becomes a permanent resident.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Five-year requirement

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A sponsor who became a permanent resident or a
  Canadian citizen after being sponsored as a spouse, common-law partner
  or conjugal partner under subsection 13(1) of the Act may not sponsor
  a foreign national referred to in subsection (1) as a spouse,
  common-law partner or conjugal partner, unless the sponsor has been a
  permanent resident, or a Canadian citizen, or a combination of the
  two, for a period of at least five years immediately preceding the day
  on which a sponsorship application referred to in paragraph (1)(c) is
  filed by the sponsor in respect of the foreign national.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-20, s. 1
- SOR/2015-139, s. 4
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-130-20120302.html "Link to previous version of section 130"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sponsorship undertaking

**[[131]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-131 .sectionLabel}** The sponsor's
undertaking shall be given

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} to the Minister; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the sponsor resides in a province that has entered
  into an agreement referred to in subsection 8(1) of the Act that
  enables the province to determine and apply financial criteria with
  respect to sponsorship undertakings and to administer sponsorship
  undertakings, to the competent authority of the province.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-140, s. 8(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-131-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 131"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Undertaking --- duration

- **[[132]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the
  sponsor's undertaking obliges the sponsor to reimburse Her Majesty in
  right of Canada or a province for every benefit provided as social
  assistance to or on behalf of the sponsored foreign national and their
  family members during the period

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} beginning

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the foreign national enters Canada with a
      temporary resident permit, on the day of that entry,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the foreign national is in Canada, on the day
      on which the foreign national obtains a temporary resident permit
      following an application to remain in Canada as a permanent
      resident, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, on the day on which the
      foreign national becomes a permanent resident; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ending

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the foreign national is the sponsor's spouse,
      common-law partner or conjugal partner, on the last day of the
      period of three years following the day on which the foreign
      national becomes a permanent resident,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the foreign national is a dependent child of
      the sponsor or of the sponsor's spouse, common-law partner or
      conjugal partner, or is a person referred to in paragraph
      117(1)(g), and is less than 22 years of age on the day on which he
      or she becomes a permanent resident, on the earlier of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the last day of the period of 10 years
        following the day on which the foreign national becomes a
        permanent resident, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the foreign national attains
        25 years of age,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if the foreign national is a dependent child of
      the sponsor or of the sponsor's spouse, common-law partner or
      conjugal partner and is 22 years of age or older on the day on
      which he or she becomes a permanent resident, on the last day of
      the period of three years following the day on which the foreign
      national becomes a permanent resident,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} on the last day of the period of 20 years
      following the day on which the foreign national becomes a
      permanent resident, if the foreign national is

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the sponsor's mother or father,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the mother or father of the sponsor's mother or
        father, or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} an accompanying family member of the foreign
        national described in clause (A) or (B), and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} if the foreign national is a person other than a
      person referred to in subparagraph (i), (ii), (iii) or (iv), on
      the last day of the period of 10 years following the day on which
      the foreign national becomes a permanent resident.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Undertaking to province --- duration

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of an undertaking to a competent
  authority of a province referred to in paragraph 131(b), the period
  referred to in subsection (1) shall end not later than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the foreign national is a sponsor's spouse,
    common-law partner or conjugal partner, on the last day of the
    period of three years following the day on which the foreign
    national becomes a permanent resident;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the foreign national is a dependent child of the
    sponsor or of the sponsor's spouse, common-law partner or conjugal
    partner, or is a person referred to in paragraph 117(1)(g), and is
    less than 22 years of age on the day on which he or she becomes a
    permanent resident, the later of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the foreign national attains 22
      years of age, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the last day of the period of 10 years following
      the day on which the foreign national becomes a permanent
      resident;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the foreign national is a dependent child of the
    sponsor or of the sponsor's spouse, common-law partner or conjugal
    partner and is 22 years of age or older on the day on which he or
    she becomes a permanent resident, on the last day of the period of
    10 years following the day on which the foreign national becomes a
    permanent resident;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} on the last day of the period of 20 years following
    the day on which the foreign national becomes a permanent resident,
    if the foreign national is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the sponsor's mother or father,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the mother or father of the sponsor's mother or
      father, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an accompanying family member of the foreign
      national described in subparagraph (i) or (ii); and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the foreign national is a person other than a
    person referred to in paragraphs (a) to (d), on the last day of the
    period of 10 years following the day on which the foreign national
    becomes a permanent resident.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Undertaking to province --- alternate
  duration

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), the period referred to in
  subsection (1) shall end not later than the day provided for by the
  laws of the province if that day is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a foreign national referred to in
    paragraph (2)(a), earlier than the last day referred to in that
    paragraph;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a foreign national referred to in
    paragraph (2)(b), earlier than the later of the days referred to in
    subparagraph (2)(b)(i) and (ii);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a foreign national referred to in
    paragraph (2)(c), earlier than the last day referred to in that
    paragraph;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a foreign national referred to in
    paragraph (2)(d), earlier than the last day referred to in that
    paragraph; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a foreign national referred to in
    paragraph (2)(e), earlier than the last day referred to in that
    paragraph.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agreement

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to paragraph 137(c), if the person is to be
  sponsored as a member of the family class or of the spouse or
  common-law partner in Canada class and is 22 years of age or older, or
  is less than 22 years of age and is the sponsor's spouse, common-law
  partner or conjugal partner, the sponsor, the co-signer, if any, and
  the person must, before the sponsorship application is approved, enter
  into a written agreement that includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a statement by the sponsor and the co-signer, if
    any, that they will provide for the basic requirements of the person
    and their accompanying family members during the applicable period
    referred to in subsection (1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a declaration by the sponsor and the co-signer, if
    any, that their financial obligations do not prevent them from
    honouring their agreement with the person and their undertaking to
    the Minister in respect of the person's application; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a statement by the person that they will make every
    reasonable effort to provide for their own basic requirements as
    well as those of their accompanying family members.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Co-signature --- undertaking

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to paragraph 137(c), the sponsor's
  undertaking may be co-signed by the spouse or common-law partner of
  the sponsor if the spouse or common-law partner meets the requirements
  set out in subsection 130(1), except paragraph 130(1)(c), and those
  set out in subsection 133(1), except paragraph 133(1)(a), and, in that
  case,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the sponsor's income shall be calculated in
    accordance with paragraph 134(1)(b) or (c) or (1.1)(b), as
    applicable; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the co-signing spouse or common-law partner is
    jointly and severally or solidarily bound with the sponsor to
    perform the obligations in the undertaking and is jointly and
    severally or solidarily liable with the sponsor for any breach of
    those obligations.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-167, s. 44
- SOR/2005-61, s. 5
- SOR/2013-246, s. 1
- SOR/2014-133, s. 8
- SOR/2014-140, s. 9
- SOR/2017-60, s. 3
:::

:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements for sponsor

- **[[133]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-133 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  sponsorship application shall only be approved by an officer if, on
  the day on which the application was filed and from that day until the
  day a decision is made with respect to the application, there is
  evidence that the sponsor

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is a sponsor as described in section 130;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} intends to fulfil the obligations in the
    sponsorship undertaking;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is not subject to a removal order;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} is not detained in any penitentiary, jail,
    reformatory or prison;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} has not been convicted under the [Criminal
    Code](/eng/acts/C-46) of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an offence of a sexual nature, or an attempt or a
      threat to commit such an offence, against any person,

    - [(i.1)]{.lawlabel} an indictable offence involving the use of
      violence and punishable by a maximum term of imprisonment of at
      least 10 years, or an attempt to commit such an offence, against
      any person, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an offence that results in bodily harm, as
      defined in section 2 of the [Criminal Code](/eng/acts/C-46), to
      any of the following persons or an attempt or a threat to commit
      such an offence against any of the following persons:

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} a current or former family member of the
        sponsor,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} a relative of the sponsor, as well as a current
        or former family member of that relative,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} a relative of the family member of the sponsor,
        or a current or former family member of that relative,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} a current or former conjugal partner of the
        sponsor,

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} a current or former family member of a family
        member or conjugal partner of the sponsor,

      - [(F)]{.lawlabel} a relative of the conjugal partner of the
        sponsor, or a current or former family member of that relative,

      - [(G)]{.lawlabel} a child under the current or former care and
        control of the sponsor, their current or former family member or
        conjugal partner,

      - [(H)]{.lawlabel} a child under the current or former care and
        control of a relative of the sponsor or a current or former
        family member of that relative, or

      - [(I)]{.lawlabel} someone the sponsor is dating or has dated,
        whether or not they have lived together, or a family member of
        that person;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} has not been convicted outside Canada of an offence
    that, if committed in Canada, would constitute an offence referred
    to in paragraph (e);

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} subject to paragraph 137(c), is not in default of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any sponsorship undertaking, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any support payment obligations ordered by a
      court;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} is not in default in respect of the repayment of
    any debt referred to in subsection 145(1) of the Act payable to Her
    Majesty in right of Canada;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subject to paragraph 137(c), is not an undischarged
    bankrupt under the [Bankruptcy and Insolvency Act](/eng/acts/B-3);

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} if the sponsor resides

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in a province other than a province referred to
      in paragraph 131(b),

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} has a total income that is at least equal to
        the minimum necessary income, if the sponsorship application was
        filed in respect of a foreign national other than a foreign
        national referred to in clause (B), or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} has a total income that is at least equal to
        the minimum necessary income, plus 30%, for each of the three
        consecutive taxation years immediately preceding the date of
        filing of the sponsorship application, if the sponsorship
        application was filed in respect of a foreign national who is

        - [(I)]{.lawlabel} the sponsor's mother or father,

        - [(II)]{.lawlabel} the mother or father of the sponsor's mother
          or father, or

        - [(III)]{.lawlabel} an accompanying family member of the
          foreign national described in subclause (I) or (II), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in a province referred to in paragraph 131(b),
      is able, within the meaning of the laws of that province and as
      determined by the competent authority of that province, to fulfil
      the undertaking referred to in that paragraph; and

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} is not in receipt of social assistance for a reason
    other than disability.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- conviction in Canada

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(e), a sponsorship application
  may not be refused

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on the basis of a conviction in Canada in respect
    of which a pardon has been granted and has not ceased to have effect
    or been revoked under the [Criminal Records Act](/eng/acts/C-47), or
    in respect of which there has been a final determination of an
    acquittal; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a period of five years or more has elapsed since
    the completion of the sentence imposed for an offence in Canada
    referred to in paragraph (1)(e).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- conviction outside Canada

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(f), a sponsorship application
  may not be refused

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on the basis of a conviction outside Canada in
    respect of which there has been a final determination of an
    acquittal; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a period of five years or more has elapsed since
    the completion of the sentence imposed for an offence outside Canada
    referred to in that paragraph and the sponsor has demonstrated that
    they have been rehabilitated.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception to minimum necessary income

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(j) does not apply if the sponsored
  person is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the sponsor's spouse, common-law partner or
    conjugal partner and has no dependent children;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the sponsor's spouse, common-law partner or
    conjugal partner and has a dependent child who has no dependent
    children; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a dependent child of the sponsor who has no
    dependent children or a person referred to in paragraph 117(1)(g).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adopted sponsor

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A person who is adopted outside Canada and whose
  adoption is subsequently revoked by a foreign authority or by a court
  in Canada of competent jurisdiction may sponsor an application for a
  permanent resident visa that is made by a member of the family class
  only if the revocation of the adoption was not obtained for the
  purpose of sponsoring that application.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Income calculation rules

- **[[134]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-134
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), for the
  purpose of clause 133(1)(j)(i)(A), the sponsor's total income shall be
  calculated in accordance with the following rules:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the sponsor's income shall be calculated on the
    basis of the last notice of assessment, or an equivalent document,
    issued by the Minister of National Revenue in respect of the most
    recent taxation year preceding the date of filing of the sponsorship
    application;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the sponsor produces a document referred to in
    paragraph (a), the sponsor's income is the income earned as reported
    in that document less the amounts referred to in subparagraphs
    (c)(i) to (v);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the sponsor does not produce a document referred
    to in paragraph (a), or if the sponsor's income as calculated under
    paragraph (b) is less than their minimum necessary income, the
    sponsor's income is the sponsor's Canadian income earned during the
    12-month period preceding the date of filing of the sponsorship
    application not including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any provincial allowance received by the sponsor
      for a program of instruction or training,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any social assistance received by the sponsor
      from a province,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any financial assistance received by the
      sponsor from the Government of Canada under a resettlement
      assistance program,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} any amounts paid to the sponsor under the
      [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), other than special
      benefits,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} any monthly guaranteed income supplement paid to
      the sponsor under the [Old Age Security Act](/eng/acts/O-9), and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} any Canada child benefit paid to the sponsor
      under the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3); and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if there is a co-signer, the income of the
    co-signer, as calculated in accordance with paragraphs (a) to (c),
    with any modifications that the circumstances require, shall be
    included in the calculation of the sponsor's income.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), for the purpose of
  clause 133(1)(j)(i)(B), the sponsor's total income shall be calculated
  in accordance with the following rules:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the sponsor's income shall be calculated on the
    basis of the income earned as reported in the notices of assessment,
    or an equivalent document, issued by the Minister of National
    Revenue in respect of each of the three consecutive taxation years
    immediately preceding the date of filing of the sponsorship
    application;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the sponsor's income is the income earned as
    reported in the documents referred to in paragraph (a), not
    including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any provincial allowance received by the sponsor
      for a program of instruction or training,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any social assistance received by the sponsor
      from a province,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any financial assistance received by the
      sponsor from the Government of Canada under a resettlement
      assistance program,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} any amounts paid to the sponsor under the
      [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), other than special
      benefits,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} any monthly guaranteed income supplement paid to
      the sponsor under the [Old Age Security Act](/eng/acts/O-9), and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} any Canada child benefit paid to the sponsor
      under the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if there is a co-signer, the income of the
    co-signer, as calculated in accordance with paragraphs (a) and (b),
    with any modifications that the circumstances require, shall be
    included in the calculation of the sponsor's income.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Updated evidence of income

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An officer may request from the sponsor, after the
  receipt of the sponsorship application but before a decision is made
  on an application for permanent residence, updated evidence of income
  if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the officer receives information indicating that
    the sponsor is no longer able to fulfil the obligations of the
    sponsorship undertaking; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} more than 12 months have elapsed since the receipt
    of the sponsorship application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Modified income calculation rules

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} When an officer receives the updated evidence of
  income requested under subsection (2), the sponsor's total income
  shall be calculated in accordance with subsection (1) or (1.1), as
  applicable, except that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of paragraph (1)(a), the sponsor's
    income shall be calculated on the basis of the last notice of
    assessment, or an equivalent document, issued by the Minister of
    National Revenue in respect of the most recent taxation year
    preceding the day on which the officer receives the updated
    evidence;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of paragraph (1)(c), the sponsor's
    income is the sponsor's Canadian income earned during the 12-month
    period preceding the day on which the officer receives the updated
    evidence; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of paragraph (1.1)(a), the sponsor's
    income shall be calculated on the basis of the income earned as
    reported in the notices of assessment, or an equivalent document,
    issued by the Minister of National Revenue in respect of each of the
    three consecutive taxation years immediately preceding the day on
    which the officer receives the updated evidence.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Default

**[[135]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-135 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
subparagraph 133(1)(g)(i), the default of a sponsorship undertaking

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} begins when

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a government makes a payment that the sponsor has
    in the undertaking promised to repay, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an obligation set out in the undertaking is
    breached; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} ends, as the case may be, when

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the sponsor reimburses the government concerned, in
    full or in accordance with an agreement with that government, for
    amounts paid by it, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the sponsor ceases to be in breach of the
    obligation set out in the undertaking.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-140, s. 11(F)
:::

Version](section-135-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 135"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension during proceedings against
sponsor or co-signer

- **[[136]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-136 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  any of the following proceedings are brought against a sponsor or
  co-signer, the sponsorship application shall not be processed until
  there has been a final determination of the proceeding:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the revocation of citizenship under the
    [Citizenship Act](/eng/acts/C-29);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a report prepared under subsection 44(1) of the
    Act; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a charge alleging the commission of an offence
    under an Act of Parliament punishable by a maximum term of
    imprisonment of at least 10 years.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension following proceeding

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In respect of a proceeding to which subsection 63(4)
  of the Act applies, the sponsorship application shall not be processed
  until the later of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the period for making an appeal under that
    subsection has expired, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if an appeal is made, the final determination of
    the appeal.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Undertaking --- Province of Quebec

**[[137]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-137 .sectionLabel}** If the sponsor resides
in the Province of Quebec, the government of which has entered into an
agreement referred to in paragraph 131(b),

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the sponsor's undertaking, given in accordance with
  section 131, is the undertaking required by An Act respecting
  immigration to Québec, R.S.Q., c.I-0.2, as amended from time to time;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} an officer shall approve the sponsorship application
  only if there is evidence that the competent authority of the Province
  has determined that the sponsor, on the day the undertaking was given
  as well as on the day a decision was made with respect to the
  application, was able to fulfil the undertaking; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} subsections 132(4) and (5) and paragraphs 133(1)(g)
  and (i) do not apply.

### [DIVISION 3.1]{.HLabel2}[Collection and Disclosure of Information]{.HTitleText2} {#h-687841 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Collection of social insurance number

- **[[137.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-137.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may collect the social
  insurance numbers of a sponsor and a co-signer who have submitted an
  application to sponsor a person set out in clause 133(1)(j)(i)(B), in
  order to verify that they meet the requirements set out in clause
  133(1)(j)(i)(B) and in paragraph 133(1)(k).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure of social insurance number

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may disclose the social insurance
  numbers of the sponsor and the co-signer to the Canada Revenue Agency
  for the purposes set out in subsection (1) if the Minister has entered
  into an arrangement with the Agency for the disclosure of that
  information.

## [PART 8]{.HLabel1}[Refugee Classes]{.HTitleText1} {#h-687849 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Convention Refugees Abroad, Humanitarian-protected Persons Abroad and Protected Temporary Residents]{.HTitleText2} {#h-687850 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2011-222, s. 2

\]
:::

#### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-687853 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[138]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138 .sectionLabel}** The definitions in this
section apply in this Division and in Division 2.

[[group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} five or more Canadian citizens or permanent
      residents, each of whom is at least 18 years of age, who are
      acting together for the purpose of sponsoring a Convention refugee
      or a person in similar circumstances; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} one or more Canadian citizens or permanent
      residents, each of whom is at least 18 years of age, and a
      corporation or unincorporated organization or association referred
      to in subsection 13(2) of the Act, acting together for the purpose
      of sponsoring a Convention refugee or a person in similar
      circumstances. ([groupe]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[referral organization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[referral organization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the United Nations High Commissioner for
      Refugees; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any organization with which the Minister has
      entered into a memorandum of understanding under section
      143. ([organisation de recommandation]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[sponsor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[sponsor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a group, a corporation or an unincorporated
      organization or association referred to in subsection 13(2) of the
      Act, or any combination of them, that is acting for the purpose of
      sponsoring a Convention refugee or a person in similar
      circumstances; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of section 158, a
      [[sponsor]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in section 1 of the
      Québec Immigration Regulation, CQLR, c. I-0.2.1, r. 3, as amended
      from time to time. ([répondant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[undertaking]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[undertaking]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an undertaking in writing
    to the Minister to provide resettlement assistance, lodging and
    other basic necessities in Canada for a member of a class prescribed
    by this Division, the member's accompanying family members and any
    of the member's non-accompanying family members who meet the
    requirements of section 141, for the period determined in accordance
    with subsections 154(2) and (3). ([engagement]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[urgent need of protection]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[urgent need of protection]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect
    of a member of the Convention refugee abroad or the country of
    asylum class, that their life, liberty or physical safety is under
    immediate threat and, if not protected, the person is likely to be

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} killed;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subjected to violence, torture, sexual assault or
      arbitrary imprisonment; or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} returned to their country of nationality or of
      their former habitual residence. ([besoin urgent de
      protection]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[vulnerable]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[vulnerable]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of a Convention
    refugee or a person in similar circumstances, that the person has a
    greater need of protection than other applicants for protection
    abroad because of the person's particular circumstances that give
    rise to a heightened risk to their physical
    safety. ([vulnérable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2009-163, s. 2(F)
- SOR/2011-222, s. 3

:::

#### [General]{.HTitleText3} {#h-687873 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}General requirements

- **[[139]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-139 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  permanent resident visa shall be issued to a foreign national in need
  of refugee protection, and their accompanying family members, if
  following an examination it is established that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national is outside Canada;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national has submitted an application
    for a permanent resident visa under this Division in accordance with
    paragraphs 10(1)(a) to (c) and (2)(c.1) to (d) and sections 140.1 to
    140.3;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national is seeking to come to Canada
    to establish permanent residence;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national is a person in respect of whom
    there is no reasonable prospect, within a reasonable period, of a
    durable solution in a country other than Canada, namely

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} voluntary repatriation or resettlement in their
      country of nationality or habitual residence, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} resettlement or an offer of resettlement in
      another country;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national is a member of one of the
    classes prescribed by this Division;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} one of the following is the case, namely

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the sponsor's sponsorship application for the
      foreign national and their family members included in the
      application for protection has been approved under these
      Regulations,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a member of the Convention
      refugee abroad class, financial assistance in the form of funds
      from a governmental resettlement assistance program is available
      in Canada for the foreign national and their family members
      included in the application for protection, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national has sufficient financial
      resources to provide for the lodging, care and maintenance, and
      for the resettlement in Canada, of themself and their family
      members included in the application for protection;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if the foreign national intends to reside in a
    province other than the Province of Quebec, the foreign national and
    their family members included in the application for protection will
    be able to become successfully established in Canada, taking into
    account the following factors:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} their resourcefulness and other similar qualities
      that assist in integration in a new society,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the presence of their relatives, including the
      relatives of a spouse or a common-law partner, or their sponsor in
      the expected community of resettlement,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} their potential for employment in Canada, given
      their education, work experience and skills, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} their ability to learn to communicate in one of
      the official languages of Canada;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} if the foreign national intends to reside in the
    Province of Quebec, the competent authority of that Province is of
    the opinion that the foreign national and their family members
    included in the application for protection meet the selection
    criteria of the Province; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsections (3) and (4), the foreign
    national and their family members included in the application for
    protection are not inadmissible.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(g) does not apply to a foreign
  national, or their family members included in the application for
  protection, who has been determined by an officer to be vulnerable or
  in urgent need of protection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Financial inadmissibility --- exemption

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A foreign national who is a member of a class
  prescribed by this Division, and meets the applicable requirements of
  this Division, is exempted from the application of section 39 of the
  Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Health grounds --- exception

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A foreign national who is a member of a class
  prescribed by this Division, and meets the applicable requirements of
  this Division, is exempted from the application of paragraph 38(1)(c)
  of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-167, s. 80(F)
- SOR/2011-222, s. 4
- SOR/2012-225, s. 4
- SOR/2014-140, s. 13(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-139-20121018.html "Link to previous version of section 139"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Class of family members

**[[140]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-140 .sectionLabel}** Family members of an
applicant who is determined to be a member of a class under this
Division are members of the applicant's class.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[140.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-140.1 .sectionLabel}** An application for
a permanent resident visa submitted by a foreign national under this
Division shall indicate that the foreign national is outside Canada and
is making a claim for refugee protection and shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} contain the name, address and country of birth of the
  applicant and of all their accompanying family members;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} contain the name and country of birth of all the
  applicant's non-accompanying family members; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} indicate whether the applicant or any of their
  accompanying or non-accompanying family members is the spouse,
  common-law partner or conjugal partner of another person.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-225, s. 5
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sponsorship of foreign national ---
requirement to attach applications

- **[[140.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-140.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the foreign national making an
  application for a permanent resident visa under this Division is being
  sponsored, the application for a permanent resident visa shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be accompanied by a sponsorship application
    referred to in paragraph 153(1)(b) by which the foreign national is
    being sponsored; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be attached to the sponsorship application sent by
    the sponsor in accordance with subsection 153(1.2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Place of application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The foreign national who has chosen to have their
  application for a permanent resident visa accompanied by the
  sponsorship application shall send the application for a permanent
  resident visa and the sponsorship application to the Department's Case
  Processing Centre in Canada for processing those applications.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-225, s. 5
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Referral requirement

- **[[140.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-140.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the foreign national making an
  application for a permanent resident visa under this Division is not
  being sponsored, a foreign national making an application for a
  permanent resident visa under this Division shall submit their
  application with one of the following referrals, if the referral has
  not yet been submitted to the immigration office by its issuer:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a referral from a referral organization;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a referral resulting from an arrangement between
    the Minister and the government of a foreign state or any
    institution of such a government relating to resettlement; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a referral resulting from an agreement relating to
    resettlement entered into by the Government of Canada and an
    international organization or the government of a foreign state.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A foreign national may submit the application without
  a referral if they reside in a geographic area as determined by the
  Minister in accordance with subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's determination

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may determine on the basis of the
  following factors that a geographic area is an area in which
  circumstances justify the submission of permanent resident visa
  applications without a referral:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} advice from referral organizations with which the
    Minister has entered into a memorandum of understanding under
    section 143 that they are unable to make the number of referrals
    specified in their memorandum of understanding for the area;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the inability of referral organizations to refer
    persons in the area;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the resettlement needs in the area, after
    consultation with referral organizations that have substantial
    knowledge of the area; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the relative importance of resettlement needs in
    the area, within the context of resettlement needs globally.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Place of application

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the foreign national who is being referred under
  any of paragraphs (1)(a) to (c) or if the foreign national resides in
  a geographic area as determined by the Minister in accordance with
  subsection (3), the foreign national shall submit their application to
  the immigration office outside Canada that serves the foreign
  national's place of residence.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-225, s. 5
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of documents

**[[140.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-140.4 .sectionLabel}** An application for
a permanent resident visa made under this Division, its related
sponsorship application made under Division 2 of this Part and all
documents submitted in support of the applications, except the
information referred to in subparagraphs 12.3(b)(i) and (ii), shall be
returned to the person who sent the applications as a result of the
choice made under subsection 140.2(1) if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an application for a permanent
  resident visa, the requirements set out in paragraph 139(1)(b) are not
  met; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a sponsorship application, the
  requirements set out in paragraph 153(1)(b) and subsections 153(1.2)
  and (2) are not met.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-225, s. 5
- SOR/2018-128, s. 6
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-140.4-20121018.html "Link to previous version of section 140.4"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-accompanying family member

- **[[141]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-141 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  permanent resident visa shall be issued to a family member who does
  not accompany the applicant if, following an examination, it is
  established that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the family member was included in the applicant's
    permanent resident visa application at the time that application was
    made, or was added to that application before the applicant's
    departure for Canada;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the family member submits their application to an
    officer outside Canada within one year from the day on which refugee
    protection is conferred on the applicant;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the family member is not inadmissible;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the applicant is the subject of a sponsorship
    application referred to in paragraph 139(1)(f)(i), their sponsor has
    been notified of the family member's application and an officer is
    satisfied that there are adequate financial arrangements for
    resettlement; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a family member who intends to
    reside in the Province of Quebec, the competent authority of that
    Province is of the opinion that the foreign national meets the
    selection criteria of the Province.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of paragraph 139(1)(b)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the requirements set out in
  paragraph 139(1)(b) do not apply to the application of a
  non-accompanying family member.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-167, s. 80(F)
- SOR/2012-225, s. 6
- SOR/2014-140, s. 14
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-141-20121018.html "Link to previous version of section 141"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements --- family members

**[[142]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-142 .sectionLabel}** Subject to subsections
25.1(1) and (6) to (8) and for the purposes of this Division, to be
considered a family member of an applicant, a person must be a family
member of the applicant

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the time the application referred to in paragraph
  139(1)(b) is made; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} at the time of the determination of the application
  referred to in paragraph 141(1)(b).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-225, s. 7
- SOR/2014-133, s. 9
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-142-20121018.html "Link to previous version of section 142"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Memorandum of understanding

- **[[143]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-143 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may enter into a memorandum of understanding with an
  organization for the purpose of locating and identifying Convention
  refugees and persons in similar circumstances if the organization
  demonstrates

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a working knowledge of the provisions of the Act
    relating to protection criteria; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an ability abroad to locate and identify Convention
    refugees and persons in similar circumstances.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of memorandum of understanding

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The memorandum of understanding shall include
  provisions with respect to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the geographic area to be served by the
    organization;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of referrals that may be made by the
    organization and the manner of referral;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the training of members or employees of the
    organization; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the grounds for suspending or cancelling the
    memorandum of understanding.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-167, s. 46(F)
- SOR/2009-163, s. 3(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-143-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 143"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Convention Refugees Abroad]{.HTitleText3} {#h-687976 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Convention refugees abroad class

**[[144]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144 .sectionLabel}** The Convention refugees
abroad class is prescribed as a class of persons who may be issued a
permanent resident visa on the basis of the requirements of this
Division.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Member of Convention refugees abroad
class

**[[145]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-145 .sectionLabel}** A foreign national is a
Convention refugee abroad and a member of the Convention refugees abroad
class if the foreign national has been determined, outside Canada, by an
officer to be a Convention refugee.

#### [Humanitarian-protected Persons Abroad]{.HTitleText3} {#h-687981 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Person in similar circumstances to those
of a Convention refugee

- **[[146]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-146 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of subsection 12(3) of the Act, a person in similar
  circumstances to those of a Convention refugee is a member of the
  country of asylum class.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Humanitarian-protected persons abroad

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The country of asylum class is prescribed as a
  humanitarian-protected persons abroad class of persons who may be
  issued permanent resident visas on the basis of the requirements of
  this Division.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2011-222, s. 5
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-146-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 146"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Member of country of asylum class

**[[147]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-147 .sectionLabel}** A foreign national is a
member of the country of asylum class if they have been determined by an
officer to be in need of resettlement because

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} they are outside all of their countries of
  nationality and habitual residence; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} they have been, and continue to be, seriously and
  personally affected by civil war, armed conflict or massive violation
  of human rights in each of those countries.

**[[148]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-148 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2011-222, s. 6\]]{.Repealed}
