[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entry to verify compliance with section
209.2

- **[[209.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209.8
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5), if any of
  the circumstances set out in section 209.5 exists, an officer may, for
  the purpose of verifying compliance with the conditions set out in
  section 209.2, enter and inspect any premises or place in which a
  foreign national referred to in that section performs work.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers on entry

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The officer may, for that purpose,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} ask the employer and any person employed by the
    employer any relevant questions;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} require from the employer, for examination, any
    documents found in the premises or place;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} use copying equipment in the premises or place, or
    require the employer to make copies of documents, and remove the
    copies for examination or, if it is not possible to make copies in
    the premises or place, remove the documents to make copies;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} take photographs and make video or audio
    recordings;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} examine anything in the premises or place;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} require the employer to use any computer or other
    electronic device in the premises or place to allow the officer to
    examine any relevant document contained in or available to it; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} be accompanied or assisted in the premises or place
    by any person required by the officer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entering private property

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An officer and any person accompanying the officer
  may enter on and pass through private property, other than a
  dwelling-house, to gain entry to a premises or place referred to in
  subsection (1). For greater certainty, they are not liable for doing
  so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Person accompanying officer

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A person may, at an officer's request, accompany the
  officer to assist them to access the premises or place referred to in
  subsection (1) and is not liable for doing so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dwelling-house

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a dwelling-house, an officer may enter
  it without the occupant's consent only under the authority of a
  warrant issued under subsection (6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance of warrant

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} On [ex parte]{.otherLang lang="la"} application, a
  justice of the peace may issue a warrant authorizing an officer who is
  named in it or the Minister of Employment and Social Development, as
  the case may be, to enter a dwelling-house, subject to any conditions
  specified in the warrant, if the justice of the peace is satisfied by
  information on oath that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} there are reasonable grounds to believe that the
    dwelling-house is a premises or place referred to in subsection (1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} entry into the dwelling-house is necessary to
    verify compliance with the conditions set out in section 209.2; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} entry was refused by the occupant or there are
    reasonable grounds to believe that entry will be refused or that
    consent to entry cannot be obtained from the occupant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister of Employment and Social
  Development

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of Employment and Social Development may
  exercise the powers set out in this section on the request of an
  officer.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-245, s. 7
- SOR/2014-84, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-209.8-20131231.html "Link to previous version of section 209.8"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entry to verify compliance with section
209.3

- **[[209.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209.9
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5), if any of
  the circumstances set out in section 209.5 exists, the Minister of
  Employment and Social Development may, for the purpose of verifying
  compliance with the conditions set out in section 209.3, enter and
  inspect any premises or place in which a foreign national referred to
  in that section performs work and any premises or place that the
  employer has provided to the foreign national as accommodation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers on entry

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of Employment and Social Development
  may, for that purpose,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} ask the employer and any person employed by the
    employer any relevant questions;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} require from the employer, for examination, any
    documents found in the premises or place;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} use copying equipment in the premises or place, or
    require the employer to make copies of documents, and remove the
    copies for examination or, if it is not possible to make copies in
    the premises or place, remove the documents to make copies;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} take photographs and make video or audio
    recordings;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} examine anything in the premises or place;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} require the employer to use any computer or other
    electronic device in the premises or place to allow that Minister to
    examine any relevant document contained in or available to it; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} be accompanied or assisted in the premises or place
    by any person required by that Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entering private property

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of Employment and Social Development and
  any person accompanying him or her may enter on and pass through
  private property, other than a dwelling-house, to gain entry to a
  premises or place referred to in subsection (1). For greater
  certainty, they are not liable for doing so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Person accompanying Minister of
  Employment and Social Development

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A person may, at the Minister of Employment and
  Social Development's request, accompany that Minister to assist him or
  her to access the premises or place referred to in subsection (1) and
  is not liable for doing so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dwelling-house

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a dwelling-house, the Minister of
  Employment and Social Development may enter it without the occupant's
  consent only under the authority of a warrant issued under subsection
  (6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance of warrant

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} On [ex parte]{.otherLang lang="la"} application, a
  justice of the peace may issue a warrant authorizing the Minister of
  Employment and Social Development to enter a dwelling-house, subject
  to any conditions specified in the warrant, if the justice of the
  peace is satisfied by information on oath that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} there are reasonable grounds to believe that the
    dwelling-house is a premises or place referred to in subsection (1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} entry into the dwelling-house is necessary to
    verify compliance with the conditions set out in section 209.3; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} entry was refused by the occupant or there are
    reasonable grounds to believe that entry will be refused or that
    consent to entry cannot be obtained from the occupant.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-245, s. 7
- SOR/2014-84, ss. 2, 3(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-209.9-20131231.html "Link to previous version of section 209.9"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption from certain conditions

**[[209.91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209.91 .sectionLabel}** The following
employers are exempt from the application of sections 209.11 and 209.2:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the government of a foreign state that makes an offer
  of employment to a foreign national in respect of the performance of
  duties as an official of that state;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the office of a political subdivision of a foreign
  state that enjoys privileges and immunities granted under subsection
  6(1) of the [Foreign Missions and International Organizations
  Act](/eng/acts/F-29.4);

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a foreign diplomatic mission or consular post
  referred to in Part I of the [Foreign Missions and International
  Organizations Act](/eng/acts/F-29.4) or an [[international
  organization]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} or [[accredited
  mission]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection 2(1) of that
  Act; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the owner or operator of an [[international bridge or
  tunnel]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in section 2 of the
  [International Bridges and Tunnels Act](/eng/acts/I-17.05).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-26, s. 3
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-209.91-20151201.html "Link to previous version of section 209.91"){.PITLink}

### [DIVISION 5]{.HLabel2}[Collection and Disclosure of Information]{.HTitleText2} {#h-689003 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Collection of information

**[[209.911]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209.911 .sectionLabel}** The Minister of
Employment and Social Development may collect information related to an
employer's compliance with the conditions set out in section 209.2.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-142, s. 14](#1361943-1374855){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1361943-1374855.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure of information

**[[209.92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209.92 .sectionLabel}** An officer may,
for the purposes of determining whether a work permit is to be issued to
a foreign national under subsection 200(1), of making a determination
under paragraphs 203(1)(a) to (g), if applicable, or of verifying
compliance with the conditions set out in sections 209.2 to 209.4,
disclose to the Minister of Employment and Social Development and to the
competent authorities of the provinces concerned information that
relates to an application for a work permit or to an employer's
compliance with the conditions set out in sections 209.2 to 209.4.

::: HistoricalNote

- SOR/2014-84, s. 2

  ajax-after="1361943-1374858.html"}

### [DIVISION 6]{.HLabel2}[Administrative Monetary Penalties and Other Consequences for Failure To Comply with Conditions Imposed on Employers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-689008 .Subheading}

#### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-689009 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[209.93]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209.93 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Division.

[[large business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[large business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any business that is
    not a small business. ([grande entreprise]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[small business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[small business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any business, including
    its affiliates, that has fewer than 100 employees or less than \$5
    million in annual gross revenues at the time a request for an
    assessment under subsection 203(2) is received, or if no such
    request is made, at the time a copy of an offer of employment for a
    work permit application is provided to the Minister under paragraph
    209.11(1)(d). ([petite entreprise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-144, s. 8
:::

#### [Purpose]{.HTitleText3} {#h-689016 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purpose of Division

**[[209.94]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209.94 .sectionLabel}** The purpose of
this Division is to encourage compliance with the provisions of the Act
and these Regulations and not to punish.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-144, s. 8
:::

#### [Violations]{.HTitleText3} {#h-689021 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Violations

- **[[209.95]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209.95
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An employer referred to in
  subsection 209.2(1) or 209.3(1) who fails to comply with one of the
  conditions set out in the provisions listed in column 1 of Table 1 of
  Schedule 2 --- if the failure to do so is not justified under
  subsection 209.2(3), (3.1) or (4), 209.3(3), (4) or (5), or 209.4(2)
  --- commits a violation and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is liable to an administrative monetary penalty of
    an amount that is determined in accordance with section 209.98 or if
    it is determined under that section that there is no penalty, is
    issued a warning informing the employer that there is no
    administrative monetary penalty for the violation but that the
    violation will be considered in the calculation of the total number
    of points under subparagraph 209.991(1)(a)(i) for any subsequent
    violation; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, is ineligible to employ a foreign
    national for whom a work permit is required for the period
    determined in accordance with section 209.99.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Discrepancy

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the event of a discrepancy between the short-form
  description in column 2 of Table 1 of Schedule 2 and the provision to
  which it pertains, the provision prevails.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-144, s. 8
- [SOR/2022-142, s. 15](#1361945-1374860){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1361945-1374860.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-209.95-20151201.html "Link to previous version of section 209.95"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Rules Applicable to Violations]{.HTitleText3} {#h-689031 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Separate violation --- multiple foreign
nationals

- **[[209.96]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209.96
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A failure to comply --- that is not
  justified under subsection 209.2(3), (4) or (5), 209.3(3), (4) or (5)
  or 209.4(2) --- with a condition that affects more than one foreign
  national constitutes a separate violation for each foreign national
  affected.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Separate violation --- occupation,
  wages or working conditions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A failure to comply --- that is not justified under
  subsection 209.2(3) or 209.3(3) --- with any one of the following
  elements of the condition set out in item 9 of Table 1 of Schedule 2,
  constitutes a separate violation:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to provide the foreign national with employment in
    the same occupation as the occupation that is set out in the foreign
    national's offer of employment;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to provide the foreign national with wages that are
    substantially the same as --- but not less favourable than --- those
    set out in the foreign national's offer of employment; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to provide the foreign national with working
    conditions that are substantially the same as --- but not less
    favourable than --- those set out in the foreign national's offer of
    employment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Separate violation --- live-in
  caregivers

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} With respect to employers who employ foreign
  nationals as live-in caregivers, a failure to comply --- that is not
  justified under subsection 209.3(3) --- with either one of the
  following elements of the condition set out in item 10 of Table 1 of
  Schedule 2, constitutes a separate violation:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to ensure that the foreign national resides in a
    private household in Canada,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to ensure that the foreign national provides child
    care, senior home support care or care of a disabled person in that
    household without supervision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Separate violation --- abuse

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A failure to comply --- that is not justified under
  subsection 209.2(3) or 209.3(3) --- with the condition set out in item
  17 of Table 1 of Schedule 2 with respect to any one of the elements
  set out in paragraphs 196.2(1)(a) to (e) constitutes a separate
  violation.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-144, s. 8
- SOR/2017-56, s. 5
- [SOR/2022-142, s. 16](#1361947-1374864){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1361947-1374864.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-209.96-20170413.html "Link to previous version of section 209.96"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Classification]{.HTitleText3} {#h-689048 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provisions

**[[209.97]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209.97 .sectionLabel}** A failure to
comply --- that is not justified under subsection 209.2(3), (4) or (5),
209.3(3), (4) or (5) or 209.4(2) --- with a condition that is set out in
one of the provisions listed in column 1 of Table 1 of Schedule 2 is
classified as a violation of Type A, Type B or Type C as set out in
column 3 of that Table.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-144, s. 8
- [SOR/2022-142, s. 17](#1361952-1374867){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1361952-1374867.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-209.97-20151201.html "Link to previous version of section 209.97"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Administrative Monetary Penalty Amount]{.HTitleText3} {#h-689053 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Administrative monetary penalty amount

**[[209.98]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209.98 .sectionLabel}** The
administrative monetary penalty for a violation is the amount set out in
column 2, 3 or 4 of Table 2 of Schedule 2 opposite the total number of
points determined under section 209.991 as set out in column 1,
depending on the type of violation and whether it is committed by an
individual or small business, or a large business.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-144, s. 8
:::

#### [Period of Ineligibility]{.HTitleText3} {#h-689058 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of ineligibility

- **[[209.99]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209.99
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The period of ineligibility for a
  violation is the period set out in column 2, 3 or 4 of Table 3 of
  Schedule 2 opposite the total number of points determined under
  section 209.991 as set out in column 1 depending on the type of
  violation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Beginning of period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The period referred to in subsection (1) begins on
  the day on which the determination referred to in subsection
  209.996(1) or (2) is made in respect of the employer.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-144, s. 8
:::

#### [Total Number of Points]{.HTitleText3} {#h-689066 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculation

- **[[209.991]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209.991
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The total number of points in
  respect of each violation is determined by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} considering

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the compliance history of the employer who
      committed the violation set out in column 1 of Table 4 of Schedule
      2, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the severity criteria set out in column 1 of
      Table 5 of Schedule 2;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ascribing

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for the criterion described in subparagraph
      (a)(i), the applicable number of points set out in column 2 of
      Table 4 of Schedule 2,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for the criteria described in subparagraph
      (a)(ii), the applicable number of points set out in column 2 of
      Table 5 of Schedule 2 having regard to the severity or the impact
      of the violation, as the case may be,

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} adding the values obtained under paragraph (b); and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the employer made an acceptable voluntary
    disclosure in accordance with subsections (2) and (3) and the value
    obtained under paragraph (c)

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is four or more, subtracting four points from the
      value obtained under that paragraph , or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is less than four, replacing that value with a
      value of zero.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Voluntary disclosure --- criteria of
  acceptability

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The voluntary disclosure made by an employer with
  respect to the commission of a violation by the employer is acceptable
  if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the disclosure is complete; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at the time the voluntary disclosure is made, the
    powers set out in sections 209.6 to 209.9 are not being exercised in
    respect of the employer nor is any enforcement action related to an
    offence arising out of the contravention of a provision of the Act
    being undertaken in respect of the employer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Voluntary disclosure --- considerations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), an officer or the Minister of
  Employment and Social Development may consider that the voluntary
  disclosure is not acceptable after considering

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the severity of the impact of the violation on the
    foreign national;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an employer described in subsection
    209.2(1), the severity of the impact of the violation on the
    Canadian economy, or in the case of an employer described in
    subsection 209.3(1), the severity of the impact of the violation on
    the Canadian labour market;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} whether the disclosure was made in a timely manner;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the number of times an acceptable voluntary
    disclosure is made by the employer; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the nature of the condition with which the employer
    failed to comply.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-144, s. 8
:::

#### [Multiple Violations]{.HTitleText3} {#h-689093 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cumulative amounts

- **[[209.992]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209.992
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a notice of preliminary finding
  under section 209.993 or a notice of final determination under section
  209.996 that is issued to an employer lists more than one violation,
  the administrative monetary penalty amounts are cumulative but the
  total must not exceed \$1 million.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applicable period of ineligibility

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a notice of preliminary finding or a notice of
  final determination that is issued to an employer includes more than
  one period of ineligibility, the longest period of ineligibility
  applies.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-144, s. 8
:::

#### [Notice of Preliminary Finding]{.HTitleText3} {#h-689101 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice --- issuance by officer

- **[[209.993]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209.993
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If an officer assesses, on the basis
  of information obtained by any officer or the Minister of Employment
  and Social Development during the exercise of the powers set out in
  sections 209.6 to 209.8 and any other relevant information, that an
  employer has committed a violation because that employer failed to
  comply with one of the conditions set out in the provisions listed in
  column 1 of Table 1 of Schedule 2 and the failure to do so was not
  justified under subsection 209.2(3), (4) or (5) or 209.4(2), the
  officer must issue a notice of preliminary finding to the employer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice --- issuance by Minister

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister of Employment and Social Development
  assesses, on the basis of information obtained during the exercise of
  the powers set out in sections 209.6, 209.7 and 209.9 and any other
  relevant information, that an employer has committed a violation
  because that employer failed to comply with one of the conditions set
  out in the provisions listed in column 1 of Table 1 of Schedule 2 and
  the failure to do so was not justified under subsection 209.3(3), (4)
  or (5) or 209.4(2), that Minister must issue a notice of preliminary
  finding to the employer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The notice of preliminary finding must include the
  following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the employer referred to in
    subsection (1) or (2), as the case may be;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the condition with which the employer failed to
    comply as well as the provision listed in column 1 of Table 1 of
    Schedule 2, the relevant facts surrounding the violation and the
    reasons for the preliminary finding;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the administrative monetary penalty
    amount and the period of ineligibility for the violation as well as
    the statement that the violation will be considered in the
    calculation of the total number of points under subparagraph
    209.991(1)(a)(i) for any subsequent violation;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the statement that a warning may be
    issued to the employer informing them that there is no
    administrative monetary penalty for the violation but that the
    violation will be considered in the calculation of the total number
    of points under subparagraph 209.991(1)(a)(i) for any subsequent
    violation; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the statement that the employer may make written
    submissions within the period set out in section 209.994 with
    respect to the information referred to in paragraphs (b) to (d).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-144, s. 8
- [SOR/2022-142, s. 18](#1361954-1374871){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1361954-1374871.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-209.993-20151201.html "Link to previous version of section 209.993"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Submissions by employer --- period

- **[[209.994]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209.994
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An employer to whom a notice of
  preliminary finding under section 209.993 or a corrected notice of
  preliminary finding under section 209.995 is issued may, within 30
  days after the day on which it is received,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} make written submissions with respect to the
    information referred to in paragraphs 209.993(3)(b) to (d); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} request an extension of that period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reduced period

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), if the notice of
  preliminary finding or the corrected notice of preliminary finding is
  solely in respect of a failure to comply with any one of the
  conditions set out in the provisions listed in items 18 to 23 of Table
  1 of Schedule 2, the employer may make a written submission or request
  referred to in paragraph (1)(a) or (b) within five days after the day
  on which the notice or corrected notice is received.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed receipt

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 9.3(2) of these Regulations and
  section 3 of the [Electronic Documents and Electronic Information
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2014-117), a notice of preliminary
  finding or a corrected or cancelled notice of preliminary finding is
  deemed to have been received 10 days after the day on which it is
  sent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reduced period

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), if the notice of
  preliminary finding or the corrected notice of preliminary finding is
  solely in respect of a failure to comply with any one of the
  conditions set out in the provisions listed in items 18 to 23 of Table
  1 of Schedule 2, the notice or corrected notice is deemed to have been
  received five days after the day on which it is sent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Submissions by employer --- extension
  of period

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An officer or the Minister of Employment and Social
  Development may extend the period referred to in subsection (1) if
  there is a reasonable explanation justifying its extension.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-144, s. 8
- [SOR/2019-174, s. 10](#1135486-1138051){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1135486-1138051.html"}
- [SOR/2020-91, s. 9](#1243463-1244440){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1243463-1244440.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-209.994-20190603.html "Link to previous version of section 209.994"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Correction or cancellation of notice

**[[209.995]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209.995 .sectionLabel}** An officer or
the Minister of Employment and Social Development may correct any
information in a notice of preliminary finding that is issued under
subsection 209.993(1) or (2), or cancel one, at any time before a notice
of final determination is issued under section 209.996.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-144, s. 8
:::

#### [Notice of Final Determination]{.HTitleText3} {#h-689131 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice --- issuance by officer

- **[[209.996]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209.996
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), if an
  officer determines, on the basis of information obtained by any
  officer or the Minister of Employment and Social Development during
  the exercise of the powers set out in sections 209.6 to 209.8 and any
  other relevant information, that an employer has committed a violation
  because that employer failed to comply with one of the conditions set
  out in the provisions listed in column 1 of Table 1 of Schedule 2 and
  the failure to do so was not justified under subsection 209.2(3), (4)
  or (5) or 209.4(2), the officer must issue a notice of final
  determination to the employer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice --- issuance by Minister

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), if the Minister of
  Employment and Social Development determines, on the basis of
  information obtained during the exercise of the powers set out in
  sections 209.6, 209.7 and 209.9 and any other relevant information,
  that an employer has committed a violation because that employer
  failed to comply with one of the conditions set out in the provisions
  listed in column 1 of Table 1 of Schedule 2 and the failure to do so
  was not justified under subsection 209.3(3), (4) or (5) or 209.4(2),
  that Minister must issue a notice of final determination to the
  employer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An officer or the Minister of Employment and Social
  Development must not make a determination before the expiry of the
  period set out in subsection 209.994(1) or the period extended under
  subsection 209.994(3) as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of notice

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The notice of final determination must include the
  following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the employer referred to in
    subsection (1) or (2), as the case may be;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the condition with which the employer failed to
    comply as well as the provision listed in column 1 of Table 1 of
    Schedule 2, the relevant facts surrounding the violation and the
    reasons for the determination;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the administrative monetary penalty
    amount and the period of ineligibility for the violation as well as
    the statement indicating that the violation will be considered in
    the calculation of the total number of points under subparagraph
    209.991(1)(a)(i) for any subsequent violation;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, a warning informing the employer
    that there is no administrative monetary penalty for the violation
    but that the violation will be considered in the calculation of the
    total number of points under subparagraph 209.991(1)(a)(i) for any
    subsequent violation;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, a statement that the administrative
    monetary penalty amount must be paid within 30 days after the day on
    which the notice of final determination is received by the employer,
    unless a payment agreement for the payment of amount and interest
    has been reached within that period; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} how the administrative monetary penalty is to be
    paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum amount within 12 months

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the sum of the administrative monetary penalty
  amount described in paragraph (4)(c) and all previous administrative
  monetary penalty amounts provided for in notices of final
  determination issued to the employer in question within 12 months
  before the day on which the determination is made exceeds \$1 million,
  then the amount of the penalty must be reduced by that excess.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed receipt

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 9.3(2) of these Regulations and
  section 3 of the [Electronic Documents and Electronic Information
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2014-117), a notice of final
  determination is deemed to have been received 10 days after the day on
  which it is sent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reduced period

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (6), if the notice of final
  determination is solely in respect of a failure to comply with any one
  of the conditions set out in the provisions listed in items 18 to 23
  of Table 1 of Schedule 2, the notice is deemed to have been received
  five days after the day on which it is sent.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-144, s. 8
- [SOR/2019-174, s. 11](#1135488-1138054){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1135488-1138054.html"}
- [SOR/2020-91, s. 10](#1243468-1244443){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1243468-1244443.html"}
- [SOR/2022-142, s. 19](#1361956-1374875){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1361956-1374875.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-209.996-20200420.html "Link to previous version of section 209.996"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [List of Employers]{.HTitleText3} {#h-689153 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication of employer's information

- **[[209.997]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209.997
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If an officer or the Minister of
  Employment and Social Development makes a determination under
  subsection 209.996(1) or (2) in respect of an employer, the Department
  or that Minister, as the case may be, must add the information
  referred to in subsection (2) to the list referred to in that
  subsection, except if the officer or that Minister issues a warning to
  the employer in accordance with paragraph 209.996(4)(d).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of list

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A list is posted on one or more Government of Canada
  websites and includes the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the employer's name;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the employer's address;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the conditions set out in the provisions listed in
    column 1 of Table 1 of Schedule 2 with which the employer failed to
    comply;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the determination was made;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the eligibility status of the employer;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if applicable,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the administrative monetary penalty amount, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the ineligibility period of the employer.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-144, s. 8
- [SOR/2022-142, s. 20](#1361958-1374877){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1361958-1374877.html"}
:::
