### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Fees for Applications to Remain in Canada as a Permanent Resident]{.HTitleText2} {#h-690176 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fee

- **[[301]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-301 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following fees are payable for processing an application to remain in
  Canada as a permanent resident:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the application is made by a person as a member
    of the spouse or common-law partner in Canada class

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a principal applicant, \$475,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a family member of the principal
      applicant who is a spouse or common-law partner, \$550, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a family member of the principal
      applicant who is a dependent child, \$150; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the application is made by a person as a
    protected person referred to in subsection 21(2) of the Act

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a principal applicant, \$550,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a family member of the principal
      applicant who is a spouse or common-law partner, \$550, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a family member of the principal
      applicant who is a dependent child, \$150.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} A person who is a member of the protected temporary
  residents class and the family members included in their application
  are not required to pay the fees referred to in subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment by sponsor

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The fee payable under subsection (1) in respect of a
  person who makes an application as a member of the spouse or
  common-law partner in Canada class or their family members

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is payable, together with the fee payable under
    subsection 304(1), at the time the sponsor files the sponsorship
    application; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} shall be repaid in accordance with regulations
    referred to in subsection 20(2) of the [Financial Administration
    Act](/eng/acts/F-11) if, before the processing of the application to
    remain in Canada as a permanent resident has begun, the sponsorship
    application is withdrawn by the sponsor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Age

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (1)(a), the age of the
  person in respect of whom the application is made shall be determined
  as of the day the sponsorship application is filed.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-167, s. 72
- SOR/2009-163, s. 16
- SOR/2012-154, s. 15
- SOR/2014-133, s. 11
- SOR/2017-78, s. 10
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-301-20140801.html "Link to previous version of section 301"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fee --- \$325

**[[302]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-302 .sectionLabel}** A fee of \$325 is
payable for processing an application by a person as a member of the
permit holder class to remain in Canada as a permanent resident.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-167, s. 73(E)
:::

### [DIVISION 4]{.HLabel2}[Right of Permanent Residence]{.HTitleText2} {#h-690202 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fee --- \$500

- **[[303]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-303 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  fee of \$500 is payable by a person for the acquisition of permanent
  resident status.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Indexation

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The fee set out in subsection (1) shall be indexed
  at 09:00:00 a.m. Eastern daylight time on April 30, 2022, and every
  two years after that on April 30 at that same time, in accordance with
  the cumulative percentage increase to the Consumer Price Index for
  Canada, published by Statistics Canada, for the two previous years,
  rounded to the nearest five dollars.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following persons are not required to pay the fee
  referred to in subsection (1):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person who is a family member of a principal
    applicant and is a dependent child referred to in paragraph (b)
    or (c) of the definition [[family member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in
    subsection 1(3);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a principal applicant who is a foreign national
    referred to in paragraph 117(1)(b), (f) or (g);

  - [(b.1)]{.lawlabel} a principal applicant in Canada who has made an
    application in accordance with section 66 and is a dependent child
    of a permanent resident or of a Canadian citizen;

  - [(b.2)]{.lawlabel} a member of the permit holder class who is a
    dependent child of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a member of the permit holder class who has made
      an application to remain in Canada as a permanent resident, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a permanent resident or a Canadian citizen;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a protected person within the meaning of subsection
    95(2) of the Act who has applied to remain in Canada as a permanent
    resident, and their family members;

  - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} a person who is a member of the protected
    temporary residents class and is described in paragraph 151.1(2)(b)
    and the family members included in their application;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a person who is a member of the Convention refugees
    abroad class, and the family members included in their application;
    and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a person who is a member of a
    humanitarian-protected persons abroad class, and the family members
    included in their application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The fee referred to in subsection (1) is payable

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an application by or on behalf of a
    person for a permanent resident visa, before the visa is issued; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an application by or on behalf of a
    foreign national to remain in Canada as a permanent resident, before
    the foreign national becomes a permanent resident.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Remission

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The fee referred to in subsection (1) is remitted if
  the person does not acquire permanent resident status, in which case
  the fee shall be repaid by the Minister to the person who paid it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional --- subsection (4)

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (4), if the fee was
  paid before the day on which this subsection comes into force, the
  amount to be remitted and repaid --- except to the extent otherwise
  remitted --- is \$975.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional --- remission

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsections (4) and (5), in the case where
  the fee of \$975 was paid in accordance with paragraph (3)(a), a
  portion of that fee in the amount of \$485 is remitted and shall be
  repaid --- except to the extent otherwise remitted --- by the Minister
  to the person who paid the fee if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person in respect of whom the fee was paid has,
    on or before the day on which this subsection comes into force, not
    yet acquired permanent resident status and they are a person
    referred to in any of paragraphs 117(1)(a), (c), (d) or (h); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person in respect of whom the fee was paid
    acquires permanent resident status on or after the day on which this
    subsection comes into force and they are not a person referred to in
    any of paragraphs 117(1)(a), (c), (d) or (h).

::: HistoricalNote

### [DIVISION 4.1]{.HLabel2}[Other Fees in Respect of Work Permits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-690232 .Subheading}

#### [Compliance Regime --- Employer Fee]{.HTitleText3} {#h-690233 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fee --- \$230

- **[[303.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-303.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A fee of \$230 is payable by an
  employer who has made an offer of employment to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a foreign national in respect of work described in
    section 204 or 205;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a foreign national described in section 207; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a foreign national referred to in paragraph (a)
    or (b) who makes an application for renewal of a work permit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment of fees

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The fee referred to in subsection (1) is payable
  before the foreign national to whom the offer of employment is made
  makes an application for a work permit or an application for renewal
  of the work permit.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2019-174, s. 15\]]{.Repealed}

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2019-174, s. 15\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4.1)]{.lawlabel} An employer who is exempt from the application of
  sections 209.11 and 209.2 under section 209.91 is not required to pay
  the fee referred to in subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} An employer is not required to pay the fee referred
  to in subsection (1) if the offer of employment is made to a foreign
  national --- other than a person referred to in paragraph 299(2)(i)
  --- who under subsection 299(2) is not required to pay a fee for
  processing an application for a work permit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Remission

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The fee referred to in subsection (1) is remitted,
  and must be repaid by the Minister to the person who paid it, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the work permit is refused; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the employer withdraws the offer of employment and
    requests a remission before the work permit is issued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum fee

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The total amount of fees payable under subsection (1)
  by an employer who has made offers of employment to a group of three
  or more foreign nationals, consisting of performing artists and their
  staff, is \$690, if those offers are made at the same time.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-25, s. 6
- SOR/2018-26, s. 4
- [SOR/2019-174, s. 15](#1135495-1138065){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1135495-1138065.html"}
:::

#### [Rights and Privileges]{.HTitleText3} {#h-690258 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fee --- \$100

- **[[303.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-303.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A fee of \$100 is payable by a
  person for the rights and privileges conferred by means of a work
  permit if that person is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a foreign national who intends to perform work
    described in section 204 or 205 but does not have an offer of
    employment to perform that work;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a foreign national described in paragraph 207(b)
    who does not have an offer of employment; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a family member of a foreign national referred to
    in paragraph (b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following persons are not required to pay the fee
  referred to in subsection (1):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person --- other than a person referred to in
    paragraph 299(2)(i) --- who under subsection 299(2) is not required
    to pay a fee for processing an application for a work permit;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person referred to in paragraph 299(2)(i) who
    intends to perform work under an international agreement between
    Canada and one or more countries, if the agreement

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} prohibits the payment of a fee other than a
      participation fee, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is in force at the time that the person makes an
      application for a work permit or for renewal of the work permit;
      and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2017-78, s. 11\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Remission

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The fee referred to in subsection (1) is remitted,
  and must be repaid by the Minister to the person who paid it, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the work permit is refused; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national withdraws their application
    and requests a remission before the work permit is issued.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-25, s. 6
- SOR/2017-78, s. 11
:::

### [DIVISION 5]{.HLabel2}[Fees for Other Applications and Services]{.HTitleText2} {#h-690278 .Subheading}

#### [Sponsorship Application for Family Classes]{.HTitleText3} {#h-690279 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fee --- \$75

- **[[304]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-304 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  fee of \$75 is payable for processing a sponsorship application under
  Part 7.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The fee referred to in subsection (1) is payable at
  the time the application is filed.

#### [Extension of Authorization to Remain in Canada as a Temporary Resident]{.HTitleText3} {#h-690285 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fee --- \$100

- **[[305]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-305 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  fee of \$100 is payable for processing an application under subsection
  181(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following persons are not required to pay the fee
  referred to in subsection (1):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person who makes an application for a work permit
    or a study permit at the same time as they make the application
    referred to in subsection (1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person who has made a claim for refugee
    protection that has not yet been determined by the Refugee
    Protection Division;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a person on whom refugee protection has been
    conferred;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a person who is a member of the Convention refugees
    abroad class or a humanitarian-protected persons abroad class;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a properly accredited diplomat, consular officer,
    representative or official of a country other than Canada, of the
    United Nations or any of its agencies or of any intergovernmental
    organization of which Canada is a member, the members of the suite
    of such a person and the family members of such a person;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a member of the armed forces of a country that is a
    designated state for the purposes of the [Visiting Forces
    Act](/eng/acts/V-2), including a person who has been designated as a
    civilian component of that visiting force under paragraph 4(c) of
    that Act, and their family members;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a person who is a member of the clergy, a member of
    a religious order or a lay person who is to assist a congregation or
    a group in the achievement of its spiritual goals, if the duties to
    be performed by the person are to consist mainly of preaching
    doctrine, presiding at liturgical functions or spiritual
    counselling, and their family members;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} an officer of a foreign government sent, under an
    exchange agreement between Canada and one or more countries, to take
    up duties with a federal or provincial agency; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a family member of any of the following persons,
    namely,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a person who holds a study permit and is
      temporarily destitute, as described in paragraph 208(a),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a person whose work is designated under
      subparagraph 205(c)(i),

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a person whose work in Canada is for a Canadian
      religious or charitable organization, without remuneration,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a person whose presence in Canada is as a
      participant in a program sponsored by the Canadian International
      Development Agency, or

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a person whose presence in Canada is as a
      recipient of a Government of Canada scholarship or fellowship.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2011-222, s. 11
- SOR/2014-19, s. 6

#### [Restoration of Temporary Resident Status]{.HTitleText3} {#h-690307 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fee --- \$200

- **[[306]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-306 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  fee of \$200 is payable for processing an application under section
  182.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who holds an unexpired temporary resident
  permit is not required to pay the fee referred to in subsection (1).

#### [Fees for a Request under Section 25 of the Act or an Examination of Circumstances under Section 25.2 of the Act]{.HTitleText3} {#h-690313 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2015-95, s. 1

\]

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fees

**[[307]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-307 .sectionLabel}** The following fees are
payable for processing an application made in accordance with section 66
or for examining the circumstances under subsection 25.2(1) of the Act
of a foreign national who applies for permanent resident status or for a
permanent resident visa, if no fees are payable in respect of the same
applicant for processing an application to remain in Canada as a
permanent resident or an application for a permanent resident visa:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a principal applicant, \$550;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a family member of the principal
  applicant who is a spouse or common-law partner, \$550; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a family member of the principal
  applicant who is a dependent child, \$150.

#### [Permanent Resident Cards]{.HTitleText3} {#h-690323 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fee --- \$50

- **[[308]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-308 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  fee of \$50 is payable for processing an application made under
  paragraph 53(1)(b) for a permanent resident card.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Renewal or replacement fee

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A fee of \$50 is payable for processing an
  application for the renewal of a permanent resident card or for the
  replacement of a lost, stolen or destroyed permanent resident card.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Replacement due to error

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No fee is payable for the replacement of a permanent
  resident card containing an error that is not attributable to the
  permanent resident.

#### [Determination of Rehabilitation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-690331 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fees

**[[309]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-309 .sectionLabel}** The following fees are
payable for processing an application for a determination of
rehabilitation under paragraph 36(3)(c) of the Act:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a foreign national inadmissible on
  grounds of serious criminality under paragraph 36(1)(b) or (c) of the
  Act, \$1,000; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a foreign national inadmissible on
  grounds of criminality under paragraph 36(2)(b) or (c) of the Act,
  \$200.

#### [Authorization to Return to Canada]{.HTitleText3} {#h-690336 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fee --- \$400

**[[310]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-310 .sectionLabel}** A fee of \$400 is
payable for processing an application for authorization to return to
Canada under subsection 52(1) of the Act.

#### [Certification and Replacement of Immigration Document]{.HTitleText3} {#h-690339 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certification --- \$30

- **[[311]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-311 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  fee of \$30 is payable for processing an application for the
  certification of an immigration document, other than a permanent
  resident card, confirming the date on which a person became a
  permanent resident.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Replacement --- \$30

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A fee of \$30 is payable for processing an
  application to replace any immigration document, other than a
  permanent resident card, that is issued by the Department.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The following persons are not required to pay the fee
  referred to in subsection (1):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a federal, provincial or municipal government
    agency;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person in receipt of provincial social assistance
    payments; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a person in receipt of assistance under the
    Resettlement Assistance Program.

#### [After-hours Examination]{.HTitleText3} {#h-690350 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fee --- \$100

- **[[312]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-312 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following fees are payable for an examination for the purpose of
  entering Canada that is made outside the applicable service hours of
  the port of entry where the officer who conducts the examination is
  based:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a fee of \$100 for the first four hours of
    examination; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a fee of \$30 for each additional hour or part of
    an hour of examination.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The fees are payable at the time of the examination

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the transporter's vehicle carrying the person to
    be examined arrives unscheduled at a port of entry outside service
    hours, by the transporter; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, by the person who requests that
    the examination take place outside service hours.

#### [Alternative Means of Examination]{.HTitleText3} {#h-690360 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fee --- \$30

- **[[313]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-313 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  fee of \$30 is payable for processing an application to enroll in a
  program for an alternative means of examination that is administered
  solely by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The fee is payable in respect of each person to be
  examined by an alternative means referred to in subsection (1).

#### [Immigration Statistical Data]{.HTitleText3} {#h-690366 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fee for statistical data

- **[[314]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-314 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following fees are payable for processing an application for
  immigration statistical data that have not been published by the
  Department:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} \$100 for the first 10 minutes or less of access to
    the Department's database in order to respond to such an
    application; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} \$30 for each additional minute or less of such
    access.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following persons are not required to pay the
  fees referred to in subsection (1):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an employee of the Department; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an employee of the Data Development Division of the
    Department of Employment and Social Development.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The fee referred to in subsection (1)(b) is payable
  at the time the service is rendered.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2009-163, s. 17

- 2013, c. 40, s. 237
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Travel document

**[[315]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315 .sectionLabel}** A fee of \$50 is
payable for processing an application for a travel document issued under
subsection 31(3) of the Act.

#### [Services in Relation to Collection of Biometric Information]{.HTitleText3} {#h-690382 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2018-128, s. 8

\]
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fee --- \$85

- **[[315.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A fee of \$85 is payable for the
  provision of services in relation to the collection of biometric
  information under section 10.01 of the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemptions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following persons are not required to pay the fee
  referred to in subsection (1):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} with respect to an application referred to in
    paragraph 12.1(a), a person who applied for refugee protection
    outside Canada and their family members;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} with respect to a request referred to in paragraph
    12.1(f),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a person referred to in paragraph 12.2(1)(h), if
      the 48-hour period has elapsed and the person ceases to be exempt
      under that paragraph, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a person who, while they are in transit to
      Canada, ceases to be exempt under paragraph 190(1)(a) from the
      requirement for a temporary resident visa, if, during the first 48
      hours after they cease to be exempt from that requirement, they
      seek to enter and remain in Canada and are inadmissible to Canada
      for the sole reason that they do not have a temporary resident
      visa;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} with respect to an application referred to in
    paragraph 12.1(j), protected persons, within the meaning of
    subsection 95(2) of the Act, who are in Canada;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} with respect to an application or request referred
    to in paragraphs 12.1(d) to (i),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a properly accredited diplomat, consular officer,
      representative or official of a country other than Canada, of the
      United Nations or any of its agencies or of any intergovernmental
      organization of which Canada is a member,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a person who is a family member of a person
      referred to in subparagraph (i),

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a person who seeks to enter Canada in the
      course of official duties as a member of the suite of a person
      referred to in subparagraph (i),

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a member of a [[visiting
      force]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in section 2 of the
      [Visiting Forces Act](/eng/acts/V-2) and their family members,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a person whose work in Canada would create or
      maintain reciprocal employment of Canadian citizens or permanent
      residents of Canada in other countries and who is a family member
      of an officer of a foreign government sent, under an exchange
      agreement between Canada and one or more countries, to take up
      duties with a federal or provincial agency,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} a foreign national who belongs to the category
      of protected temporary residents and any family member included by
      that foreign national in the application or request,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} a person who makes a claim for refugee
      protection in Canada,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} a person referred to in section 12.9 who
      provides their biometric information under section 10.01 of the
      Act, before the end of the 10-year period referred to in paragraph
      12.7(1)(b), and

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} a person who is a member of the clergy, a member
      of a religious order or a lay person who is to assist a
      congregation or a group in the achievement of its spiritual goals,
      if the duties to be performed by the person are to consist mainly
      of spiritual counselling, preaching doctrine or presiding at
      liturgical functions, and their family members;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} with respect to an application or request referred
    to in paragraphs 12.1(d) to (g),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a person who is seeking to enter Canada for a
      period of less than 48 hours and who is

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} travelling by transporter's vehicle to a
        destination other than Canada, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} transiting through or stopping over in Canada
        for refuelling or for the continuation of their journey in
        another vehicle of a transporter, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a foreign national who is transiting through
      Canada as a passenger on a flight that, owing to an emergency or
      other unforeseen circumstances, makes an unscheduled stop in
      Canada;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} with respect to an application or request referred
    to in paragraphs 12.1(d) to (h),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a person seeking to enter Canada

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} for the purpose of attending a meeting hosted
        by the Government of Canada, an organization of the United
        Nations or the Organization of American States, as a
        participant,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} for the purpose of attending a meeting as a
        representative of the Organization of American States or the
        Caribbean Development Bank, or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} for the purpose of attending a meeting hosted
        by the Government of Canada, an organization of the United
        Nations or the Organization of American States, at the
        invitation of the Government of Canada,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a person seeking to enter Canada as a
      competitor, coach, judge, team official, medical staff member or
      member of a national or international sports organizing body
      participating in the Pan-American Games, when held in Canada, or
      as a performer participating in a festival associated with any of
      those Games,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a person seeking to work in Canada if the work
      is designated by the Minister under subparagraph 205(c)(i),

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a person seeking to work in Canada for a
      religious or charitable organization, without remuneration, or

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a person seeking to work in Canada under an
      agreement entered into with a country by Canada, or by or on
      behalf of a province, that provides for reciprocal employment
      opportunities of an artistic, cultural or educational nature;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} with respect to an application or request referred
    to in paragraphs 12.1(d) to (g) and (i), a person seeking to study
    in Canada under an agreement or arrangement entered into with a
    country by Canada that provides for reciprocal opportunities with
    respect to student exchange programs;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} with respect to an application referred to in
    paragraph 12.1(h), a person who holds a study permit and is
    temporarily destitute, as described in paragraph 208(a);

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} with respect to an application referred to in
    paragraphs 12.1(h) and (i),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the family members of a person in Canada whose
      claim for refugee protection has not yet been determined by the
      Refugee Protection Division,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the family members of a person in Canada who has
      been granted refugee protection, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the family members of a person who is a member
      of the Convention refugees abroad class or a member of a
      humanitarian-protected persons abroad class; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} with respect to an application referred to in
    paragraph 12.1(m), the person referred to in that paragraph.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum fee

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The total amount of fees payable under subsection (1)
  is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if an applicant and their family members make their
    applications or requests referred to in any of paragraphs
    12.1(a),(b) and (d) to (i) at the same time, \$170 for all
    applications and requests together; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if three or more persons who are part of the same
    group of performing artists or that group's staff make their
    applications for a work permit at the same time, \$255 for all
    applications together.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-73, s. 4
- SOR/2018-128, s. 9
:::

#### [Services in Relation to an Assessment from the Department of Employment and Social Development]{.HTitleText3} {#h-690432 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- 2013, c. 40, s. 237
- SOR/2015-147, s.1

\]
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fee --- \$1,000

- **[[315.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A fee of \$1,000 is payable for the
  provision of services in relation to an assessment from the Department
  of Employment and Social Development that is requested by an employer
  or group of employers under subsection 203(2) for each offer of
  employment in respect of which the request is made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The fee must be paid at the time the request is made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions --- agricultural work

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No fee is payable if the request is made in respect
  of an offer of employment that relates to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} work to be performed under an international
    agreement between Canada and one or more countries concerning
    seasonal agricultural workers; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other work in the primary agriculture sector.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Primary agriculture sector

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (3)(b), work in the
  primary agriculture sector means, subject to subsection (5), work that
  is performed within the boundaries of a farm, nursery or greenhouse
  and involves

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the operation of agricultural machinery;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the boarding, care, breeding, sanitation or other
    handling of animals, other than fish, for the purpose of obtaining
    animal products for market, or activities relating to the
    collection, handling and assessment of those products; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the planting, care, harvesting or preparation of
    crops, trees, sod or other plants for market.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusions

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Work in the primary agriculture sector does not
  include work involving

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the activities of agronomists or agricultural
    economists;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} landscape architecture;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2014-169, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the preparation of vegetable fibres for textile
    use;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} activities related to commercial hunting and
    trapping; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} veterinary activities.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- care for medical needs

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} No fee is payable if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the request is made in respect of an offer of
    employment that relates to work performed in a private household
    providing care to a person who is incapable of caring for themselves
    without assistance due to a physical or mental condition and who has
    a medical certificate from a medical practitioner entitled to
    practice medicine under the laws of a province, attesting to their
    incapacity; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the employer is the person receiving care or is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that person's spouse or common-law partner,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} that person's relative,![blank
      line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.Leader}

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the child or grandchild of that person's spouse
      or common-law partner,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} any person legally authorized to act on behalf
      of that person such as a guardian, a tutor, a curator or a person
      acting under a power of attorney or protective mandate, or

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} any person living with that person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- care for children

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} No fee is payable if the request is made in respect
  of an offer of employment that relates to work performed in a private
  household providing care to children under 13 years of age, by an
  employer who meets the following conditions:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the employer lives with the child; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the total annual gross income of the employer and,
    if applicable, of their spouse or common-law partner, living in the
    same private household does not exceed \$150,000 for the taxation
    year ending before the date of the request.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2013, c. 40, s. 237
- SOR/2013-149, s. 1
- SOR/2014-169, s. 1
- SOR/2015-147, s. 1
- SOR/2017-279, s. 1
:::

## [PART 19.1]{.HLabel1}[Information Sharing Between Countries]{.HTitleText1} {#h-690472 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Agreement between the Government of Canada and the Government of the United States of America for the Sharing of Visa and Immigration Information]{.HTitleText2} {#h-690473 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2024-257, s. 1(E)

\]
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

**[[315.21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.21 .sectionLabel}** The definitions
in this section apply in this Division.

[[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Agreement between the
    Government of Canada and the Government of the United States of
    America for the Sharing of Visa and Immigration Information, signed
    on December 13, 2012, as amended by the Agreement to Amend the
    Agreement between the Government of Canada and the Government of the
    United States of America for the Sharing of Visa and Immigration
    Information, signed on July 19, 2024. ([Accord]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[national of a third country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[national of a third country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person
    who is neither a Canadian citizen nor a citizen or national of the
    United States. ([ressortissant d'un pays tiers]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[parties]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[parties]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the parties to the Agreement,
    namely the Government of Canada and the Government of the United
    States. ([parties]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[query]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[query]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a request that triggers an
    electronic search process requiring minimal human
    intervention. ([requête]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-6, s. 1
- [SOR/2024-257, s. 2](#1499617-1506974){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1499617-1506974.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-315.21-20140129.html "Link to previous version of section 315.21"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purpose

**[[315.22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.22 .sectionLabel}** The purpose of
this Division is to implement the Agreement, the objectives of which, as
elaborated more specifically through its provisions, are to specify the
terms, relationships, responsibilities and conditions for the parties to
share information by means of a query to assist in the administration
and enforcement of the parties' respective immigration laws.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-6, s. 1
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authority to disclose information

- **[[315.23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.23
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may disclose
  information to the Government of the United States in making a query
  to that Government, or in response to a query made by that Government,
  only for the following purposes:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to support an examination following an application
    made by a national of a third country to obtain a permanent or
    temporary resident visa, an electronic travel authorization, a work
    permit, a study permit, protected person status or another
    immigration benefit under federal immigration legislation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to support an examination or determination as to
    whether a national of a third country is authorized or not
    authorized to travel to, enter or remain in Canada or the United
    States, as the case may be;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to ensure the accuracy and reliability of
    biographic data or other immigration-related data.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Response to query --- limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a response to a query made by the
  Government of the United States, the Minister may disclose information
  only in respect of any of the following nationals of a third country:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} those who were previously determined to be
    inadmissible under the Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} those who did not meet the requirements under the
    Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} those in respect of whom a fingerprint match is
    established; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} those who have been issued or denied a document
    required to enter Canada as a temporary or permanent resident.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-6, s. 1
- SOR/2017-53, s. 11
- SOR/2017-79, s. 1(F)
- [SOR/2024-257, s. 3](#1499619-1506976){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1499619-1506976.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-315.23-20170505.html "Link to previous version of section 315.23"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Necessary, relevant and proportionate
information

**[[315.24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.24 .sectionLabel}** Only information
that is necessary, relevant and proportionate to achieving the purposes
of this Division may be disclosed.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-6, s. 1
- SOR/2017-79, s. 2(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-315.24-20140129.html "Link to previous version of section 315.24"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information categories

- **[[315.25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.25
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Only information belonging to the
  following information categories in respect of a national of a third
  country may be disclosed:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} biographic data to be used for the purposes of
    identity verification, such as name, alias, date of birth, country
    of birth, gender, citizenship and travel document number;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} biometric data consisting of a photograph,
    fingerprints or both to be used for the purposes of identity
    verification; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a response to a query made by the
    Government of the United States, other immigration-related data,
    including the immigration status of the national of a third country,
    a previous determination that the national of a third country failed
    to meet the requirements of Canada's immigration laws, a previous
    admissibility decision or determination and data relevant to the
    admissibility of the national of a third country if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a match is established in respect of the
      biographic data referred to in paragraph (a); or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a match is established in respect of the
      biometric data referred to in paragraph (b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refugee claim --- limitation on
  disclosure of data

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a response to a query made by the
  Government of the United States in respect of a national of a third
  country making a refugee claim in the United States, only information
  related to an application for a permanent or temporary resident visa,
  a work or study permit or another immigration benefit under federal
  immigration legislation may be disclosed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Accuracy and reliability

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The disclosure must be made in a manner that ensures
  the accuracy and reliability of the information in question.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to disclose

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister determines that disclosing
  information in response to a query would be inconsistent with domestic
  law or detrimental to national sovereignty, national security, public
  policy, or other important national interests, the Minister may refuse
  to provide all or part of the available information or offer to
  provide all or part of the information subject to any terms and
  conditions that he or she may specify.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-6, s. 1
- SOR/2017-79, s. 3(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-315.25-20141001.html "Link to previous version of section 315.25"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Destruction of information

**[[315.26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.26 .sectionLabel}** Any information
collected by the Minister that is determined not to be relevant to a
query and that was not used for an administrative purpose, as defined in
section 3 of the [Privacy Act](/eng/acts/P-21), must be destroyed as
soon as feasible.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-6, s. 1
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Correction of previously disclosed
information

- **[[315.27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.27
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister is made aware that
  previously disclosed information is inaccurate, the Minister must
  notify the Government of the United States and provide correcting
  information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of correction and
  destruction of inaccurate information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister receives correcting information from
  the Government of the United States, the Minister must notify that
  Government once the necessary corrections have been made and, unless
  the information was used for an administrative purpose, as defined in
  section 3 of the [Privacy Act](/eng/acts/P-21), any inaccurate
  information and any information derived from that inaccurate
  information must be destroyed as soon as feasible.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Note to file

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If inaccurate information has been used for an
  administrative purpose, as defined in section 3 of the [Privacy
  Act](/eng/acts/P-21), a note must be placed in the file to that
  effect.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-6, s. 1
- SOR/2017-79, s. 4(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-315.27-20140129.html "Link to previous version of section 315.27"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Annex Regarding the Sharing of Information on Asylum and Refugee Status Claims to the Statement of Mutual Understanding on Information Sharing]{.HTitleText2} {#h-690533 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

**[[315.28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.28 .sectionLabel}** The definitions
in this section apply in this Division.

[[Asylum Annex]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Asylum Annex]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Annex Regarding the
    Sharing of Information on Asylum and Refugee Status Claims to the
    Statement of Mutual Understanding on Information Sharing, signed on
    behalf of Canada on August 22, 2003, as amended from time to
    time. ([Annexe sur l'asile]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[participants]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[participants]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the participants to the
    Asylum Annex, taking into account their successors, namely the
    Department of Citizenship and Immigration Canada, the Canada Border
    Services Agency and the Department of Homeland Security of the
    United States. ([participants]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[refugee status claimant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[refugee status claimant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who
    has made a claim for refugee protection in Canada or at a port of
    entry. ([demandeur du statut de réfugié]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-6, s. 1
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purpose

**[[315.29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.29 .sectionLabel}** The purpose of
this Division is to implement the Asylum Annex, the objectives of which,
as elaborated more specifically through its provisions, are to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} preserve and protect the participants' refugee status
  determination systems;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} enhance the participants' abilities to assist those
  who qualify for protection from persecution or from torture;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} support efforts to share responsibility between the
  participants in providing protection to qualified refugee status
  claimants;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} identify and prevent abuse of the participants'
  refugee status determination systems and citizenship and immigration
  laws; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} identify those who are excluded from protection or
  denied protection according to the Refugee Convention, as implemented
  in the participants' domestic legislation or whose refugee protection
  may be subject to termination, cancellation or revocation.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-6, s. 1
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authority to disclose information

**[[315.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.3 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may
only disclose information to the Department of Homeland Security of the
United States in respect of a refugee status claimant other than a
refugee status claimant who is alleging persecution in the United
States.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-6, s. 1
- SOR/2017-79, s. 5(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-315.3-20141001.html "Link to previous version of section 315.3"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Necessary, relevant and proportionate
information

**[[315.31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.31 .sectionLabel}** Only information
that is necessary, relevant and proportionate to achieving the purposes
of this Division may be disclosed.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-6, s. 1
- SOR/2017-79, s. 5(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-315.31-20141001.html "Link to previous version of section 315.31"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Method of disclosure

- **[[315.32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.32
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The disclosure of information must
  be made in accordance with article 6 of the Asylum Annex.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Accuracy and reliability

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The disclosure must be made in a manner that ensures
  the accuracy and reliability of the information in question.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-6, s. 1
- SOR/2017-79, s. 5(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-315.32-20141001.html "Link to previous version of section 315.32"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data elements to be disclosed

**[[315.33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.33 .sectionLabel}** Only information
belonging to the following information categories may be disclosed:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} information relating to the identity of a refugee
  status claimant;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} information relating to the processing of a refugee
  status claimant's claim;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} information relevant to a decision to deny a refugee
  status claimant access to or to exclude such a claimant from the
  protection of the refugee status determination system or to cease,
  vacate or nullify a refugee status claimant's refugee protection; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} information regarding the substance or history of a
  previous claim made by a refugee status claimant that will assist in
  determining a subsequent claim.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-6, s. 1
- SOR/2017-79, s. 6(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-315.33-20141001.html "Link to previous version of section 315.33"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Destruction of information

**[[315.34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.34 .sectionLabel}** Any information
collected by the Minister that is determined not to be relevant to the
purposes of this Division and that was not used for an administrative
purpose, as defined in section 3 of the [Privacy Act](/eng/acts/P-21),
must be destroyed as soon as feasible.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-6, s. 1
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Correction of previously disclosed
information

- **[[315.35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.35
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister is made aware that
  previously disclosed information is inaccurate, the Minister must
  notify the Department of Homeland Security of the United States and
  provide correcting information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of correction and
  destruction of inaccurate information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister receives correcting information from
  the Department of Homeland Security of the United States, the Minister
  must notify that Department once the necessary corrections have been
  made and, unless the information was used for an administrative
  purpose, as defined in section 3 of the [Privacy Act](/eng/acts/P-21),
  any inaccurate information and any information derived from that
  inaccurate information must be destroyed as soon as feasible.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Note to file

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If inaccurate information has been used for an
  administrative purpose, as defined in section 3 of the [Privacy
  Act](/eng/acts/P-21), a note must be placed in the file to that
  effect.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-6, s. 1
- SOR/2017-79, s. 7(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-315.35-20141001.html "Link to previous version of section 315.35"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Information Sharing Between the Government of Canada and the Governments of Australia, New Zealand and the United Kingdom]{.HTitleText2} {#h-690586 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

**[[315.36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.36 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Division.

[[national of a third country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[national of a third country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a foreign
    national other than a national or citizen of the country of the
    party making or receiving a query. ([ressortissant d'un pays
    tiers]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[party]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[party]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Minister; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any of the following government departments or
      their successors that have entered into an arrangement with the
      Department and the Canada Border Services Agency for the purpose
      of facilitating information sharing to assist in the
      administration and enforcement of their respective countries'
      immigration and citizenship laws:

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} Australia's Department of Immigration and
        Border Protection,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} New Zealand's Ministry of Business, Innovation
        and Employment,

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the United Kingdom's Home
        Office. ([partie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[query]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[query]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an automated request for
    information made by one party to another for the purposes of this
    Division. ([requête]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-79, s. 8
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purpose

**[[315.37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.37 .sectionLabel}** The purpose of
this Division is to define the parameters for the sharing of
information, by means of a query, between parties in support of the
administration and enforcement of Canada's laws in respect of
immigration and other parties' laws in respect of citizenship and
immigration.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-79, s. 8
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Making of query

**[[315.38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.38 .sectionLabel}** A query in
respect of a person must be made by submitting to another party either
the person's fingerprints accompanied by a unique transaction number or
the unique transaction number assigned to a previous query received in
respect of the person.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-79, s. 8
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure of information --- query or
response

**[[315.39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.39 .sectionLabel}** Disclosure in
relation to the making of or a response to a query is limited to
information that is necessary, relevant and proportionate to achieving
the purposes of this Division and must be made in a manner that ensures
the accuracy and reliability of the information.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-79, s. 8
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Making of query --- permitted purposes

- **[[315.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may make a query to
  another party only for the following purposes:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to support an examination or determination
    following an application or claim made by a national of a third
    country for a permanent or temporary resident visa, a work or study
    permit, protection, refugee protection or any other immigration
    benefit under Canada's immigration laws; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to support an examination or determination as to
    whether a national of a third country is authorized to travel to,
    enter or remain in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must not make a query to another party
  in respect of a person who has made a claim for refugee protection or
  an application for protection if the person has alleged persecution in
  that party's country.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-79, s. 8
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure of information --- permitted
purposes

- **[[315.41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.41
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may disclose
  information in response to a query made by another party only for the
  following purposes:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to support an examination or determination by that
    party following an application or claim made by a national of a
    third country for a visa or immigration-related permit, status or
    benefit;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to support an examination or determination by that
    party as to whether a national of a third country is authorized to
    travel to, enter or remain in that party's territory;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to support an examination or determination by that
    party following an application made by a national of a third country
    for citizenship; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} to support an examination or determination by that
    party following an application or claim made by a permanent resident
    of Canada for refugee status.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of disclosure

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may disclose to the requesting party the
  following information in relation to the national of a third country
  or the permanent resident of Canada:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} their biographic data, such as last name, first
    name, date of birth, gender and country of birth;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} their photograph; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} information in relation to the administration and
    enforcement of Canada's immigration laws, including the issuing
    country of the person's passport, their immigration status,
    information relevant to --- and any previous decision or
    determination relating to --- their admissibility, and any decision
    or determination relating to a claim for refugee protection or an
    application for protection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to disclose

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister determines that the disclosure of
  information to another party in response to a query would be
  inconsistent with domestic law or would be detrimental to national
  sovereignty, national security, public policy or other important
  national interests, the Minister must refuse to provide all or part of
  the available information.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-79, s. 8
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Correction of previously disclosed
information

- **[[315.42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.42
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister becomes aware that
  information that he or she disclosed in response to a query is
  inaccurate, the Minister must, as soon as feasible, notify the party
  to which he or she disclosed the information of the error and provide
  correcting information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Correction of inaccurate information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister receives information from another
  party correcting information previously disclosed by that party in
  response to a query, the Minister must, as soon as feasible, make the
  necessary correction and notify the other party once it has been made.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-79, s. 8
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention and disposal of information

- **[[315.43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315.43
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must retain or dispose
  of any information obtained in response to a query in accordance with
  the laws of Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Destruction of fingerprints

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must, immediately after completion of
  the information search triggered by the receipt of a query, destroy
  any fingerprints submitted as part of the query, regardless of whether
  a match is identified.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-79, s. 8
:::

## [PART 20]{.HLabel1}[Transitional Provisions]{.HTitleText1} {#h-690650 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-690651 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[316]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-316 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  definitions in this subsection apply in this Part.

  [[former Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[former Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Immigration
      Regulations, 1978, as enacted by Order in Council P.C. 1978-486
      dated February 23, 1978 and registered as SOR/78-172. ([ancien
      règlement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Humanitarian Designated Classes Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Humanitarian Designated Classes
      Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Humanitarian
      Designated Classes Regulations , as enacted by Order in Council
      P.C. 1997-477 dated April 8, 1997 and registered as
      SOR/97-183. ([Règlement sur les catégories d'immigrants précisées
      pour des motifs d'ordre humanitaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Immigration Act Fees Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Immigration Act Fees Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      Immigration Act Fees Regulations, as enacted by Order in Council
      P.C. 1996-2003 dated December 19, 1996 and registered as
      SOR/97-22. ([Règlement sur les prix à payer --- Loi sur
      l'immigration]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Refugee Claimants Designated Class Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Refugee Claimants Designated Class
      Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Refugee Claimants
      Designated Class Regulations, as enacted by Order in Council P.C.
      1989-2517 dated December 21, 1989 and registered as
      SOR/90-40. ([Règlement sur la catégorie admissible de demandeurs
      du statut de réfugié]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- Interpretation --- [[former Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, in this Part [[former
  Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in section 187 of
  the [Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation --- [Immigration and
  Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A reference in this Part to the [Immigration and
  Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) includes the regulations and
  rules made under it.

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[General Provisions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-690663 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decisions and orders made under former
Act

- **[[317]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-317 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  decision or order made under the former Act that is in effect
  immediately before the coming into force of this section continues in
  effect after that coming into force.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documents issued under former Act

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A document, including a visa, that is issued under
  the former Act and is valid immediately before the coming into force
  of this section continues to be valid after that coming into force.

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Enforcement]{.HTitleText2} {#h-690669 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms and conditions

**[[318]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-318 .sectionLabel}** Terms and conditions
imposed under the former Act become conditions imposed under the
[Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal order

- **[[319]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-319
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), a removal
  order made under the former Act that was unexecuted on the coming into
  force of this section continues in force and is subject to the
  provisions of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
  Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Stay of removal

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The execution of a removal order that had been stayed
  on the coming into force of this section under paragraphs 49(1)(c)
  to (f) of the former Act continues to be stayed until the earliest of
  the events described in paragraphs 231(1)(a) to (e).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not apply if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the subject of the removal order was determined by
    the Convention Refugee Determination Division not to have a credible
    basis for their claim; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the subject of the removal order

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is subject to a removal order because they are
      inadmissible on grounds of serious criminality, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} resides or sojourns in the United States or St.
      Pierre and Miquelon and is the subject of a report prepared under
      subsection 44(1) of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
      Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) on their entry into Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditional removal order

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A conditional removal order made under the former Act
  continues in force and is subject to subsection 49(2) of the
  [Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Executed removal order

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Section 52 of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
  Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) applies to a person who immediately before the
  coming into force of this section was outside Canada after a removal
  order was executed against them.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inadmissibility --- security grounds

- **[[320]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-320 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person is inadmissible under the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
  Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) on security grounds if, on the coming into force
  of this section, the person had been determined to be a member of an
  inadmissible class described in paragraph 19(1)(e), (f), (g) or (k) of
  the former Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Violating human or international rights

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person is inadmissible under the [Immigration and
  Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) on grounds of violating human
  or international rights if, on the coming into force of this section,
  the person had been determined to be a member of an inadmissible class
  described in paragraph 19(1)(j) or (l) of the former Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Paragraph 19(1)(l) of the former Act

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, an opinion of the Minister
  under paragraph 19(1)(l) of the former Act continues as an opinion of
  the Minister under paragraph 35(1)(b) of the [Immigration and Refugee
  Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Serious criminality

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person is inadmissible under the [Immigration and
  Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) on grounds of serious
  criminality if, on the coming into force of this section, the person
  had been determined to be a member of an inadmissible class described
  in paragraph 19(1)(c) or (c.1) of the former Act or had been
  determined to be inadmissible on the basis of paragraph 27(1)(a.1) of
  the former Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Criminality

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A person is inadmissible under the [Immigration and
  Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) on grounds of criminality if,
  on the coming into force of this section, the person had been
  determined to be a member of an inadmissible class described in
  paragraph 19(2)(a), (a.1) or (b) of the former Act, or had been
  determined to be inadmissible on the basis of paragraph 27(1)(a.2) or
  (a.3) or (2)(d) of the former Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Paragraph 27(1)(d) of former Act

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A person who on the coming into force of this section
  had been determined to be inadmissible on the basis of paragraph
  27(1)(d) of the former Act is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} inadmissible under the [Immigration and Refugee
    Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) on grounds of serious criminality
    if the person was convicted of an offence and a term of imprisonment
    of more than six months has been imposed or a term of imprisonment
    of 10 years or more could have been imposed; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} inadmissible under the [Immigration and Refugee
    Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) on grounds of criminality if the
    offence was punishable by a maximum term of imprisonment of five
    years or more but less than 10 years.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Organized crime

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A person is inadmissible under the [Immigration and
  Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) on grounds of organized
  criminality if, on the coming into force of this section, the person
  had been determined to be a member of an inadmissible class described
  in paragraph 19(1)(c.2) or subparagraph 19(1)(d)(ii) of the former
  Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Health grounds

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} A person --- other than an applicant described in
  section 7 of the former Regulations or section 4 of the Humanitarian
  Designated Classes Regulations who made an application for admission
  under the former Act --- is inadmissible under the Immigration and
  Refugee Protection Act on health grounds if, on the coming into force
  of this section, the person had been determined to be a member of the
  inadmissible class described in paragraph 19(1)(a) of the former Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Financial reasons

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} A person is inadmissible under the [Immigration and
  Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) for financial reasons if, on
  the coming into force of this section, the person had been determined
  to be a member of the inadmissible class described in paragraph
  19(1)(b) of the former Act or had been determined to be inadmissible
  on the basis of paragraph 27(1)(f) or (2)(l) of the former Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Misrepresentation

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} A person is inadmissible under the [Immigration and
  Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) for misrepresentation if, on
  the coming into force of this section, the person had been determined
  to be inadmissible on the basis of paragraph 27(1)(e) or (2)(g) or (i)
  of the former Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Failing to comply

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} A person is inadmissible under the [Immigration and
  Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) for failing to comply with
  that Act if, on the coming into force of this section, the person had
  been determined to be a member of an inadmissible class described in
  paragraph 19(1)(h) or (i) or (2)(c) or (d) of the former Act, or had
  been determined to be inadmissible on the basis of paragraph 27(1)(b)
  or (2)(b), (e), (f), (h), (i) or (k) of the former Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-167, s. 75
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reports

- **[[321]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-321 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  report made under section 20 or 27 of the former Act is a report under
  subsection 44(1) of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
  Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Equivalency

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (1)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} inadmissibility as a member of a class described in
    paragraph 19(1)(e), (f), (g) or (k) of the former Act is
    inadmissibility on security grounds under the [Immigration and
    Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} inadmissibility as a member of a class described in
    paragraph 19(1)(j) or (l) of the former Act is inadmissibility under
    the [Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) on
    grounds of violating human or international rights;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} inadmissibility as a member of a class described in
    paragraph 19(1)(c) or (c.1) of the former Act or inadmissibility on
    the basis of paragraph 27(1)(a.1) of the former Act is
    inadmissibility under the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
    Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) on grounds of serious criminality;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} inadmissibility as a member of a class described in
    paragraph 19(2)(a), (a.1) or (b) of the former Act or
    inadmissibility on the basis of paragraph 27(1)(a.2) or (a.3) or
    (2)(d) of the former Act is inadmissibility under the [Immigration
    and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) on grounds of
    criminality;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} inadmissibility on the basis of paragraph 27(1)(d)
    of the former Act is inadmissibility under the [Immigration and
    Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) on grounds of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} serious criminality, if the person was convicted
      of an offence and a term of imprisonment of more than six months
      has been imposed or a term of imprisonment of 10 years or more
      could have been imposed, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} criminality if the offence was punishable by a
      maximum term of imprisonment of five years or more but less than
      10 years;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} inadmissibility as a member of a class described in
    paragraph 19(1)(c.2) or subparagraph 19(1)(d)(ii) of the former Act
    is inadmissibility under the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
    Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) on grounds of organized criminality;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} inadmissibility as a member of the inadmissible
    class described in paragraph 19(1)(a) of the former Act --- other
    than an applicant described in section 7 of the former Regulations
    or section 4 of the Humanitarian Designated Classes Regulations who
    made an application for admission under the former Act --- is
    inadmissibility under the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
    Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5)on health grounds if, on the coming into force
    of this section, the person had been determined to be a member of
    the inadmissible class described in paragraph 19(1)(a) of the former
    Act;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} inadmissibility as a member of a class described in
    paragraph 19(1)(b) of the former Act or inadmissibility on the basis
    of paragraph 27(1)(f) or (2)(l) of the former Act is inadmissibility
    under the [Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5)
    for financial reasons;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} inadmissibility on the basis of paragraph 27(1)(e)
    or (2)(g) or (i) of the former Act is inadmissibility under the
    [Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) for
    misrepresentation; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} inadmissibility as a member of a class described in
    paragraph 19(1)(h) or (i) or (2)(c) or (d) of the former Act or
    inadmissibility on the basis of paragraph 27(1)(b) or (2)(b), (e),
    (f), (h), (i) or (k) of the former Act is inadmissibility under the
    [Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) for
    failing to comply with the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reports forwarded to a senior
  immigration officer

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A report that was forwarded to a senior immigration
  officer under the former Act and in respect of which a decision has
  not been made on the coming into force of this section is a report
  transmitted to the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inquiry

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The causing by a senior immigration officer of an
  inquiry to be held under the former Act is the referring by the
  Minister of a report to the Immigration Division under subsection
  44(2) of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5)
  unless that subsection allows the Minister to make a removal order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No substantive evidence

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If no substantive evidence was adduced before the
  Adjudication Division, the causing by a senior immigration officer of
  an inquiry to be held under the former Act is, if subsection 44(2) of
  the [Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) allows
  the Minister to make a removal order, a report on the basis of which
  the Minister may make a removal order.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Detention

- **[[322]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-322 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  first review of reasons, after the coming into force of this section,
  for the continued detention of a person detained under the former Act
  shall be made in accordance with the provisions of the former Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of detention

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the review referred to in subsection (1) was the
  first review in respect of a person's detention, the period of
  detention at the end of which that review was made shall be considered
  the period referred to in subsection 57(1) of the [Immigration and
  Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsequent review

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a review of reasons for continued detention
  follows the review referred to in subsection (1), that review shall be
  made under the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
  Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order issued by Deputy Minister

**[[323]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-323 .sectionLabel}** An order issued by a
Deputy Minister under subsection 105(1) of the former Act continues in
force and the review of reasons for continued detention shall be made
under the [Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Release

**[[324]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-324 .sectionLabel}** A release from
detention under the former Act is the ordering of release from detention
under the [Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) and
any terms and conditions imposed under the former Act become conditions
imposed under the Immigration and Refugee Protection Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Warrants

- **[[325]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-325 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  warrant for arrest and detention made under the former Act is a
  warrant for arrest and detention made under the [Immigration and
  Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Detention orders

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An order for the detention of a person made under the
  former Act is an order to detain made under the [Immigration and
  Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Danger to the public

- **[[326]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-326 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  claim to be a Convention refugee made by a person described in
  subparagraph 19(1)(c.1)(i) of the former Act in respect of whom the
  Minister was of the opinion under subparagraph 46.01(1)(e)(i) of the
  former Act that the person constitutes a danger to the public in
  Canada is deemed, if no determination was made by a senior immigration
  officer under section 45 of the former Act, to be a claim for refugee
  protection made by a person described in paragraph 101(2)(b) of the
  [Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) who is
  inadmissible and in respect of whom the Minister is of the opinion
  that the person is a danger to the public.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeals

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person in respect of whom subsection 70(5) or
  paragraph 77(3.01)(b) of the former Act applied on the coming into
  force of this section is a person in respect of whom subsection 64(1)
  of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5)
  applies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal not prohibited

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person whose removal on the coming into force of
  this section was allowed by the application of paragraphs 53(1)(a)
  to (d) of the former Act is a person referred to in subsection 115(2)
  of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificates

**[[327]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-327 .sectionLabel}** A certificate
determined to be reasonable under paragraph 40.1(4)(d) of the former Act
is deemed to be a certificate determined to be reasonable under
subsection 80(1) of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permanent residents

- **[[328]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-328 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who was a permanent resident immediately before the coming into
