  force of this section is a permanent resident under the [Immigration
  and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Returning resident permit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any period spent outside Canada within the five years
  preceding the coming into force of this section by a permanent
  resident holding a returning resident permit is considered to be a
  period spent in Canada for the purpose of satisfying the residency
  obligation under section 28 of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
  Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) if that period is included in the five-year
  period referred to in that section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Returning resident permit

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any period spent outside Canada within the two years
  immediately following the coming into force of this section by a
  permanent resident holding a returning resident permit is considered
  to be a period spent in Canada for the purpose of satisfying the
  residency obligation under section 28 of the [Immigration and Refugee
  Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) if that period is included in the
  five-year period referred to in that section.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Visitors and permit holders

- **[[329]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-329 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  of the following persons who were in Canada immediately before the
  coming into force of this section are temporary residents under the
  [Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) and are
  subject to its provisions:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a visitor under the former Act; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person issued a permit under section 37 of the
    former Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permits

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A permit issued by the Minister under section 37 of
  the former Act is deemed to be a temporary resident permit referred to
  in section 24 of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
  Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Examination

**[[330]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-330 .sectionLabel}** Any of the following
persons who were in Canada immediately before the coming into force of
this section are deemed to have been authorized under section 23 of the
[Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) to enter
Canada:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person in respect of whom an examination remains
  incomplete and whose examination was adjourned and referred to another
  immigration officer for completion under subsection 12(3) of the
  former Act;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person in respect of whom an examination remains
  incomplete and whose examination was deferred under paragraph 13(1)(a)
  of the former Act;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a person in respect of whom an examination remains
  incomplete and who was authorized to come into Canada for further
  examination under paragraph 14(2)(b) of the former Act;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} a person in respect of whom an examination remains
  incomplete and who was authorized to come into Canada for further
  examination under paragraph 23(1)(b) of the former Act; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} a person who has made a claim to be a Convention
  refugee in respect of which a determination of eligibility was not
  made before the coming into force of this section.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Performance bonds and security deposits

**[[331]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-331 .sectionLabel}** A performance bond
posted or security deposited under the former Act that remains posted or
deposited immediately before the coming into force of this section
continues as a deposit or a guarantee under the [Immigration and Refugee
Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) and is governed by its provisions.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Seizures

**[[332]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-332 .sectionLabel}** A thing seized under
the former Act continues to be seized on the coming into force of this
section, and the seizure is governed by the provisions of the
[Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debts

**[[333]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-333 .sectionLabel}** Any debt under
subsection 118(3) of the former Act continues as a debt on the coming
into force of this section and is governed by the provisions of the
[Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

### [DIVISION 4]{.HLabel2}[Refugee and Humanitarian Resettlement Program]{.HTitleText2} {#h-690789 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applications for protection abroad

**[[334]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-334 .sectionLabel}** With the exception of
subsection 140.3(1) of these Regulations, the [Immigration and Refugee
Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) applies to an applicant described in
section 7 of the former Regulations or section 4 of the Humanitarian
Designated Classes Regulations, who made an application for admission
under the former Act if the application is pending on the day on which
this section comes into force and no visa has been issued to the
applicant.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-225, s. 10
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-334-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 334"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Family member

**[[335]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-335 .sectionLabel}** An applicant described
in section 7 of the former Regulations or section 4 of the Humanitarian
Designated Classes Regulations who made an application for admission
under the former Act that has not been refused may add to their
application at any time prior to their departure for Canada a person
included in the definition [[family member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in
subsection 1(3).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sponsorship agreements

**[[336]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-336 .sectionLabel}** A sponsorship agreement
with the Minister made under the former Act and former Regulations does
not cease to have effect for the sole reason of section 152 coming into
force.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sponsors

- **[[337]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-337
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (3),
  a sponsor who made an undertaking within the meaning of paragraph (b)
  of the definition [[undertaking]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection
  2(1) of the former Regulations, or of the definition
  [[undertaking]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection 1(1) of the
  Humanitarian Designated Classes Regulations, and in respect of whom an
  immigration officer was satisfied that the requirements of paragraph
  7.1(2)(d) or 5(2)(d) of those Regulations, respectively, were met is
  deemed to be a sponsor whose application has been approved by an
  officer under section 154.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional persons sponsored

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a sponsor who
  requests that a person be added to their undertaking.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ineligibility to sponsor

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a sponsor who is
  ineligible to be a party to a sponsorship under section 156.

### [DIVISION 5]{.HLabel2}[Refugee Protection]{.HTitleText2} {#h-690805 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refugee protection

**[[338]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-338 .sectionLabel}** Refugee protection is
conferred under the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) on a person who

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} has been determined in Canada before the coming into
  force of this section to be a Convention refugee and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} no determination was made to vacate that
    determination, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} no determination was made that the person ceased
    to be a Convention refugee;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} as an applicant or an accompanying dependant was
  granted landing before the coming into force of this section after
  being issued a visa under

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} section 7 of the former Regulations, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} section 4 of the Humanitarian Designated Classes
    Regulations; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} was determined to be a member of the
  post-determination refugee claimants in Canada class before the coming
  into force of this section and was granted landing under section 11.4
  of the former Regulations or who becomes a permanent resident under
  subsection 21(2) of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
  Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rejection of a claim for refugee
protection

**[[339]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-339 .sectionLabel}** A determination made in
Canada before the coming into force of this section that a person is not
a Convention refugee is deemed to be a claim for refugee protection
rejected by the Board.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ineligibility

**[[340]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-340 .sectionLabel}** A determination made
before the coming into force of this section that a person is not
eligible to have their Convention refugee claim determined by the
Convention Refugee Determination Division is deemed to be a
determination that the claim is ineligible to be referred to the Refugee
Protection Division.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Withdrawal and abandonment

**[[341]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-341 .sectionLabel}** A claim to be a
Convention refugee that was withdrawn or declared to be abandoned before
the coming into force of this section is deemed to be a claim determined
to be withdrawn or abandoned under the [Immigration and Refugee
Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligibility

**[[342]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-342 .sectionLabel}** A claim made in Canada
to be a Convention refugee in respect of which a determination of
eligibility was not made before the coming into force of this section is
deemed to be a claim for refugee protection made in Canada that is
received on the day on which this section comes into force.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Redetermination of eligibility

**[[343]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-343 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 191
of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5), a
claim of a person who was determined eligible before the coming into
force of this section to have a claim to be a Convention refugee
determined by the Convention Refugee Determination Division, and in
respect of which no determination was made by that Division, is a claim
that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} is referred under the [Immigration and Refugee
  Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) to the Refugee Protection Division
  unless an officer gives notice under subsection 104(1) of that Act;
  and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} is subject to the provisions of that Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cessation of refugee protection

**[[344]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-344 .sectionLabel}** A determination made in
Canada before the coming into force of this section that a person has
ceased to be a Convention refugee is deemed to be a determination by the
Board that refugee protection has ceased.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vacation

**[[345]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-345 .sectionLabel}** A decision made in
Canada before the coming into force of this section to approve an
application to reconsider and vacate a determination that a person is a
Convention refugee is deemed to be a determination by the Board to
vacate a decision to allow a claim for refugee protection.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Post-determination refugee claimants in
Canada class

- **[[346]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-346 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application for landing as a member of the post-determination refugee
  claimants in Canada class in respect of which no determination of
  whether the applicant is a member of that class was made before the
  coming into force of this section is an application for protection
  under sections 112 to 114 of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
  Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) and those sections apply to the application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification re additional submissions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before a decision is made on the application, the
  applicant shall be notified that they may make additional submissions
  in support of their application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A decision on the application shall not be made until
  30 days after notification is given to the applicant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Giving notification

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Notification is given

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} when it is given by hand to the applicant; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if it is sent by mail, seven days after the day on
    which it was sent to the applicant at the last address provided by
    them to the Department.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Stay of removal

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the execution of a removal
  order made under the former Act against an applicant referred to in
  subsection (1) is stayed, and the stay is effective until the earliest
  of the applicable events described in section 232 occurs.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-167, s. 76
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for landing --- Convention
refugees

- **[[347]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-347 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  landing was not granted before the coming into force of this section,
  an application for landing submitted under section 46.04 of the former
  Act is an application to remain in Canada as a permanent resident
  under subsection 21(2) of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
  Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for landing ---
  undocumented Convention refugee in Canada class

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If landing was not granted before June 28, 2002, an
  application for landing as a member of the undocumented Convention
  refugee in Canada class is an application to remain in Canada as a
  permanent resident under subsection 21(2) of the [Immigration and
  Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for landing ---
  post-determination refugee claimants in Canada class

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If landing was not granted before the coming into
  force of this section, an application for landing submitted by a
  person pursuant to a determination that the person is a member of the
  post-determination refugee claimants in Canada class is an application
  to remain in Canada as a permanent resident under subsection 21(2) of
  the [Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-154, s. 16
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-347-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 347"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 6]{.HLabel2}[Court Proceedings]{.HTitleText2} {#h-690855 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Judicial review

- **[[348]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-348 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  the coming into force of this section, any application for leave to
  commence an application for judicial review and any application for
  judicial review or appeal from an application that was brought under
  the former Act that is pending before the Federal Court or the Supreme
  Court of Canada is deemed to have been commenced under Division 8 of
  Part 1 of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
  Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) and is governed by the provisions of that
  Division and section 87.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for non-disclosure

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On the coming into force of this section, any
  application under subsection 82.1(10) of the former Act that is
  pending before the Federal Court is deemed to be an application under
  section 87 of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
  Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where no leave required

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), an application for judicial
  review that was not subject to the requirement of an application for
  leave under the former Act and was pending on the coming into force of
  this section does not require such an application under the
  [Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Judicial review after coming into force

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Any judicial review proceeding brought in respect of
  any decision or order made or any matter arising under the former Act
  after the coming into force of this section is governed by Division 8
  of Part 1 and section 87 of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
  Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time for filing

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A person in respect of whom the 30-day period
  provided by section 18.1 of the [Federal Courts Act](/eng/acts/F-7)
  for making an application for judicial review from a decision or
  matter referred to in subsection 82.1(2) of the former Act has not
  elapsed on the coming into force of this section and who has not made
  such an application has 60 days from the coming into force of this
  section to file an application for leave under section 72 of the
  Immigration and Refugee Protection Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Validity or lawfulness of a decision or
  act

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The validity or lawfulness of a decision or act made
  under the former Act that is the subject of a judicial review
  procedure or appeal referred to in subsection (1) is determined in
  accordance with the provisions of the former Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 8, s. 182
- SOR/2009-163, s. 18(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-348-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 348"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other court proceedings

**[[349]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-349 .sectionLabel}** On the coming into
force of this section, an appeal made under section 102.17 of the former
Act or an application for an order made under section 102.2 of the
former Act that is pending remains governed by the provisions of the
former Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decisions referred back

- **[[350]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-350
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (3),
  if a decision or an act of the Minister or an immigration officer
  under the former Act is referred back by the Federal Court or Supreme
  Court of Canada for determination and the determination is not made
  before this section comes into force, the determination shall be made
  in accordance with the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
  Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decisions or acts not provided for by
  [Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the decision or act referred to in subsection (1)
  was made under paragraph 46.01(1)(e), subsection 70(5) or paragraph
  77(3.01)(b) of the former Act and the [Immigration and Refugee
  Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) makes no provision for the decision
  or act, no determination shall be made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Skilled workers and self-employed
  persons

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a decision or an act of the Minister or an
  immigration officer under the former Act in respect of a person
  described in subparagraph 9(1)(b)(i) or paragraph 10(1)(b) of the
  former Regulations is referred back by the Federal Court or Supreme
  Court of Canada for determination and the determination is not made
  before December 1, 2003, the determination shall be made in accordance
  with subsections 361(4) and (5.2) of these Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Investors, entrepreneurs and provincial
  nominees

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a decision or an act of the Minister or an
  immigration officer under the former Act in respect of a person
  described in subparagraph 9(1)(b)(ii) or (iii) of the former
  Regulations is referred back by the Federal Court or Supreme Court of
  Canada for determination and the determination is not made before
  December 1, 2003, the determination shall be made in accordance with
  subsections 361(5), (5.1) and (6) of these Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Immigration Appeal Division decisions

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a decision of the Immigration Appeal Division made
  under the former Act is referred back by the Federal Court or Supreme
  Court of Canada for determination and the determination is not made
  before the date of the coming into force of this section, the
  Immigration Appeal Division shall dispose of the matter in accordance
  with the former Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjudication Division decisions

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a decision of the Adjudication Division made under
  the former Act is referred back by the Federal Court or Supreme Court
  of Canada for determination and the determination is not made before
  the date of the coming into force of this section, the Immigration
  Division shall dispose of the matter in accordance with the
  [Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2003-383, s. 7
:::

### [DIVISION 7]{.HLabel2}[Undertakings]{.HTitleText2} {#h-690888 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of the Act to existing
undertakings

- **[[351]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-351
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), an
  undertaking referred to in section 118 of the former Act that was
  given before the day on which this section comes into force is
  governed by the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
  Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recovery of social assistance payments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Payments that are made to or for the benefit of a
  person as social assistance or as financial assistance in the form of
  funds from a government resettlement assistance program referred to in
  subparagraph 139(1)(f)(ii) as a result of the breach of an
  undertaking, within the meaning of subparagraph (a)(ii) or
  paragraph (b) of the definition "undertaking" in subsection 2(1) of
  the former Regulations or of the definition "undertaking" in
  subsection 1(1) of the Humanitarian Designated Classes Regulations,
  that was given before the day on which this section comes into force,
  may be recovered from the person or organization that gave the
  undertaking as a debt due to Her Majesty in right of Canada or in
  right of a province.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duration

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the duration of an undertaking
  referred to in section 118 of the former Act that was given to the
  Minister before the day on which this section comes into force is not
  affected by these Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duration and terms

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, if an immigrant visa was
  issued to a person described in section 7 of the former Regulations or
  section 4 of the Humanitarian Designated Classes Regulations before
  the day on which this section comes into force, the duration and terms
  of an undertaking, referred to in section 118 of the former Act,
  relating to that person are not affected by these Regulations.

### [DIVISION 8]{.HLabel2}[Non-Accompanying Family Members]{.HTitleText2} {#h-690898 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Not required to be included

**[[352]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-352 .sectionLabel}** A person is not
required to include in an application a non-accompanying common-law
partner or a non-accompanying child who is not a dependent son or a
dependent daughter within the meaning of subsection 2(1) of the former
Regulations and is a dependent child as defined in section 2 of these
Regulations if the application was made under the former Act before the
day on which this section comes into force.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements not applicable

**[[353]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-353 .sectionLabel}** If a person has made an
application under the former Act before the day on which this section
comes into force, the following provisions do not apply to the person in
respect of any of their non-accompanying dependent children, referred to
in section 352, or their non-accompanying common-law partner:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 70(1)(e);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} subparagraph 72(1)(e)(i); and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 108(1)(a).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements not applicable

**[[354]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-354 .sectionLabel}** If a person makes an
application under the former Act before June 28, 2002, their
non-accompanying dependent children, referred to in section 352, and
their non-accompanying common-law partner shall not, for the purposes of
that application, be considered inadmissible non-accompanying family
members, referred to in paragraph 42(1)(a) of the [Immigration and
Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5), and are not subject to the
requirements of paragraph 16(2)(b) of the [Immigration and Refugee
Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) or 51(b) of these Regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-154, s. 17
- SOR/2014-269, s. 6
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-354-20120815.html "Link to previous version of section 354"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Family members not excluded from family
class

**[[355]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-355 .sectionLabel}** If a person who made an
application under the former Act before June 28, 2002 sponsors a
non-accompanying dependent child, referred to in section 352, who makes
an application as a member of the family class or the spouse or
common-law partner in Canada class, or sponsors a non-accompanying
common-law partner who makes such an application, paragraph 117(9)(d)
does not apply in respect of that dependent child or common-law partner.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-167, s. 77
:::

### [DIVISION 9]{.HLabel2}[Fiancés]{.HTitleText2} {#h-690914 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pending applications

**[[356]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-356 .sectionLabel}** If a person referred to
in paragraph (f) of the definition [[member of the family
class]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 2(1) of the former
Regulations made an application under those Regulations for a permanent
resident visa, or their sponsor submitted a sponsorship application
under those Regulations, before June 28, 2002, the person's application
or the sponsorship application, as the case may be, is governed by the
former Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-167, s. 78
:::

### [DIVISION 10]{.HLabel2}[Fees]{.HTitleText2} {#h-690919 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Remission --- right of landing fee

**[[357]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-357 .sectionLabel}** The fee set out in
column III of item 19 of the schedule to the Immigration Act Fees
Regulations is remitted and shall be repaid by the Minister to the
person who paid it if the fee is paid in respect of a person before they
become a permanent resident under the [Immigration and Refugee
Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) and the person, at the time they made
an application for landing under the former Regulations, was

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a member of the family class and 19 years of age or
  older and, on the day on which this section comes into force, is a
  foreign national referred to in paragraph 117(1)(b) or (e) of these
  Regulations; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} an accompanying dependant of an immigrant, within the
  meaning of subsection 2(1) of the former Regulations, 19 years of age
  or older and not a spouse of the principal applicant.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fees to be reapplied

- **[[358]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-358 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  fee paid for processing an application in respect of which no decision
  has been made before the day on which this section comes into force or
  an application that has been refused but the refusal has not been
  communicated to the applicant before that day shall be applied to the
  cost of completing the processing of the application under the
  [Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of an
  application for a returning resident permit.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Remission --- returning resident permit
fee

**[[359]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-359 .sectionLabel}** The fee for a returning
resident permit set out in column III of item 3 of the schedule to the
Immigration Act Fees Regulations is remitted if, before the day on which
this section comes into force, no decision has been made on the
application for the permit or the application has been refused and the
refusal has not been communicated to the applicant. If the fee is
remitted, it shall be repaid by the Minister to the person who paid it.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Remission --- fee for review of family
business employment offer

**[[360]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-360 .sectionLabel}** The fee set out in
column III of item 16 of the schedule to the Immigration Act Fees
Regulations for the review of an offer of employment made to an
applicant in respect of a family business is remitted if, before the day
on which this section comes into force, no determination has been made
on the family business application or the application has been refused
and the refusal has not been communicated to the applicant. If the fee
is remitted, it shall be repaid by the Minister to the person who paid
it.

### [DIVISION 11]{.HLabel2}[Economic Classes]{.HTitleText2} {#h-690933 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Equivalent assessment

- **[[361]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-361 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If,
  before the day on which this section comes into force, a foreign
  national referred to in subsection (2) has been assessed by a visa
  officer and awarded the number of units of assessment required by the
  former Regulations, that assessment is, for the purpose of these
  Regulations, an award of points equal or superior to the minimum
  number of points required of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a skilled worker, in the case of a foreign national
    described in paragraph (2)(a);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an investor, in the case of a foreign national
    described in paragraph (2)(b);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an entrepreneur, in the case of a foreign national
    described in paragraph (2)(c); or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a self-employed person, in the case of a foreign
    national described in paragraph (2)(d).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applicant for immigrant visa

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) applies in respect of a foreign
  national who submitted an application under the former Regulations, as
  one of the following, for an immigrant visa that is pending
  immediately before the day on which this section comes into force:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person described in subparagraph 9(1)(b)(i) or
    paragraph 10(1)(b) of the former Regulations;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an investor; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an entrepreneur.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application before January 1, 2002

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} During the period beginning on the day on which this
  section comes into force and ending on March 31, 2003, units of
  assessment shall be awarded to a foreign national, in accordance with
  the former Regulations, if the foreign national is an immigrant who,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is referred to in subsection 8(1) of those
    Regulations, other than a provincial nominee, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} before January 1, 2002, made an application for an
    immigrant visa under those Regulations that is still pending on the
    day on which this section comes into force and has not, before that
    day, been awarded units of assessment under those Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pending applications --- skilled
  workers

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Beginning on December 1, 2003, a foreign national who
  is an immigrant who made an application under the former Regulations
  before January 1, 2002 for an immigrant visa as a person described in
  subparagraph 9(1)(b)(i) or paragraph 10(1)(b) of those Regulations,
  other than a self-employed person within the meaning of subsection
  2(1) of those Regulations, and whose application is still pending on
  December 1, 2003 and who has not, before that day, been awarded units
  of assessment under those Regulations must, in order to become a
  permanent resident as a member of the federal skilled worker class,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be awarded at least the minimum number of units of
    assessment required by those Regulations for a person described in
    subparagraph 9(1)(b)(i) or paragraph 10(1)(b) of those Regulations,
    other than a self-employed person within the meaning of subsection
    2(1) of those Regulations; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} meet the requirements of subsection 75(2) and
    paragraph 76(1)(b) of these Regulations and obtain a minimum of 67
    points based on the factors set out in paragraph 76(1)(a) of these
    Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pending applications --- investors

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Beginning on December 1, 2003, a foreign national who
  is an immigrant who made an application under the former Regulations
  before January 1, 2002 for an immigrant visa as an investor and whose
  application is still pending on December 1, 2003 and who has not,
  before that day, been awarded units of assessment under those
  Regulations must, in order to become a permanent resident as a member
  of the investor class,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be determined to be an investor within the meaning
    of subsection 2(1) of those Regulations and be awarded at least the
    minimum number of units of assessment required by those Regulations
    for an investor; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be an investor within the meaning of subsection
    88(1) of these Regulations and obtain a minimum of 35 points based
    on the factors set out in subsection 102(1) of these Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pending applications --- entrepreneurs

  [(5.1)]{.lawlabel} Beginning on December 1, 2003, a foreign national
  who is an immigrant who made an application under the former
  Regulations before January 1, 2002 for an immigrant visa as an
  entrepreneur and whose application is still pending on December 1,
  2003 and who has not, before that day, been awarded units of
  assessment under those Regulations must, in order to become a
  permanent resident as a member of the entrepreneur class,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be determined to be an entrepreneur within the
    meaning of subsection 2(1) of those Regulations and be awarded at
    least the minimum number of units of assessment required by those
    Regulations for an entrepreneur; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be an entrepreneur within the meaning of subsection
    88(1) of these Regulations and obtain a minimum of 35 points based
    on the factors set out in subsection 102(1) of these Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pending applications --- self-employed
  persons

  [(5.2)]{.lawlabel} Beginning on December 1, 2003, a foreign national
  who is an immigrant who made an application under the former
  Regulations before January 1, 2002 for an immigrant visa as a
  self-employed person and whose application is still pending on
  December 1, 2003 and who has not, before that day, been awarded units
  of assessment under those Regulations must, in order to become a
  permanent resident as a member of the self-employed persons class,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be determined to be a self-employed person within
    the meaning of subsection 2(1) of those Regulations and be awarded
    at least the minimum number of units of assessment required by those
    Regulations for a self-employed person; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be a self-employed person within the meaning of
    subsection 88(1) of these Regulations and obtain a minimum of 35
    points based on the factors set out in subsection 102(1) of these
    Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provincial nominees

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If, before the day on which this section comes into
  force, a foreign national who was a provincial nominee submitted an
  application for a permanent resident visa under the former Regulations
  that is pending immediately before that day, the foreign national
  shall be assessed, and units of assessment shall be awarded to them,
  in accordance with those Regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2003-383, s. 8
- SOR/2010-195, s. 15(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-361-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 361"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Investors

**[[362]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-362 .sectionLabel}** If, before April 1,
1999, a foreign national made an application for an immigrant visa as an
investor and signed any document referred to in clause 1(v)(iii)(A) of
Schedule X to the former Regulations, as that Schedule read immediately
before that date, or, in the case of an investor in a province, either
applied for a selection certificate under section 3.1 of An Act
respecting immigration to Québec, R.S.Q., c.I-0.2, as amended from time
to time, or applied for an immigrant visa as an investor, and signed an
investment agreement in accordance with the law of that province, the
relevant provisions of the former Regulations respecting an applicant
for an immigrant visa as an investor, an approved business, an investor
in a province, a fund manager, an eligible business, an approved fund, a
fund, an escrow agent, a privately administered venture capital fund or
a government-administered venture capital fund continue to apply as they
read immediately before April 1, 1999 to all persons governed by their
application before that date.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-140, s. 18(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-362-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 362"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entrepreneurs

**[[363]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-363 .sectionLabel}** For greater certainty,
section 98 does not apply in respect of an entrepreneur within the
meaning of subsection 2(1) of the former Regulations who was issued an
immigrant visa under subparagraph 9(1)(b)(ii) or (c)(i) of those
Regulations.

## [PART 21]{.HLabel1}[Repeals and Coming into Force]{.HTitleText1} {#h-690976 .Part}

### [Repeals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-690977 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations repealed

**[[364]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-364 .sectionLabel}** The following
Regulations are repealed:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Immigration Regulations, 1978[[Footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^1^](#footnote1_e){#footnote1_e-ID0EBAHA};

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Refugee Claimants Designated Class
  Regulations[[Footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^2^](#footnote2_e){#footnote2_e-ID0EBAGA};

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Immigration Act Fees Regulations[[Footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^3^](#footnote3_e){#footnote3_e-ID0EBAFA}; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the Humanitarian Designated Classes
  Regulations[[Footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^4^](#footnote4_e){#footnote4_e-ID0EBAEA}.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Coming into force

  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} These Regulations, except paragraph

  force on June 28, 2002.

  31, 2003.

<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Attend any inspection, unless
the employer was not notified, give all reasonable assistance to the
person conducting the inspection and provide that person with any
required document or information</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Type C</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">8</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">209.2(1)(a)(ii) and
209.3(1)(a)(ii)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Comply with the federal or
provincial laws that regulate the employment or recruitment of
employees, including foreign nationals, in the province in which the
foreign national works</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Type B</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">9</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">209.2(1)(a)(iii)
and 209.3(1)(a)(iv)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Provide the foreign national
with employment in the same occupation and substantially the same, but
not less favourable, wages and working conditions as outlined in the
foreign national’s offer of employment</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Type B</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">10</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
scope="row">209.3(1)(a)(iii)(A)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">For employers of a live-in
caregiver: ensure that foreign national resides in a private household
in Canada and provides child care, senior home support care or care of a
disabled person in that household without supervision</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Type B</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">11</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">209.3(1)(b)(i)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Ensure that the employment of
the foreign national will result in direct job creation or retention for
Canadian citizens or permanent residents, if that was a factor that led
to the issuance of the work permit</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Type B</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">12</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
scope="row">209.3(1)(b)(ii)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Ensure that the employment of
the foreign national will result in the development or transfer of
skills and knowledge for the benefit of Canadian citizens or permanent
residents, if that was a factor that led to the issuance of the work
permit</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Type B</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">13</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
scope="row">209.3(1)(b)(iii)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Hire or train Canadian citizens
or permanent residents, if that was a factor that led to the issuance of
the work permit</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Type B</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">14</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
scope="row">209.3(1)(b)(iv)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Make reasonable efforts to hire
or train Canadian citizens or permanent residents, if that was a factor
that led to the issuance of the work permit</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Type B</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">15</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">209.2(1)(a)(i) and
209.3(1)(a)(i)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Be actively engaged in the
business in which the offer of employment was made, unless the offer was
made for employment as a live-in caregiver</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Type C</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">16</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
scope="row">209.3(1)(a)(iii)(B)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">For employers of a live-in
caregiver: provide the foreign national with adequate furnished private
accommodation in the household</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Type C</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">17</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">209.2(1)(a)(iv) and
209.3(1)(a)(v)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Make reasonable efforts to
provide a workplace that is free of abuse</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Type C</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">18</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">209.2(1)(a)(v) and
209.3(1)(a)(vii)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Not do anything that prevents
the foreign national from complying with an order or regulation made
under the <a href="/eng/acts/E-4.5">Emergencies Act</a> or the <a
href="/eng/acts/Q-1.1">Quarantine Act</a></td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Type C</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">19</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">209.2(1)(a)(vi) and
209.3(1)(a)(viii)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Not do anything that prevents
the foreign national from complying with a provincial law that governs
public health in response to COVID-19</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Type C</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">20</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">209.2(1)(a)(vii)
and 209.3(1)(a)(xii)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Provide, during the period the
foreign national must isolate or quarantine themselves on entry into
Canada, wages to the foreign national that are substantially the same as
those set out in the offer of employment</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Type C</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">21</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
scope="row">209.3(1)(a)(ix)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Provide the foreign national
with accommodations that are separate from those provided to persons who
are not in quarantine and that permit the foreign worker to remain at
least two metres away from any other person</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Type C</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">22</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">209.3(1)(a)(x)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Provide the foreign national
with cleaning products for the purposes of cleaning and disinfecting the
accommodations regularly</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Type C</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">23</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
scope="row">209.3(1)(a)(xi)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Provide a foreign national who
becomes infected with or develops any signs or symptoms of COVID-19 with
accommodations that have a bedroom and a bathroom that are solely for
the use of the foreign national while they isolate themselves</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Type C</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">24</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
scope="row">209.3(1)(a)(vi)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Provide adequate accommodations
to a foreign national employed to perform work under an international
agreement between Canada and one or more countries concerning seasonal
agricultural workers</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Type B</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">25</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">209.2(1)(a.1) and
209.3(1)(a.1)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Provide the foreign national,
with a copy in their chosen official language of Canada, of the most
recent information with respect to the foreign national’s rights in
Canada that is made available by the Government of Canada for that
purpose</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Type B</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">26</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">209.2(1)(a)(ii.1)
and 209.3(1)(a)(ii.1)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Make available to the foreign
national the information referred to in paragraph 209.2(1)(a.1) or
paragraph 209.3(1)(a.1), as the case may be, in both official languages
of Canada</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Type B</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">27</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">209.2(1)(a)(ix) and
209.3(1)(a)(xv)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Not directly or indirectly
charge or recover from the foreign national the fees indicated</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Type C</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">28</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">209.2(1)(a)(x) and
209.3(1)(a)(xvi)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Ensure that any person who
recruited the foreign national for the employer does not, directly or
indirectly, charge or recover from the foreign national the fees
indicated</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Type C</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">29</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
scope="row">209.3(1)(a)(xiii)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Except in the case of an
employer who employs a foreign national to perform work under an
international agreement between Canada and one or more countries
concerning seasonal agricultural workers and the agreement includes
health insurance, must obtain and pay for private health insurance from
a Canadian insurance provider that covers emergency medical care for any
period during which the foreign national is not covered by the
applicable provincial health insurance system</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Type C</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">30</td>
<td class="noBorderRight" scope="row">209.2(1)(a)(viii) and
209.3(1)(a)(xiv)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">Make reasonable efforts to provide to the
foreign national access to health care services when the foreign
national is injured or becomes ill at the workplace</td>
<td>Type C</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE 1</p>
<p>Employer Conditions</p></figcaption>
</figure>

<figure id="691171">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize9 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom bottom"></th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Criterion</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Points</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">The employer derived
competitive or economic benefit from the violation</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">0 to 6</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">The violation involved
abuse of a foreign national (physical, psychological, sexual or
financial)</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">0 to 10</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">3</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">The violation negatively
affected the Canadian labour market or the Canadian economy</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">0 to 6</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">4</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">The employer did not make
reasonable efforts to minimize or remediate the effects of the
violation</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">0 to 3</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">5</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">The employer did not make
reasonable efforts to prevent recurrence of the violation</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">0 to 3</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">6</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"><p>The violation put the
foreign national’s health or safety at risk in relation to a <span
class="DefinitionRef"><dfn>communicable disease</dfn></span> as defined
in section 2 of the <a href="/eng/acts/Q-1.1">Quarantine
Act</a></p></td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">0 to 10</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">7</td>
<td class="noBorderRight"><p>The violation put the public’s health or
safety at risk in relation a <span
class="DefinitionRef"><dfn>communicable disease</dfn></span> as defined
in section 2 of the <a href="/eng/acts/Q-1.1">Quarantine
Act</a></p></td>
<td>0 to 10</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE 5</p>
<p>Severity of the Violation</p></figcaption>
</figure>
